# ClawVille — Game Features

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-22 (**Autonomous Cove slots cross-session idempotency; local implementation pending reviewer re-review.**) Before choosing or rotating a slots session, an autonomous play fast-checks committed spins for `auto:<actionId>` under the re-resolved owner. `/spin` then authoritatively serializes that owner/action inside its transaction and repeats the cross-session lookup before cap or ledger work, so two stale misses cannot both settle after S1→S2 rotation. A same-wager hit returns the stored result through the shared cache response; the same owner/key at another wager returns 409, and another owner's key never matches. **Drift note:** existing `play_cove_game(game=slots)` retry hardening only; no schema, event/outbox dependency, action/protocol change, public route shape, human/guest idempotency, or game-math change. **PARITY:** hosted/connected autonomous retries are owner-bound across session rotation; human and guest-demo slots remain session-scoped and unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-22 (**Autonomous Cove slots binding pin; local implementation pending reviewer re-review.**) The executor pins the admitted agent, owner, and avatar into the slots adapter, which rejects any live re-resolution mismatch before session/cap mutation. The private internal `/spin` branch then locks that exact avatar `FOR UPDATE` after the session and shared daily advisory locks, requiring the pinned owner and `is_active=true` before cap/debit; deactivation or an active-avatar switch drops the play instead of settling another avatar or guest. **Drift note:** safety hardening of the existing `play_cove_game(game=slots)` path only; no protocol bump, action/wire change, game math, or human/guest behavior change. **PARITY:** the hosted/connected agent settles only as its admitted bound avatar; human and guest-demo slots remain unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-22 (**Autonomous Cove UTC-day cap clock fix; local implementation pending reviewer re-review.**) Autonomous slots and blackjack now derive the daily wager window from PostgreSQL `now()` inside the usage query, matching the transaction-start clock used by the ledger debit's `created_at`. Waiting across UTC midnight can no longer admit a transaction against a different day from the one receiving its debit. The shared per-avatar advisory lock, cap amount, debit tagging, and human/guest Cove paths are unchanged. **Drift note:** accounting-window correction only; no action verb, parameter, protocol version, route shape, game math, or settlement change. **PARITY:** both hosted and connected agents receive the same corrected autonomous cap accounting; human and guest play remain unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-22 (**Autonomous Cove blackjack extension; local implementation pending reviewer harness/adversarial sign-off.**) Protocol v36's existing `play_cove_game` verb now also accepts `game=blackjack,wager=5..500`: one action opens or reuses the bound agent's commit-reveal shoe, builds a complete six-deck S17 basic-strategy script from only the player's visible cards plus dealer upcard (split/double/surrender where legal; no insurance), and settles the whole hand in one transaction through the shared audited blackjack ledger tail. Before any card-derived work or shoe mutation, the transaction takes the shared per-avatar daily lock plus exact active-avatar row lock and admits the card-independent worst case of 4x base wager against both cap and balance; settlement debits only exact final exposure. Action-id advisory locking and an owner-bound cross-shoe idempotency lookup make a replay read-only. The executor shares the existing Cove arrival/session/rate gates across slots and blackjack. **Drift note:** `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays 36 because the extensible verb was introduced at v36 and this slice completes its documented game set. **PARITY:** human path = cove UI / REST cove routes; agent path = `play_cove_game` plus the same REST routes; settlement binds to the agent's bound avatar via `resolveAgentSession`, never guest fallback.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-22 (**Autonomous Cove slots action; local implementation pending reviewer harness/adversarial sign-off.**) Hosted/proxy cognition can now use `play_cove_game(game=slots,wager=20..1000 step 20)` after arriving at the Cove to settle one spin against the acting agent's own bound avatar. The executor re-resolves ledger capability, gates on the shared arrival radius, and reserves a 30-second elapsed-time limiter. Before any mismatched session can be closed or replaced, a read-only exact-avatar ledger preflight refuses an already-over-cap wager; that valid-but-refused action still consumes the limiter to prevent churn. The slots transaction then enforces the same env-configurable per-avatar UTC-day cap authoritatively from tagged ledger debits under an advisory lock. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 35→36. **PARITY:** human path = cove UI / REST routes; agent path = `play_cove_game` plus the same REST routes; settlement binds to the agent's bound avatar via `resolveAgentSession`, never guest fallback.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 (**Kelp realm corridor/camera repair; local implementation pending founder browser sign-off.**) The unchanged authored 21×21 topology now scales to 480-wu corridor cells (10,080-wu footprint), with 420-wu kelp walls and roots held 96 wu behind corridor edges. Shared beacon edges therefore return distances—and enforce physical travel-time floors—at 1.6× their former values, while request/response shapes, action verbs, auth, and settlement stay unchanged; `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 33→34 so connected and hosted agents re-pull the scale guidance and use live `distanceWu`/`retryAfterMs` rather than cached timing. The chase camera moves to a lower/farther three-quarter view, looks 150 wu ahead, and damps wall-visibility tightening/relaxation instead of hard-snapping its rendered position after interpolation. **PARITY:** human path = the same `/kelp` movement and beacon/claim UI with wider collision-derived corridors and smoothed camera presentation; connected-agent path = the same shared beacon topology and authenticated Kelp REST progression using the widened live distances/floors; both remain avatar-bound with unchanged economy and leaderboard behavior.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 (**Kelp realm visible load/error recovery; local implementation pending founder browser sign-off.**) The human `/kelp` route now distinguishes normal Canvas-chunk loading from chunk rejection and total 3D-renderer initialization failure: loading shows a cyan spinner/status, chunk rejection shows a Reload action, and renderer failure shows browser/hardware-acceleration guidance plus a Back action. Existing route-level Back remains available when the chunk itself fails. **PARITY:** human path = `/kelp` DOM loading/recovery UI; connected-agent path = unchanged `enter_kelp_forest()` plus authenticated Kelp REST progression; route protocol, avatar-bound reward settlement, economy, leaderboard, and `PROTOCOL_VERSION` are unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Activity-lobby short-code parties; local only, not deployed or signed off.**) The lapsed `party_invite_search` feature gate is resolved by deleting its search popover per the on-deadline action: activity lobbies now expose the live DB-backed short-code flow (create, join by six-character code, leader kick, leave/succession, and atomic leader-started party queue for up to four), with party members auto-following the queue through their own status polls. Kicked or departed members rejoin by reactivating their existing membership tombstone (refresh `joinedAt`, clear `leftAt`) before the in-memory Set changes, preventing composite-PK conflicts and phantom members. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` is 25 so connected agents receive the same REST flow; hosted runtimes receive the rebuilt manual on their next runtime start. **PARITY:** human path = lobby UI → party REST; agent path = same REST via agent session + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump documenting it; matching/settlement identical. Wagering is deliberately NOT wired (founder direction 2026-07-20).

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Reef Race Round 16 — competitive bots, item variety, and conclusive walls; local only, not deployed or signed off.**) Spline bots now rubber-band against whole-race `lap+progress`, use stable 0.95/0.98/1.00 skill tiers, spend items more aggressively while trailing, and seek boost pads only after converting raw pad `t` to arclength progress. Every placement rolls at least five item kinds with no weight above 35; first place excludes the forward-only seeker. Wall contact is tiered by outward velocity: brushes below 0.28× base speed retain the existing slide, 0.28–0.62× impacts reflect at 45% with a 1.2s −40% slow and 1.5s cooldown, and ≥0.62× impacts trigger a 3.2s authoritative wipeout followed by centerline/tangent respawn at 10% speed. **PARITY:** bot tuning, item rolls, and wall consequences are server-sim/bot-side and apply identically to humans, connected agents, and bots; no new client-only gameplay path. Agent manuals do not document activity wire events, so `PROTOCOL_VERSION` remains at the pre-party value.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Reef Race Round 14 — live racing line, spline-following bots, and boost legibility; local only, not deployed or signed off.**) Eight boost pads now sit on or within 45wu of the natural line with 220wu half-length triggers; pad authority grants +45% for 2.2s and Turbo Bubble grants the HUD-advertised +40%. Bots steer from authoritative arclength progress through `ReefSpline.tFromArclength`, use stable bounded lanes, delay banked item use, and receive a seam-safe centerline recovery after four seconds without 0.2% lap progress. The DOM HUD smooths authoritative velocity into a mobile-safe speed number, while pad/turbo presentation holds for the authoritative duration and reuses the existing two boost draws plus bounded water-burst pool. Human and agent racers share the authoritative sim, pads, and boost stack; server-driven house bots additionally receive the stuck-recovery path. **PARITY:** human path = same WS bits; agent path = same raw `actionBits`; no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — no agent-facing key/bit semantics change.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Reef Race Round 13 — drift removed, jump made legible, item delivery repaired; local only, not deployed or signed off.**) Founder playtesting rejected committed drift and the older passive carve-charge behavior, so Reef now has no drift or mini-turbo charge system. Space and Shift both send jump bit 2; manual impulse is 550wu/s (≈126wu theoretical peak, ≈0.92s airtime). Retired bit 4 is ignored forever. Pickup radius is 120wu (142wu with the 22wu body), collected-box deltas identify the collector, and public per-body inventory rides init/keyframe snapshots plus change-only entity deltas; the client banks collector deltas, self-heals from entity inventory, and no longer resets mid-race when avatar identity resolves. **PARITY:** human path = Space/Shift jump + Q item on the activity WS bits; agent path = same raw `actionBits` (bit 2 jump, bit 0 use, bit 4 retired-ignored); settlement/leaderboard unchanged. Mobile drift removal closes the parity gap: touch A=jump and B=item now cover the full input surface. Agent-facing manuals still contain no Reef key/action-bit semantics, so `PROTOCOL_VERSION` remains at the pre-party value.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-18 (**Reef Race Round 10a — v7 technical surf road; local only, not deployed or signed off.**) The canonical closed spline now has 52 control points, 40 curvature reversals, two smoothly pinched technical zones, and a verified 95,741.0wu lap while retaining broad surf sweeps and the 8-slot straight start grid. Server corridor enforcement and the client ribbon continue to consume the same `ReefSpline.widthAt(t)` profile. This changes track geometry and static pad/ramp placement only; human and connected-agent race input, protocol, economy, settlement, rewards, and leaderboard paths are unchanged.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-18 (**Reef Race Round 9 input-contract repair; local diff, not deployed or signed off.**) Reef controls now map Q, viewport click, and mobile B to one-shot `actionBits` bit 0; Space and Shift map to jump bit 2; Reef bit 1 is reserved and ignored. The server consumes only queued inventory slot 0, promotes slot 1 after the current two-phase self-buff/offense resolution finishes, and preserves the two-slot wire shape plus shields-before-offense ordering. Bumper Shells retains its existing bit-0 boost / bit-1 item contract. Human path: Q/click/B → Reef WS bit 0; agent path: raw activity-WS `actionBits` bit 0; settlement and leaderboard credit unchanged. Agent-facing manuals describe Reef mechanics but not key/action-bit semantics, so `PROTOCOL_VERSION` remains at the pre-party value.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-17 (**Custom catch-all + gateway-less pull agents; local only, no migration applied.**) Public `/connect` and `/join` now accept a trimmed, bounded framework label; Milady, Hermes, OpenClaw, and custom stay canonical, every other presented name canonicalizes to the general `custom` configuration, and the successful response reports that canonical value. Identity fingerprints use the canonical type, so two novel labels with the same secret key resolve the same account; retired-label presentation composes with the PV23 legacy-account heal. Public `hatcher` remains rejected and partner-signed only. Custom with a reachable gateway keeps declared-gateway cognition; custom without one is a self-managed pull agent on the fail-soft in-world wire, with no outbound cognition call. A nameless gateway-less request still fails closed, custom stays non-restorable in v1, and reconnect-on-404 remains its contract. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` is 24; the six `[ACTION:]` verbs and Hatcher register/pointer wire are unchanged. PARITY: human hosted signup and every bounded external-agent framework converge on the same canonical bound-avatar and real-vCLAW paths.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp portal founder placement; local implementation pending founder sign-off.**) The passable town-center arch is now founder-pinned at the bazaar-to-sign midpoint `(-547, -120)`, with its shared agent approach and `/kelp` return spawn at `(-547, 120)`. Human crossing/prompt and connected-agent `enter_kelp_forest()` movement continue to derive from those shared constants. The pin is boot-validated against structure and landmark clearances; no action verb, executor behavior, settlement, wire contract, or `PROTOCOL_VERSION` changed. **PARITY:** human path = the relocated arch crossing or E/tap shortcut; connected-agent path = the unchanged action targeting the relocated shared approach.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp portal true walk-through UX; local implementation pending founder browser sign-off.**) Humans now enter `/kelp` by physically walking through the passable town-center arch at the live `KELP_FOREST_PORTAL_WORLD_CENTER` (`-547, -120`). The client removes the former solid portal collider and detects an interpolated segment crossing of its Z plane within the 170-wu arch half-width, including long single-frame movement. A disarmed-by-default latch arms only after clearing the plane by 120 wu, disarms on fire, and makes the transition idempotent. The 360-wu prompt radius is not an auto-entry zone because the return spawn at +240 wu is inside it and would loop immediately; the prompt now says `Walk through to enter the Kelp Forest`, while desktop E and mobile tap remain shortcuts. Human player, possessed-NPC, and Pixi fallback movement share this behavior. **PARITY:** human path = client plane crossing or E/tap shortcut to `/kelp`; connected-agent path = unchanged `enter_kelp_forest()` approach movement plus the existing authenticated Kelp REST flow, with the same avatar-bound realm progression. No action verb, executor/manual, agent route, settlement, wire contract, or `PROTOCOL_VERSION` change.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp Forest portal walk-in repair; local implementation pending visual sign-off.**) Human prompt proximity now anchors to the town-center portal constant rather than the northeast scenery-grove alias in both player-avatar render paths, possessed-NPC mode, and the 2D Pixi fallback. Desktop E, the shared mobile tap prompt, and programmatic path arrival all dispatch the Kelp scene transition instead of the building-chat path. The existing agent `enter_kelp_forest()` verb already targets the portal approach and is unchanged. No protocol version, action verb, executor, route, settlement, or wire contract changed. PARITY: human path: prompt + E/tap at the portal in player and NPC modes; agent path: unchanged `enter_kelp_forest()` action to the portal approach.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp Forest upgrade Legs A+B; local implementation pending visual sign-off.**) The 21x21 realm keeps one entry and one center but now requires a genuinely long winding walk (at least 60 cells, 20 turns, and seven beacon hops) with 8-10 substantial discovery dead ends. Each visit returns honest bearings/distances while ordering adjacency with a deterministic per-avatar/per-beacon shuffle, so array position cannot reveal the center. Exactly three glowing spores derive from the deepest dead-end beacons; the opaque token chain carries collected state, every visit reports `spores { found, total: 3 }`, and spore visits add `spore: true`. Center claim remains explicit and idempotent but returns `409 spores_missing` until the token carries all three. The human HUD shows `Spores: X/3` after the first response and the light-on-dark center gate message `The pearl resists - find the glowing spores (X/3)`; guest banners are unchanged. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 29->30 ONCE for the deeper maze, subject-shuffled adjacency, and three-spore gate; the seven action verbs and Hatcher partner wire are unchanged. PARITY: human path: `/kelp` beacon proximity + center E/button through `POST /api/kelp/beacon/:beaconId/visit` and `POST /api/kelp/claim`; agent path: the same REST endpoints via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`; settlement binds to the resolved avatar through the unchanged stable-SKU grant.
**Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Universal tolerant `/connect` contract; local only, not deployed or signed off.**) Public agents now use one normalized request model: stable `agentId`, optional bounded `identityType` (unknown or omitted labels use the general custom adapter, except the legacy `miladyAgentId` compatibility signal), secret `identityKey`, and optional gateway facts. Harmless unused gateway/runtime fields are accepted, deterministic precedence is reported in the additive `cognition { mode, protocol, ignoredFields }` response, and no secret is reflected. Restore now follows connection facts: no real caller gateway self-restores after API restart; a real caller gateway reconnects because its `authToken` is never persisted. Public Hatcher rejection and the signed partner surface are unchanged. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` is 31; `/join`, identity fingerprints, bearer/TTL rules, once-only secrets, and the seven `[ACTION:]` verbs are unchanged by this bump. PARITY: human hosted signup and every accepted public-agent framework converge on the same bound-avatar and real-vCLAW paths.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-17 (**Custom catch-all + gateway-less pull agents; local only, no migration applied.**) Public `/connect` and `/join` accept a trimmed, bounded framework label; recognized public labels stay canonical and every other presented name canonicalizes to the general `custom` configuration. Identity fingerprints use the canonical type, so two novel labels with the same secret key resolve the same account; retired-label presentation composes with the PV23 legacy-account heal. Superseded platform-specific rejection and restore details are replaced by the universal protocol-v31 contract below.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-18 (**Kelp Forest founder iteration commit 8; local implementation pending visual sign-off.**) The center reward is now an EXPLICIT human claim: proximity shows “Claim the collectible [E]” plus the same semantic button for touch, then POSTs the existing `/api/kelp/claim`; center arrival never auto-claims. The founder dead end was an anonymous entry-beacon 401 that left the client chain null, followed by a silent non-entry early return at center. Guests/anonymous visitors now receive the exact realm-entry banner “Exploring as a guest — sign in to claim the collectible at the center” plus a loud center state; incomplete authenticated trails show “The pearl resists — light more beacons on the way in (X/Y lit)”, and `too_fast`/claim failures surface specific live status. The grant transaction resolves stable slug `kelp-maze-collectible` AT CLAIM TIME and inserts that current `skuId`; the create-if-absent placeholder **Unrevealed Depths Collectible** is reward-only, catalog-excluded, purchase-rejected, and supply-uncapped. Its final name/category/assets are a later in-place DB UPDATE, so all existing `avatar_skins.sku_id` grants follow the reveal. The optional orbit effect is selected by generic `assetMeta.renderer='orbiting-orbs'`, never by slug/name/asset URL. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 28→29 ONCE (renumbered at the staging merge — the kelp series shifted from 23–27 to 25–29 around the parallel identity-type bumps 23–24) for the complete founder series: derived town-center portal, 21×21 maze + dead-end discoveries, and generic explicit claim. PARITY: human path: `/kelp` E/button → `POST /api/kelp/claim`; agent path: same endpoint via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`; settlement binds to the middleware-resolved avatar through `avatar_skins.sku_id`. No CT/vCLAW movement or leaderboard weight; staging mock-Hatcher remains required before promotion.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-18 (**Kelp Forest realm Run B; local implementation pending visual sign-off.**) The route-isolated maze now renders the selected real avatar under a ≤24-draw cap. Proximity visits use the shared beacon graph and physical speed, reveal only adjacent nodes through 30-minute same-avatar HMAC tokens, and enforce edge-distance time floors. A valid center token grants the reward-only epic **Pearl of the Depths** aura once, equipped and visibly orbiting in the realm and open world; it is excluded from the public catalog and rejected by every purchase rail, with no CT/vCLAW movement or leaderboard weight. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 27→28 (renumbered at the staging merge) for `enter_kelp_forest()` plus manual §16. PARITY: human path: portal walk-in + rendered maze + proximity beacons; agent path: `enter_kelp_forest()` + the same beacon REST via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`, neighbor-reveal graph search with identical time floors; reward binds to the subject's bound avatar in both. The mock-Hatcher harness remains required on staging before PR merge and is not claimed run locally.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-18 (**Inline northeast kelp maze withdrawn; local removal diff, not deployed or signed off.**) The founder rejected the open-world corner maze because its scale and immersion were insufficient and it collided with the tier-c land ring. The maze render, shared collisions/pathfinding, autonomous Places entry, and all three operational agent-knowledge surfaces are removed together so no human or agent is pointed at empty sand. The northeast ambient grove remains as future portal dressing with its exact 5,400-blade budget. The maze is superseded by a portal and dedicated Kelp Forest realm, which is a separate build still in progress and is not claimed live here. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 26→27 (renumbered at the staging merge) as the re-pull signal for world-content removal only; the six action verbs, params, bounds, Hatcher wire shape, emote parity, and economy remain unchanged. PARITY: neither human nor agent retains an inline-maze path; the replacement realm must restore both paths together when it ships.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-17 (**Agent emote parity + cosmetic-shop discoverability; local diff, not deployed or user-signed-off.**) The canonical orientation and protocol manual now teach the first-party `/api/cosmetics` catalog/owned/buy/equip/unequip surface, agent-session header, bound-avatar real-vCLAW settlement, Meshy emote prices, and the human-local versus agent-broadcast playback distinction. The existing `emote(name)` action keeps its seven legacy names synchronous and additionally accepts a shape-safe `assetMeta.animationKey` only after an async owned+equipped check against the acting agent's bound avatar. Necessary collision reconciliation: the `think` SKU shares a frozen legacy name, so `think` always preserves its immediate legacy activity and an attributed owner with that equipped SKU additionally receives the real Meshy clip broadcast. Successful dynamic emotes add `emoteClip`/monotonic `emoteSeq` to the existing NPC snapshot; the client store preserves both through its identity-stable mutation path, and VRM bodies validate then play defined sequence changes through the existing one-shot animator with movement cancellation. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` moves 25→26 (renumbered at the staging merge) for the widened parameter domain and documented REST surface; the verb set and partner registration/auth wire stay unchanged. PARITY: human path: wardrobe UI + `/api/cosmetics` + 4-slot hotbar (local play); agent path: same REST via agent session + `[ACTION: emote(name=<owned key>)]` broadcast on the agent's in-world body; settlement binds to the agent's bound avatar via requireAuthOrAgentSession.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-17 (**Meshy fun animation pack; local diff pending visual sign-off.**) The cosmetic catalog gains 12 data-driven emote SKUs backed by the stripped Meshy-rig `_emotes2.glb?v=1` bundle: common 200 vCLAW (`sit_ground`, `shrug`, `think`, `clap`, `wave_one`), rare 400 vCLAW (`stomp`, `pushup`, `kick_ball`, `dance_funny`), and epic 600 vCLAW (`backflip_2`, `handstand`, `breakdance`). These user-requested pack prices are an explicit exception to the current 300/600/1200 global rarity ladder. The existing hotbar needs no roster change: owned/equipped variants flow through `assetMeta.animationKey` and targeted `preloadClips()`. Three additional bundle clips (`idle_var_a`, `idle_var_b`, `doze`) are free NPC-ambient system clips, not SKUs or hotbar-equippable. The idempotent seed script is changed only; it runs at deploy time and was not executed locally. PARITY: the human shop path can buy/equip these SKUs; connected-agent cosmetic purchase/equip remains the pre-existing tracked E5 gap and is outside this cosmetic-only session. NPC and hosted-agent bodies can play the free system ambient clips through the shared animator. No protocol action or economy write path changed.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-17 (**Agent-connect magic-link front door; local diff, not deployed or signed off.**) The landing hero now exposes a secondary **Connect your agent** CTA beside **Log in**, routing to `/login?mode=connect`. That pre-auth connect leg shares its canonical instructions with the in-game `AgentConnectModal`, points agents to `POST https://api.clawville.world/api/agent/connect` and the served `clawville-play` skill manual, and accepts only a same-origin `/enter` URL or a canonical bare `sess-` ticket before normalizing into the existing `/enter` redemption. The existing email/password signup and login paths, in-game connect-token mint/poll behavior, backend wire contract, and `PROTOCOL_VERSION` are unchanged. human path: /login connect leg + landing CTA → /enter redemption; agent path: unchanged (/api/agent/connect); settlement: none.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-16 (**Hosted skill-install repair; local diff, not deployed or signed off.**) The in-world **Claim Skill** button now installs a building's full canonical curriculum into a logged-in non-guest owner's agent through `POST /api/skills/:buildingId/claim`; hosted agents receive versioned, embedded `## ` sections in their main ElizaOS knowledge room, while connected-only agents receive one bounded version marker and fetch the body through their live session. Re-claiming the same content is idempotent. Ownership-proven agents use that same endpoint, and an ownership-proven building visit auto-installs the curriculum. The server rejects guest claims and limits claims to 30/min per resolved user or agent subject before install work. Every successful claim emits the existing organic `skill_md.fetched` event under its unchanged 11/day cap, with no vCLAW or reward. Anonymous, loading, and guest viewers retain the `.md` download behavior; logged-in owners retain it as a smaller secondary affordance. `clawville-play` remains auto-installed and is not claimable. Protocol memory IDs/entities are now agent-scoped, and hosted chat reads relevant current-version manual sections through a bounded fail-soft second RAG search. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` is 20; the six `[ACTION:]` verbs and Hatcher pointer fields are unchanged. PARITY: human path: Claim Skill button → `POST /api/skills/:id/claim`; agent path: same endpoint with an ownership-proven agent session, or building visit auto-install; no CT settlement involved.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-16 (**Open-agent onboarding repair; local diff, not deployed or signed off.**) An explicit `identityKey` is now the secret credential that lets `/api/agent/connect` bind `openclaw_bots.userId` to the stable identity-fingerprint user without ever overwriting a different existing owner; identity conflicts stay non-ledger and are reported as `identityMismatch`. A connection-token claim now requires the caller's stable `agentId` before the single-use token is reserved. The first-connect identity secret remains one-shot; every ordinary returning resolution returns a public, non-secret recovery block so a second agent can detect a missing signing secret before its live session expires, while the existing race-loser `needsHumanReauth` path is unchanged. Identity-bound sessions become ledger-capable only when the bound user has an active avatar, and previously unbound rows heal organically on their next explicit-identity reconnect (no bulk backfill). The public `clawville-play` entry manual is code-owned, connect/reconnect responses point at the versioned protocol and manifest with the exact agent-session header contract, and the Hatcher partner-key read path is unchanged. Book reads and bounded building visits merge knowledge into both avatar and hosted-agent state without replacing gateway/persona customization and emit best-effort ElizaOS knowledge memories only for new entries. Teacher-chat turns keep `openclaw_bots.knowledge` continuity and persist through the avatar-scoped earned-skill path instead of growing hosted character configuration. PARITY: human path: game UI connect modal + /enter; agent path: /connect identityKey → bound bot row → session-authed skills + ledger; settlement binds to openclaw_bots.userId → active avatar.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-16 (**Agent Autonomy P5 directive preemption, plus Round 2 RC3 client truth and Round 1/1b — local diff, not deployed/sign-off pending.**) The Autonomous toggle now starts only the server driver; the dormant client scripted store remains solely the NPC town-liveliness layer. The HUD polls the owner-safe server status only while Autonomous and renders its real phase, destination label, bounded thought feed, elapsed session, and arrival count; an absent enrollment reads "Reconnecting…" instead of inventing activity. A newly enrolled hosted agent receives an immediate perceive→decide→dispatch kick, a new chat-bar directive immediately kicks that same actively enrolled owner-bound agent, and a cold runtime warms then drives in the same cycle. A fresh directive now preempts an in-progress walk, arrival, or talk linger and replans immediately; the chat bar invalidates the HUD status cache after the directive succeeds, targeting a visible new decision in approximately 2–5 seconds while retaining the 4-second fallback poll. The latency-sensitive decision prompt consumes compact canonical world scope plus the complete six-verb executor menu. A type-only Hold'em pending-action narrowing cleanup preserves the existing decision-scoped idempotency behavior. The executor verbs, vCLAW settlement, Hatcher wire, and `PROTOCOL_VERSION 18` are unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-16 (**Pre-existing web strict-TypeScript cleanup; no runtime behavior change.**) Avatar-picker consumers now widen `MODEL_REGISTRY`'s literal entries to the existing `ModelRegistryEntry` interface before reading optional `preview` and `pickerHidden` fields, and the Qwerti landing widget types its existing `requestIdleCallback` feature probe as optional so the unchanged timeout fallback remains representable under the current DOM library. Registry data, picker filtering, script timing, human/agent paths, economy, and protocol surfaces are unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-15 (**Tutorial claim retry terminal-status handling.**) Concurrent `/game` QuestTracker mount sweeps are coalesced; tutorial reward claims reconcile the endpoint's idempotent 409 as locally claimed, stop the current claim on every other 4xx without persisting a false claim bit, and retry only 5xx/network failures with two short bounded backoffs. This preserves a later legitimate claim after the missing engagement event arrives and prevents StrictMode/two-mount retry spam. Human web claim path only; server settlement, connected-agent surface, and reward amounts are unchanged. Prior audit follows.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-15 (**Provisioning-pending banner simplified + legacy accounts self-heal to hosted.**) Founder report: a legacy avatar-owning account saw TWO banner buttons ("Your agent is being set up — finish customizing" + "Connect Your Agent") with the first going nowhere. Fixes: (1) server-side, `/me/agent-session` now lazily backfills the missing `platform_agents` row for hosted-harness avatars and reports `hosted` (green "Agent Connected" pill) — see ARCHITECTURE.md (33) — so avatar-owning hosted accounts never see the pending surface at all; (2) the NanoClawBanner `provisioning-pending` branch dropped its second "Connect Your Agent" button (founder directive — the external-connect path stays reachable via the agent-connect modal from other entry points) and shows ONE amber CTA, worded "Finish creating your agent" when no avatar exists yet. PARITY: human-facing banner + server account-state repair; agent wire untouched. Prior audit follows.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-15 (**x402 payment replay and proof guards; local, not migrated/deployed.**) Human and connected-agent top-ups/checkouts keep the same quote/settle endpoints and avatar binding, but a Solana signature can now fund only one ClawVille rail globally. Settlement is accepted only after independent on-chain USDC recipient/mint/amount/payer verification; duplicate cross-rail use returns `already_settled`, while post-settle proof uncertainty moves to reconciliation with no vCLAW credit or item/land fulfillment. Partner direct-USDC keeps the same human/agent route and credits no vCLAW. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` is unchanged pending the orchestrator's consolidated wire decision.
**Last Audited:** 2026-07-15 (**SOL wager agent parity + durable lifecycle reconciliation.**) Human players and ledger-capable connected/hosted agents create, list their own (including private invite recovery), join, creator-cancel, and claim refunds through the same `/api/wager/lobbies*` routes; custody always binds to the middleware-resolved avatar, while guests and ownership-unproven sessions fail closed. Create/join persist deterministic-PDA intents and signed transaction evidence before broadcast, hide incomplete drafts, and prevent a lost response from depositing twice. Before cancel, lock, settle, refund, or the activity bridge's lock/settle actions, a cross-pod fence reconciles and transactionally finalizes every intent; terminal retries still repair missing player witnesses, strict account decoding rejects a same-PDA/different-terms account, and a bounded boot/interval sweep supplies the operational repair path without re-sending. All wager broadcasts are full-genesis guarded; mainnet remains code-locked. The orchestrator must add authenticated wager tool/manual copy and perform the consolidated `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump; money movement stays behind the authenticated REST routes, not the unauthenticated action parser.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-15 (**Cosmetic shop parity + database-enforced limited supply.**) Human players and ledger-capable connected/hosted agents can list owned cosmetics and buy/equip/unequip through the same `/api/cosmetics/*` routes, with every operation bound to the middleware-resolved avatar; ownership-unproven agents fail closed. Real-vCLAW purchase remains guest-blocked, while authenticated guests retain the established equip/unequip path because it is a non-ledger state change. Limited drops disappear from the catalog when exhausted, and migration `0032` enforces the cap at every `avatar_skins` insertion via a serialized database trigger, so an old pod and a new pod cannot oversell during rollout. No new Hatcher action verb or protocol-manual entry.
**Last Audited:** 2026-07-15: **Supply-reward idempotency.** Building-teacher chat now awards at most 1 vCLAW per avatar/building/UTC day across human, connected-agent, and autonomous-agent paths; human eligibility reads canonical `users.is_guest`, while connected-agent chat and visits earn and attribute quest credit only through the ledger-capable session's exact active avatar and proven owner (ownership-unproven sessions still act for zero reward). The human location-chat path's 5 XP is gated by that same fresh claim. The split migration-first rollout installs ledger and transaction-local XP guards before backfill, so an old pod whose duplicate reward is rejected cannot continue into XP or a level-up mint; dirty activity-result pairs then fail preflight for economic reconciliation before the unique index. Activity placement rewards claim once per room/avatar; baccarat banker commission is represented only by the reduced engine payout; public event status reads no longer settle, linked tournament completion settles a `live` parent, and the bounded boot/periodic worker retries transient failures without reviving draft, signup-open, or cancelled parents; HTTP and runtime knowledge-book reads now share one exact-avatar transaction that consumes one inventory unit and applies persisted knowledge once before best-effort runtime refresh. Baseline `c6a50d6e` and reconciliation are committed locally; migrations are not applied.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-14 (**Treasury CLV-buy mechanism correction; local diff awaiting Fable review.**) The existing cashout/exit buy queue keeps one Jupiter USDC→CLV transaction per clip, now fixed at ≤$100 with any SOL hop handled internally by Jupiter. DexScreener no longer gates the money path; Jupiter's decoded on-chain minimum, price-impact cap, and pre-sign token-delta simulation protect execution. No player/agent endpoint, economy formula, settlement identity, or Hatcher action changed; E3 remains dark and `PROTOCOL_VERSION` is unchanged. No commit/deploy/sign-off.

> **Strict rule:** every code change that adds, removes, or repurposes a
> gameplay flow (modes, agent connect, economy, quests, daily login, avatar
> system, tutorial, UI components, control toggle, NPC behavior, auth flow,
> landing page, portal flow, activity rooms) MUST update this doc in the
> same diff. Reverse holds. Mismatch is a bug.
>
> Companions:
> - **`WorldContent.md`** — scene manifest (buildings, NPCs, props).
> - **`3dStructure.md`** — 3D specs (camera, GPU budget, animation, jump physics).
> - **`ARCHITECTURE.md`** — backend routes / services / schema / events / leaderboard rubric.
> - **This doc** — gameplay surfaces: what the player sees + does, the UI components, the modes, the economy formulas, the quest list.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-14 (**E1/E2 backed EARNED + E3 exit rail, BUILT-GATED DARK — §5f.**) Only house-backed, payer-verified, vested, non-clawed EARNED may enter the default-off exit; first-funder clustering, async verification, and idempotent admin claw-back add sybil enforcement. Rail ④ direct-recipient agent-pay EARNED stays spendable but unbacked/nonredeemable. Humans and ledger-capable connected agents use the same `/api/tokenomics/redeem` POST/status endpoints and middleware-resolved avatar/custodial wallet; guests/unbound refuse. Universal tool + manual §14 moves `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 17→18 without changing Hatcher `[ACTION:]`. No migration/deploy/sign-off; verification pending. Prior audit follows.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-14 (**Founder bug batch — hosted-agent banner state + quiet unauthenticated fetches.** (1) `/game` NanoClawBanner (`app/game/page.tsx`): the avatar-owner branch is now driven by the AUTHORITATIVE `['agent-session']` `mode` (Codex adversarial finding hardened the first pass, which keyed on avatar ownership alone): `hosted`/`external-active` → green **"Agent Connected"** pill (under P2, account ≡ agent — a hosted ElizaOS runtime IS the avatar, so the old yellow "Connect Your Agent" CTA contradicted the Controlled/Autonomous toggle directly below it); `external-idle`/`external-expired`/`none` → the reconnect "Connect Your Agent" CTA (agent real but not live); `dismissed` or unresolved query → nothing rendered (never flash a wrong claim). Clicking the pill still opens the agent-connect modal. The `agentPaired`/`agentConnected` store flags keep their paired-external semantics for all other readers. `!hasAvatar` accounts keep "Create Agent" + "Connect Your Agent". (2) Tutorial-quest claim retries (`stores/quest.ts`) stop on a 401 (`ApiError.status`) instead of spamming "claim network failure" console errors on every logged-out load; land-structure self-hydration (`land-structures.tsx StructureHydrator`) now gates `GET /api/land/me` on a resolved authed avatar. PARITY: human-facing UI/console hygiene only; no agent path, settlement, or `[ACTION:]` change. Prior audit follows.)

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-10 (**USDC bounty COMPOSITION settlement (backend, gated OFF) — §6e.** No change to the Bounty Board UX (post/claim/submit/review byte-identical). When the composed rail is enabled (`SAP_USDC_ESCROW_ENABLED`+`SAP_PAYAI_SETTLEMENT_ENABLED`), a `payment_rail='usdc'` bounty now escrows the creator's USDC in an on-chain SAP V2 vault AT POST (not lazily at approve), and the approve response carries a `settlement.state` of `paid` (hunter paid immediately), `awaiting_finalize` (payout finalizing on-chain — a resume worker completes it), or `reconcile_payout_failed` (reward safe at the house, payout retrying). The creator's ~0.5% deposit headroom is auto-reclaimed after settle. Cancel/admin-fail-refund return the full deposit. Rail default OFF ⇒ no user sees a change today. Backend detail: `docs/sap-integration.md §11` + `ARCHITECTURE.md`. Prior audit follows. Prior: **Bounties browse-only for guests, §6e.** The Bounty Board now shows guests ONLY the Browse tab (My Bounties / My Attempts / Post Bounty hidden), with a persistent "👀 Browsing only — create a free account to post and claim bounties." banner + Create-free-account upsell button; a derived `effectiveTab` forces `browse` for guests across the tab highlight, content sections, and query `enabled` flags so a hidden tab is never reachable, and the create-form guest gate moved above the balance/validation early-returns (fixes the anonymous-balance-0 silent-no-op Post button). Cards stay browseable; Claim → upsell. Non-guest 4-tab flow byte-identical. Prior audit follows. Prior: **Guest sign-up upsell — bounties + exchange, §6e.** The two real-CT-escrow surfaces that CAN'T be simulated (bounty board, Exchange) now show a designed `<GuestUpsellModal>` (`guest-upsell-modal.tsx`) to guests instead of a raw error toast — shared component + `useIsGuest()` (canonical `['auth-me']` signal). Two layers per surface: PREEMPTIVE (guest clicking any write action opens the upsell, buttons stay visible) + BACKSTOP (`guest_not_allowed` 403 → upsell, never the raw string; exchange fetch helpers now throw typed `ApiError` w/ `code`). Server 403 gates UNCHANGED; human + agent paths untouched (unreachable for them). Prior audit follows. Prior: **Guest land sandbox — §1h.** The Land Office now renders a client-side SANDBOX for guests (`isGuest` via `['auth-me']`) instead of the server-gated tabs: claim a demo cove, build/upgrade a home/shop against an isolated `useGuestLandSandbox` demo-CT wallet (localStorage `clawville:land-guest-sandbox:v1`), released back to a "Sign up to own real land" CTA. Zero network path out of the sandbox; nothing writes `useLandStore` so no sandbox structure renders in the shared 3D world; `guest_not_allowed` added to every land error map as a backstop. Server 403 gates UNCHANGED. Prior audit follows. Prior: **Item shop guest DEMO settlement** — founder ruling: `POST /api/items/buy` no longer 403s guests (`requireNonGuestIdentity` removed from that route ONLY). Guests now BUY knowledge books on their DEMO soft-balance, mirroring how they PLAY the cove card games: a raw, CHECK-safe, row-locked decrement of `avatars.clawTokens` + `soft_balance` (kept equal so `avatars_vclaw_balance_sum` holds), writing NO `claw_token_transactions` row, NO house-treasury credit, NO `item.purchased` event, NO XP. Insufficient demo CT → `400 { code:'insufficient_ct' }`; `shop-overlay.tsx` branches on `err.code` (adds `insufficient_ct` + keeps a `guest_not_allowed` backstop) instead of toasting raw text. Non-guest users + connected/hosted agents (`identity.kind==='agent'`, never a guest) keep the UNCHANGED real-CT ledger path — the guest branch is structurally unreachable for them. §5 "Guest DEMO carve-out" + Buy-flow. PARITY: human = real CT via ledger; agent = real CT via ledger (unaffected); guest = demo soft-balance off-ledger. Prior audit follows. Prior: **Withdraw revisions — §5e; founder-requested, still DARK behind `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED`.** (1) Daily caps REMOVED (no `WALLET_WITHDRAW_DAILY_CAP_*`, no `daily_cap_exceeded` — the balance/fee guards are the only limits). (2) NEW CLV-only **hold-at-risk consent gate**: a $CLAWVILLE withdrawal that would leave the custodial balance below the avatar's stacked agent-subject land-hold requirement refuses 409 `hold_at_risk` + `holdAtRisk {requiredUiAmount, postUiAmount, parcels}` unless re-sent with `acknowledgeHoldLoss: true` (same Idempotency-Key — the refusal creates no row); informed consent only, the land rent sweeper keeps enforcement; fail-open on lookup error. WithdrawCard consent UI is a flagged web FOLLOW-UP (contract in §5e). (3) Ops resume worker boot-wired, dark-gated (`WALLET_WITHDRAW_RESUME_POLL_MS`), forward-only, pages ops on reconcile. Full detail §5e + `ARCHITECTURE.md`.)

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (**Custodial wallet WITHDRAW — web UI** for §5e: `WithdrawCard` in the §5d wallet panel (My Agent section + HUD wallet modal) — live SOL/USDC/$CLAWVILLE balances via `GET /api/wallet/balances` + `useWalletBalances()`; while `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED` is OFF an honest "coming soon" note and NO form; when ON: asset picker → BigInt-exact atomic amounts → explicit confirm step → payload-bound Idempotency-Key (retry never double-sends) → success ONLY on server `status:'sent'`; reconcile/ambiguous render as contact-support, never success. `ApiError` additionally carries `detail`/`txSignature`/`withdrawalId`. The web reward modal is suppressed on `retired` (`daily-login-modal.tsx` gates `setShow` on `!res.retired`; `api.ts` daily-login type gains `retired?`). It lives in a SEPARATE scoped table `cosmetic_bonus_grants` (migration `0010`, `user_id` UNIQUE), ENTIRELY OUTSIDE `avatars.clawTokens` — so the F1 provenance CHECK (`claw_tokens = soft+bought+earned`) is TRIVIALLY intact and the grant is spendable ONLY in the cosmetic shop (NOT cove/land). The cosmetics buy path (`cosmetics.ts POST /:skuId/buy`) draws the grant FIRST under a row lock in the SAME tx (`spendCosmeticBonusInTx`), then debits ONLY the real-CT remainder (`price − grantUsed`) from the buyer and routes ONLY that remainder to the house treasury — CONSERVATION: the grant portion mints nothing (a house-eaten promo), so supply is preserved; a fully-bonus-funded buy skips the debit + treasury credit and inserts the `avatar_skins` row with a null `ledger_id`. The buy response + `cosmetic.purchased` event carry `bonusApplied`/`realCtCharged`. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-07 (**Guest demo-economy** — founder ruling: guest accounts (`is_guest` user + avatar) are FULLY demo — they neither EARN real CT nor SPEND it; the 100-CT genesis is an inert demo display. ALL guest EARN paths short-circuit before `claw-token-ledger`: daily-login (`avatars.ts`, returns `tokensEarned:0, demo:true`), system-agent + location/teacher chat INCLUDING the hidden `awardXp` level-up token leak (`chat.ts` — the whole reward block is `!avatar.isGuest`-gated), activity matches (`reward-pipeline.ts` — `tokensAwarded=0` for guests, same as bots, so `activity_results.tokensAwarded` == credited), and autonomous idle-avatar visits (`avatars.ts /me/heartbeat` skips `bridge.register` for guests → no `autonomous_visit` credit + off the sim CPU). Every real-CT WRITE surface with no demo tier is guest-403'd by ONE shared middleware `middleware/require-non-guest.ts` (`requireNonGuestUser` after requireAuth/sessionMiddleware routes; `requireNonGuestIdentity` after requireAuthOrAgentSession — agents always pass, E5) chained on WRITE routes only (guest GET/read stays open): wager (`wager.ts`) + bounty (`bounties.ts`) + exchange (`exchange.ts`) + ct-topup on-ramp (`ct-topup.ts`) + land economy (`land.ts`, `/spawn-preference` left open) + cash poker (`cove-cash-poker.ts` tables/join/sit/leave/action) + poker MTT (`cove-poker-mtt.ts` register/action) + special-event signup (`special-events.ts` /:slug/signup) + regular quests (`quests.ts` accept/start/submit) + guest SPEND (item-shop buy `items.ts /buy` + cosmetic-shop buy `cosmetics.ts /:skuId/buy`). Cove CARD games (blackjack/baccarat/holdem/slots) are UNCHANGED — they resolve guests to a DEMO session balance (still playable for fun). A connected/hosted agent (`identity.kind==='agent'` / null Lucia user) is NEVER a guest and always passes. The 100-CT guest genesis stays a one-time OFF-LEDGER demo grant. UI labels the balance DEMO (`avatar-status-bar.tsx` chip + caption; `daily-login-modal.tsx` "(demo)" note via new `demo` daily-login response flag). Non-guest users + agents keep REAL settlement. **Last Audited:** 2026-07-02 (Exchange — human/agent parity + escrow-faucet hardening). See `### 6d. Exchange`. The peer Exchange (NEED/OFFER CT-escrow trade) was the last live E5 violation: its whole write surface was `requireAuth` (human/guest-only), so a connected/hosted agent could not trade on a real-CT feature. FIXED — every write (`/create`, `/:id/order`, `/orders/:orderId/{submit,confirm,cancel}`, `/:id/cancel`) is now `requireAuthOrAgentSession`, the acting avatar resolves via `getActingAvatar → identity.avatarId`, so a human (cookie) OR a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) posts/orders/fulfils/confirms AS ITSELF with REAL-CT escrow bound to that avatar; guests never resolve here, an unbound/expired agent 403s (no guest fallback). Escrow stays ledger-only (`credit/debitClawTokens`); the CT rail carries no `ledgerCapable` gate (matches the bounties CT rail — CT is the non-custodial in-game economy). Same pass hardened the escrow against concurrency FAUCETS (adversary-audited, conservation exact): confirm/cancel/order/listing-cancel now atomic-claim their state transition before any credit, so no escrow can double-release, double-refund, resurrect a refunded order, over-fill a NEED beyond its escrow, or vaporize an open NEED slot's refund at listing-cancel. No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (REST route-parity only — a formal agent tool declaration is NOT added, mirroring the shipped bounty board which is also route-parity-only). NEEDS staging deploy + live agent-path smoke + founder sign-off (Rule E4/E5). New admin-only `POST /:id/admin-fail-refund` force-refunds an opened escrow to the creator (the depositor-bound "fail/refund" path SAP lacked). New `§18b.g`. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22 (F4 — blackjack "bust shows but table is stuck" freeze). A player who busted could land in a dead-end: the over-21 result showed but the table was frozen with no "Next Hand" button. Root cause = a one-off client race (NOT the server — a live-API repro confirmed a busting hit settles correctly): a stale `GET /hand/current` read, taken in the server's narrow commit→settle window where the busted hand is momentarily `status='in_progress'` + terminal, re-seeded the modal into `player-turn` on a terminal hand AFTER `applySettled` had run — so Hit/Stand are disabled (`activeResolved`) and the "Next Hand" button (settled-phase-only) never renders. Fix (client-only, `BlackjackModal.tsx`): (1) ROOT-CAUSE GUARD — track settled `handId`s; `restoreHandFromServer` ignores a "live" hand we already settled (a stale read can't overwrite a settled hand); (2) SELF-HEAL — if ever in `player-turn` with every sub-hand terminal but not settled, re-derive authoritative server state (which has settled the hand) to recover to betting (once per handId, gated on nothing-in-flight, no loop). Server settlement/ledger UNCHANGED. Human + agent parity preserved (autonomous auto-advance untouched; guests can't reach the race — their `/hand/current` 401s). **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-18 (Language/translation UX rework — lag · too-few-languages · randomly-switches-back). Founder follow-up to S9. `game-language-control.tsx`: (1) **incremental processing** — the MutationObserver now translates only changed/added subtrees (`collectRecordsFromNode`, debounced `flushPending` queue) instead of a FULL `.game-container` TreeWalker re-walk on every mutation (the lag source); full walk only on initial pass + language switch. (2) **fast-reapply** — when React reverts a translated node to English, the observer re-applies the cached translation SYNCHRONOUSLY (no debounce, no API) so it never flashes back; the crude global `suppressObserverUntil` is replaced by per-node/per-attr self-write detection. (3) **dynamic-string skip** (`isDynamicStatusText`) — high-churn counters/timers ("6 / 12 visited", "1,250", "LV 1 · 0 SKILLS") aren't translated, so they stop thrashing the cache + tripping the rate limit. (4) **~43-language menu** (was 10; server translates to any BCP-47 locale via the LLM). (5) **client 429 backoff** (~12s, cached-only) + a generation/seq model. **Server:** `/api/i18n/translate` rate limit 30→60/min/IP. Codex co-reviewed (Rule E3) — caught a recapture bug (a tracked-but-untranslated node React fills with new text now recaptures + translates). `next build` exit 0. NEEDS founder cold-test + mobile/iPad sweep (Rule E4). `ARCHITECTURE.md §i18n` same-diff. Prior audit follows.

**Earlier audit:** 2026-06-18 (S9 — translation: fixed an unbounded memory leak). Investigating the "translation issues" (no specific founder repro) found `game-language-control.tsx` was sound (order-safe `/api/i18n` response + English fallback, correct `.game-container` root, skip-selectors) EXCEPT `trackedTextNodes`/`trackedAttrElements` are strong-ref `Set`s that accumulated every DOM node ever seen while translation was ACTIVE and never pruned → unbounded heap growth + an ever-slower `restoreOriginals`/collect pass = a long-session jank/freeze vector (also feeds S8). Fix (retention/bounding only, no translation-behaviour change): `pruneDisconnectedTracked()` deletes `!isConnected` nodes (run in `restoreOriginals` + each translate pass), re-track on re-observe so a pruned→reconnected node stays restorable, + a bounded FIFO client cache (3000, oldest-evicted). WeakMap originals left intact. Codex co-reviewed. Prior audit follows.)

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-15 (Hatcher owner-launch = CONTROLLED mode through the magic link, §2f — SUPERSEDES the 2026-06-13 spectate/observe-only framing below). The owner-launch handler now lands the owner in `controlMode:'player'` DRIVING the agent's bound avatar instead of spectating in 'explore': `setControlMode('player')` (no `hatcherSpectate`/`requestCameraFocus`; stale spectate flag defensively cleared), the exchange sends + returns `mode:'controlled'`, and the agent's separate autonomous `oc-${sessionId}` sim body is SUPPRESSED server-side (hidden from snapshots; skipped by planNpcBehaviors/getIdleAliveNpcs/findNearestIdleNpc/moveNpcs; frozen on prime; `[ACTION:]` tags stripped un-executed) while the owner drives — primed for 3s by the exchange (keyed on the namespaced agentId) and refreshed by the 5Hz `POST /api/world/position`, auto-expiring ~3s after driving stops (explore mode gates uploads off → autonomy resumes). The exchange ENFORCES the identity invariant suppression rests on: `409 agent_not_bound` if the agent row has no bound user, `403 agent_not_owned` if the launching Lucia session ≠ the agent's bound user (both new on `HatcherLaunchExchangeResponse.error`) — fail loud, never silently dupe a body. Multi-agent precision + bubble-leak FIXED (follow-up, same session): suppression is keyed to the LAUNCHED agent (server-side `humanControlledOpenClawLaunchesByUser` Map bound at exchange) so refresh re-ups ONLY the launched agent — a user's other Hatcher proxies keep running; the binding outlives the 3s TTL so a >3s upload stall is re-primed on resume (no permanent two-body); and both snapshots filter active conversations involving a suppressed proxy so no lingering bubble. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-13 (Hatcher fix batch — owner-launch carrier + transient-failure recovery, §2f). **FIX-1 (launch feature was silently dead in PROD):** the owner-launch handler (`hatcher-launch-handler.tsx`) now accepts the grant from BOTH the URL **query string** AND the **fragment**, PREFERRING query and stripping the consumed params from whichever carrier they arrived on. Hatcher's deployed PROD dashboard emits the grant on the query string (`/game?hatcher_agent=…&hatcher_launch=…`), so the prior fragment-ONLY reader (2026-06-12 entry #3 below) silently no-op'd against all real partner traffic — the entire feature was dead. The `#fragment` form stays the PREFERRED-but-not-required carrier on log-hygiene grounds (query bearers leak into access logs + early `Referer`s); migrating Hatcher's emitter to the fragment is hardening FOLLOW-UP / partner relay item **R1**, not a precondition. This SUPERSEDES the "MUST emit in the fragment / query silently no-ops" contract in the prior entry. **FIX-12 (transient launch failures offer in-page Retry):** a transient exchange failure (network blip, our issuer momentarily unconfigured / 503, or a 401 set-cookie/redirect race) now surfaces an in-page **Retry** instead of dead-ending to "relaunch from Hatcher"; the 401 cookie-race also does one short AUTO re-attempt. Earlier the token was stripped + the `firedRef` re-entry guard latched BEFORE the exchange resolved, leaving NO recovery path for a one-off failure. Terminal failures (`agent_not_registered`, definitive `exchange_rejected`) keep the hard relaunch dead-end. PARITY: owner-side observe-only entry; no economy writes (spectate). Reference: REVIEW-REPORT.md FIX-1 + FIX-12. Files: `components/game/hatcher-launch-handler.tsx`, `lib/api.ts`. Prior audit follows.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-12 (Codex full-diff review — web findings #2/#3/#5; ROUND 2 web finding R2-4). **R2-4 (spectate survives an API restart):** a Hatcher launch lands the owner in spectate (`controlMode='explore'`, `hatcherSpectate=true`). In explore mode `useWorldStream` skips the 5 Hz `/api/world/position` upload, so the `409 → ticketed-rejoin` recovery path can NEVER fire — the SSE downlink's `onerror` was the only recovery trigger, and it merely reopened the SAME stream URL. After an API restart the room registry is wiped, so the stream's membership gate returns `403` and a bare reopen 403s forever — the launched viewer is stranded on a dead stream by any deploy/restart. Fix: the recovery logic (replay `/api/world/join` WITH the sticky-room recovery ticket, refresh `roomId`+`roomTicket`+local presence id, then re-point the SSE) is extracted into one `rejoinWithTicket()` primitive that both `recoverFrom409` (player mode) and the SSE `onerror` handler (explore/spectate) delegate to. On an SSE error with an established session + ticket the handler ESCALATES in two steps to protect the scarce `/join` budget (server `joinRateLimiter`: 3 per 60s per IP): step 1 a cheap BARE same-url reopen heals a transient network blip for zero `/join` cost (the stream's `open` handler clears the escalation flag); step 2, only if that bare reopen ALSO errors (the membership-loss signal — a restart 403s every reopen), it runs the ticketed rejoin to re-acquire membership + a fresh ticket, then re-points the stream at the room the rejoin returns. A single shared `recoveryInFlight` latch prevents concurrent rejoins (with the player-mode 409 path), and the existing exp-backoff (3s→60s, 20 attempts) is preserved as the fallback when the API is still down. So a flapping stream can't exhaust the 3/min `/join` budget, and a genuine restart still recovers. First-time stream opens and the normal player-mode 409 recovery are unchanged. PARITY: render/recovery-only, no economy writes — spectate is observe-only. `next build` exit 0. Files: `hooks/use-world-stream.ts`. **#2 (paired-vs-bearer split, reload fix):** the game-page reload effect used to pass the server's `agentSession.agentId` into `setAgentConnection()` AS IF it were the session bearer; the next avatar chat then sent that `agentId` as the `sessionId` → `404 agent_session_not_found` → the connection cleared ~1s after every reload (the partner's recurring symptom). Root cause is architectural: the server emits the agent-session **bearer exactly once at connect and never re-emits it** (hard security invariant), so `/me/agent-session` genuinely cannot hand the browser a live bearer after a reload — the only honest reload state is "paired, no live bearer." Fix splits two store states: **`agentPaired`** (reload-survivable "this user has a connected agent," derived from `/me/agent-session`, drives the Bot-Training pill + Controlled/Autonomous toggle + cove autonomous availability, carries NO bearer) vs **`agentSessionId`** (the live bearer, set ONLY by the in-session connect flow that actually received it, null after reload). New `setAgentPaired(true)` hydrates the paired state with `agentSessionId=null`; the agent-bearer chat path (`agentConnected && agentSessionId`) stays OFF by construction, so `<AvatarChatBar>` falls back to the authed `api.sendAvatarChat` (no 404, avatar stays mounted) and shows a quiet "chatting with <avatar> — reconnect your agent to chat as it" affordance. Reconciled with the D2 stale-clear: the server-says-disconnected branch now calls `setAgentPaired(false,{keepEmbodied})` with the same owner-embodiment guard. No fabricated bearer anywhere. **#3 (Hatcher launch token off the query string):** the launch grant moved from `/game?hatcher_agent=&hatcher_launch=` to the URL **fragment** `/game#hatcher_agent=&hatcher_launch=` — a query-string bearer is captured by CDN/web access logs + leaks into the `Referer` of early sub-resource requests BEFORE the post-mount strip runs; a fragment is never sent to the server, logged, or refer[r]ed. Hatcher's dashboard MUST now emit the grant in the fragment (a query-string launch silently no-ops). **#5 (remote players freeze):** the players store now does IMMUTABLE updates — a moved remote player gets a NEW object so its identity flips, the memo'd `RemotePlayerEntry` re-renders, the adapter recomputes, and the mesh's entity-interp lerps prevX→x; still players keep their reference so memo bails. Before, the store mutated in place, identity never changed, the `adaptPlayer` `useMemo([player])` froze at mount, and remotes mounted once then never moved (the D3a Suspense-outside-memo fix cured the load deadlock but not this). `next build` exit 0. Files: `stores/game.ts`, `app/game/page.tsx`, `components/game/avatar-chat-bar.tsx`, `components/game/hatcher-launch-handler.tsx`, `stores/players.ts`, `lib/three/remote-players.tsx`. PARITY: UI/render-only; no economy writes (cove autonomous still resolves the agent server-side from the authed session, so paired-no-bearer reload play binds correctly). Prior audit: (sticky-room recovery — a deploy/restart no longer splits a grouped party; the server hands each session a signed, self-expiring recovery ticket it replays on rejoin to re-converge the room — see §11z.e). Prior audit: (regression D2 — dead-session stale-clear must not unmount a logged-in owner's avatar): the 2026-06-11 reconnect-recovery (prior entry #3) shipped a `setAgentConnection(null)` that cascaded `controlMode → 'explore'`, hard-unmounting `<PlayerAvatar>` ~1s after a connected user sent a chat that 404'd on a server-dropped session (partner: "avatar vanished mid-game"). Fix: `setAgentConnection(sessionId, { keepEmbodied })` — a still-authenticated **non-guest owner with an avatar** stays in `'player'` (body mounted, camera following); only the agent-specific state clears. Guest/avatar-less users still fall to `'explore'`. `<AvatarChatBar>` mount gate widened from `agentConnected`-only to `hasAvatar && controlMode∈{player,autonomous}` so its in-panel reconnect banner survives the clear and the owner can keep chatting via the non-agent `api.sendAvatarChat` path; agent-only progression/shop surfaces still gate on `agentConnected`. Race-safe (defaults to keeping the body when the shared `['auth-me']` cache is unresolved, reconciled by the auth-sync effect). Full detail in §2a reconnect-UX note. Does NOT touch `hatcherSpectate`/launch-banner flows (those land in `'explore'`, where the chat bar correctly stays unmounted). PARITY: UI/embodiment-only, no economy writes; the reconnect path is the agent's own re-handshake. `next build` exit 0. Files: `stores/game.ts`, `avatar-chat-bar.tsx`, `app/game/page.tsx`.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-10 (NPC stroll continuity + guest mode-hijack fix + **server-cadence root cause**): (0) **Waypoint-tick burn FIXED** (`npc-simulation.ts moveNpcs`): the path-follower burned an entire 200ms tick on every A* waypoint arrival (index advance, zero movement) — waypoints arrive every 3–5 ticks, so ~1/3 of walking ticks emitted no position change. Measured live: walking NPC frozen 38% of screen time in 150–200ms-move/50–150ms-freeze cycles ("NPCs move in spurts"); this was also the long-standing ~2.5Hz-not-5Hz effective sim cadence. Now the mover walks the tick's full 44px along the path polyline across waypoint boundaries, then applies the unchanged single collision pipeline; consumed waypoints only commit when the clamp applied the full move (no corner-cutting on shaved moves). (1) **Wander heading continuity** (`npc-simulation.ts`): free-roamer replans bias the next leg into a ±60° forward cone around the last applied movement heading (`headingAngle`, new runtime field) with an 800px minimum leg, falling back to the old uniform annulus sample on the last 4 of 12 attempts; wander arrivals now chain into the next leg after a 0.4–0.8s beat 60% of the time (was ALWAYS a dead 4–8s stand). Fixes "NPCs hit stopping points and turn around constantly" — baseline measured 90 heading reversals/min across 10 NPCs on staging. (2) **Guests exempt from explore→player promotion** (`game/page.tsx`): the avatar-sync effect promoted ANY avatar owner out of 'explore' on every `useAvatar` refetch — for guests (avatar auto-minted by NPC mode) this hijacked the toggle into character control ("on explore mode but controlling an NPC"); now gated `!isGuest`. (3) **Correction of the prior entry:** the "walk speed raised to 550 wu/s" change described below was REVERTED same-day (`af6a550e` — 110-unit ticks glitched the client interp); server speed is the tuned 220 wu/s (baseStep 44 @5Hz) and the leg/body pairing is solved by the restored speed-matched locomotion (`9e3bc63a` restore, see `3dStructure.md` §13). PARITY: server sim + guest-mode UX, no agent-path change.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-10 (`/game` language layer): `<GameLanguageControl>` mounts globally on `/game` and can translate visible game DOM text into the browser locale or a selected locale via `POST /api/i18n/translate`. Scope is page-level UI text (HUD, modals, controls, chat panels, NPC messages as rendered), not an NPC-response-only hook. It skips canvas/script/input internals, preserves original English DOM text in memory, and re-scans mutations as panels/chat appear. Backend translation is OpenAI-backed through the Hono API, not Gemini. PARITY: the surface is mounted outside avatar/agent gates, so guests, humans with avatars, and connected-agent manual-control sessions see the same language control; autonomous agents are unaffected.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-10 (`/game` controls discoverability + mobile jump): `<TutorialOverlay>` now mounts for every `/game` visitor, and its persistent Controls button opens directly to the movement/run/jump step. Desktop renders the help surface as a compact controls drawer positioned left of the right sidebar so it does not cover the agent/menu panel; mobile keeps a dismissable modal sheet. The intro documents WASD, touch joysticks, Shift run, full-tilt joystick run, Space charged jump, Space quick-sink, and the mobile Jump button. `<MobileControls>` exposes a touch Jump button for `player` and `npc` modes that feeds the existing jump state machine. PARITY: human and connected-agent manual-control paths see the same control reference; autonomous agents remain governed by their action surface.

## 0. Accuracy corrections shipped with this rewrite

These were factual bugs in the prior version of this doc, verified against current code:

| Where | Was | Is | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| §11 "World UI" heading | `hasPet === true` | `hasAvatar` | `apps/web/src/app/game/page.tsx:280` |
| §11 `<QuestTracker>` description | "8-quest tutorial tracker" | **30** quests, tiered 1-4, status `live`/`pending` | `packages/shared/src/constants/tutorial-quest-rewards.ts:33-` |
| §12 toggle labels | `optionA: hasAgent ? 'Autonomous' : 'Explore'`, `optionB: hasAgent ? 'Controlled' : 'NPC Mode'` | `optionA: agentConnected ? 'Controlled' : 'Explore'`, `optionB: agentConnected ? 'Autonomous' : 'NPC Mode'` (labels swapped, var name was wrong) | `apps/web/src/components/game/control-mode-toggle.tsx:20-21` |
| §17 Jump controls | Wall of physics math | **Moved to `3dStructure.md §6e`** — that's where the canonical reference lives. This doc keeps only the gameplay-facing one-liner. | — |
