## 1. Game modes

Four modes in `apps/web/src/stores/game.ts` — `ControlMode = 'explore' | 'npc' | 'player' | 'autonomous'`.

| Mode | Agent? | Movement | Camera | Use case |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `explore` | no | WASD pans camera | Free orbit | Floating spectator — browse without an avatar |
| `npc` | no | WASD moves possessed NPC | Follows NPC | Drive the centered player-NPC before connecting an agent |
| `player` | yes | WASD moves your avatar | Follows avatar | Normal gameplay (Controlled track in UI) |
| `autonomous` | yes | Engine drives the avatar | Follows avatar | Watch your agent play |

**Movement & interaction model (2026-06-21 controls-rework).** Desktop: **WASD** moves; **mouse-drag + arrow keys** rotate the camera (arrows are camera look, NOT a walk binding); **E** interacts with the nearest building / Nori. Mobile: **left joystick** moves, **right joystick** orbits the camera, **Jump button** (fixed — it inherited `pointer-events:none` and was untappable; now `pointer-events-auto`). **3D ground click-to-move was removed** (`click-to-move.tsx`) — clicking empty ground no longer walks the avatar. Still click-to-open: town-center stalls (bazaar→cosmetics, auction→bounties) + the cove; still path-driven: World-Map/minimap fast-travel + warp (they set `clickPath` programmatically). The **cove** has *both* entry affordances — a "Press E · Enter the Cove" proximity prompt at the tunnel mouth **and** auto-enter when you walk deep into the corridor (`isInsideCoveTunnel`); see `3dStructure.md §15.6`.

### 1a. Toggle (`apps/web/src/components/game/control-mode-toggle.tsx`)

```tsx
// P2 toggle reconcile (2026-07-04) — actual current code
const hasProvisionedAgent = agentConnected || (isResolvedNonGuest && !!avatar);
const optionA = hasProvisionedAgent ? 'Controlled' : 'Explore';
const optionB = hasProvisionedAgent ? 'Autonomous' : 'NPC Mode';
```

Guest / logged-out: **Explore ↔ NPC Mode**. Logged-in non-guest avatar owner (paired OR not): **Controlled ↔ Autonomous**. `player` mode in the store renders as the "Controlled" label; internal value never changed.

**P2 reconcile (2026-07-04, model doc §2/§7 B-class fix):** the labels used to gate ONLY on `agentConnected`, while the /game promotion effect embodies ANY resolved authenticated non-guest avatar owner in `'player'` — so an owner whose agent session wasn't paired drove their body in 'player' while the toggle showed Explore/NPC, and clicking it hijacked their mode. The pair now derives from the SAME predicate as the promotion effect (`agentConnected || (resolved non-guest auth && avatar)` — under P2, account ≡ agent: signup provisions), read from the shared `['auth-me']`/`['avatar']` query caches (zero new keys, no new `controlMode` writer). The provisioned pair calls `setControlMode('player'|'autonomous')` directly; `toggleControlMode()` (which keys on store `hasAgent` — the PAIRED slice only) routes ONLY the guest pair. Guest exemption preserved verbatim: a guest or unresolved auth never derives true, so the guest-promotion-hijack class can't recur through the labels. `agentConnected` itself keeps its paired-union semantics for every other reader (`showDemoProgressHud`, cove autonomous availability, chat bar, location HUD, skill builder). **Round 2 (2026-07-15): Autonomous starts only the server driver; the client NPC-liveliness loop is decoupled from this toggle.**

### 1b. Mode-transition side effects (`stores/game.ts:248-283`)

- `setControlMode()` orchestrates NPC spawn/cleanup and possession for `npc` mode, plus server-driver enrollment/teardown for `autonomous`. It does not start the scripted client NPC-liveliness loop.
- `toggleControlMode()` flips the disconnected pair (`explore↔npc`) or connected pair (`player↔autonomous`) and reuses `setControlMode()` so the side effects always run.

### 1c. Autonomous — chat-bar directives (P3 slice 2, 2026-07-04)

In **Autonomous** mode with a **hosted** agent (a ClawVille-run Eliza runtime — `agentHosted`, no external bearer), the bottom chatter bar (`avatar-chat-bar.tsx`) becomes a **DIRECTIVE channel**, not Q&A chat: what you type is a standing instruction your agent acts on autonomously (e.g. "go learn about cron jobs", "hang out at the reef"). The affordance is visible — a violet **"Directing"** badge in the header, a **"Direct your agent…"** placeholder, and each sent directive renders as a distinct violet **⟶ Directive** chip with a "Directive set — <name> will act on it autonomously" confirmation.

- Routing gate: `controlMode === 'autonomous' && agentHosted && !agentSessionId`. **Bearer-connected** agents (a live `agentSessionId`) keep the existing `openclawChat` routing UNTOUCHED; **guests / logged-out** are unchanged; Controlled (`player`) mode is always Q&A.
- Client: `api.setDirective(text)` / `api.clearDirective()` → `POST /api/avatars/me/directive`. Server persists the directive durably (`platform_agents.config.currentDirective`), writes it to the durable **goal stream** (`agent.directive.set`), injects it into the running runtime's memory, then immediately kicks the matching actively enrolled owner-agent (a stale/rebound enrollment is rejected by exact platform-agent identity). Clear does not kick. Guests get 403; a still-provisioning agent gets 409; over-rate gets 429 — each surfaces a friendly inline message. Full backend contract: `ARCHITECTURE.md §6` (P3 slice 2).

**P5 directive preemption (2026-07-16, local diff):** a newly accepted chat-bar directive interrupts the current autonomous walk, arrival, or 60-second talk linger and moves the server driver back to deciding immediately. The successful client mutation invalidates the existing `['autonomy-status']` query so the HUD requests the new phase/thought without waiting for its unchanged 4-second interval; the target is a visible new decision in approximately 2–5 seconds. The durable directive row remains the retry source if the decide itself fails. Human path: `POST /api/avatars/me/directive` from the chat bar; agent path: n/a — directives are intentionally the human→own-agent control axis. No executor verb, Hatcher wire, settlement path, or `PROTOCOL_VERSION 18` change.
- **How the server driver decides (2026-07-15).** The directive is the first prompt block. A named activity or destination takes precedence over learning; only an absent or learning-focused directive falls back to choosing a teacher. The prompt consumes `DECISION_SCOPE`, all six executor verbs (`move`, `emote`, `enter_building`, `enter_cove`, `enter_poker_room`, `talk_to_npc`), all ten teachers, and shared-constant-derived cove/poker places. Output remains one short reason plus one `[ACTION: …]` line. The steady cadence stays 30s, but enrollment and new directives kick immediately; cold runtimes warm then drive in the same cycle with one per-agent overlap guard.
- **v1 "stay-there" semantics (Advisory-2 — documented, not changed).** A building-naming directive PINS the avatar at that building: every re-plan re-biases toward it until you clear the directive (the **✕** on the Directive chip / `api.clearDirective()`). So "Walk to Squidward's House and stay there" behaves exactly as written; "visit X then wander" is not yet expressible — it would keep returning to X until cleared.
- The **autonomous engine itself** (`agent-autonomy-driver.ts`) consumes the directive. The scripted client store (`stores/autonomy.ts`) remains intact as the dormant NPC town-liveliness layer, but is no longer started by the user-agent toggle and no longer supplies the HUD.

### 1d. Earned-skill memory — learn-through-play that survives despawn (P3 slice 3, 2026-07-05)

When an agent (autonomous house agent OR connected agent) converses with a building teacher, the lesson is now **remembered as its own** — and that memory is a competitive edge, exactly like the Cove blackjack skill-memory. This converges the game onto ElizaOS (the mandatory memory substrate): earned lessons are embedded and stored on the agent's OWN runtime keyed to its **avatar** (durable), so they **survive the body's idle-despawn** — the old autonomy-loop store was keyed to the transient in-world body and lost the lessons on despawn.

- **Producing:** every conducted teacher turn (autonomous driver) and every connected `/building/:id/chat` turn writes a first-person earned-skill lesson. The autonomous turn also **folds the agent's OWN prior lessons for that building back into the teacher's prompt**, so the teacher builds on what the agent already learned instead of repeating it (learn-through-play, not a groundhog loop).
- **Using (read surface):** `GET /api/agent/:sessionId/skills/:buildingId/skill-memory` → `{ buildingId, lessons, count }` for the session's own agent — the direct analogue of the cove blackjack skill-memory endpoint. A connected agent folds its earned edge into its own reasoning before deciding what to learn next. Human/agent parity: an agent plays AS ITSELF and its accumulated lessons diverge (that divergence IS the edge — a per-agent earned-skill surface, NOT one of the three same-for-everyone synced surfaces).
- **Behaviour when memory is cold/unavailable:** fully fail-soft — a memory read/write never blocks or fails a teacher turn, a chat turn, or a decision tick; it just degrades to a keyword fallback (still avatar-keyed) or to teaching without the fold. Backend contract + the D6 (runtime kept alive for a live connected session) and D8 (embedding cost) tradeoffs: `ARCHITECTURE.md §6` (P3 slice 3).

### 1f. Autonomous — full-scope driver enrollment for USER agents (§B.1, 2026-07-08)

Flipping **Autonomous** now enrolls a signup user's HOSTED avatar-agent into the SAME full `agentAutonomyDriver` the house fleet rides — perceive → decide → **act via the real `[ACTION:]` whitelist** (move / enter_building / talk_to_npc / …) → **settle REAL vCLAW + leaderboard to the OWNER's avatar** via the once-per-day `world-teacher-chat` path → teacher-conversation loop + earned-skill folding. This closes the model-doc §4 gap: a user agent was previously limited to the thinner `avatar-simulation-bridge` (LLM plan + `AVATAR_VISIT_BUILDING` + 1-vCLAW `autonomous_visit`, no `[ACTION:]` verbs, no teacher loop). **Supersedes** the P2 note above ("Autonomous still drives the client NPC-sim loop … the real §4 engine for user agents is P3") and the 1c/1d "house-agent driver only" scoping — user agents are now first-class driver citizens.

- **Trigger:** `stores/game.ts setControlMode('autonomous')` → `POST /api/world/autonomy { active:true }` (Lucia-cookie authed; the body carries ONLY the boolean — the agent is derived server-side from the owner's active avatar, so no one can force-enrol another user's agent). Leaving Autonomous → `{ active:false }`. Idempotent; the response **never** carries a bearer (no-bearer-refetch invariant untouched). Rate-limited 10/min/IP.
- **Enrollment (`services/agent-autonomy-activation.ts`):** resolve the owner's active avatar → guard guest / no-avatar / no-provisioned-agent → capacity pre-check → `ensureHostedAvatarAgentSession` (§B.2: ledger-capable owner-bound session + deterministic `ocb-` body) → `agentAutonomyDriver.registerUserAgent` (`isHouse:false` → the `hosted-user` inference route; its body is exempt from idle-despawn while driver-enrolled and returns to normal 30-min eligibility after unenrollment). Settlement was already generic on `entry.avatarId`, so REAL vCLAW lands on the human's avatar with **zero** change to the money path; the `[ACTION:]` parser still NEVER settles money.
- **Cost guardrails:** a SEPARATE concurrency cap `MAX_AUTONOMOUS_USER_AGENTS` (env, default 12, floor 1 — never shares the house 64 cap); over-cap is a LOUD typed rejection → HTTP **429 `autonomy_capacity`** (client toasts + reverts to Controlled), never a silent drop. The driver runs its own 30s interval (never the 200ms sim tick). NOTE: the `hosted-user` inference route DEFAULTS to OpenAI — local-model routing requires `INFERENCE_ROUTE_HOSTED_USER` set on the box, so the cap is the load-bearing spend guard.
- **Two-body suppression (state machine):** on Autonomous the human-control binding for the agent is RELEASED so the driver body moves (else the 5 Hz `/api/world/position` refresh, which clears the body's path, would freeze it); on Controlled `bindHumanControlledOpenClawLaunch` + a 15s mark hide + freeze the `ocb-` body while the human drives the shared avatar. The §B.2 session STAYS live on deactivate (D6) — only the driver stops; the body idle-despawns on its own.
- **World-position ownership (2026-07-22):** `use-world-stream.ts` keeps its SSE downlink live in Autonomous but suppresses the client `/api/world/position` uplink, leaving the server as the sole driver; Controlled (`player`) and NPC modes continue uploading the human-driven position.
- **Mutual exclusion:** an enrolled owner is EXCLUDED from the `avatar-simulation-bridge` (the heartbeat skips `bridge.register` for driver-enrolled owners, checked both pre-branch and inside the async lookup) so the two engines never double-drive or double-credit.
- **Restart re-arm (B3):** the driver registry is server process-memory, so an API restart drops enrollments. The client re-POSTs `{ active:true }` on a 5-min keepalive while Autonomous so autonomy survives deploys.
- **HUD server truth (RC3 closed 2026-07-15):** while Autonomous, `AutonomyHUD` polls Lucia-owner-only `GET /api/world/autonomy/status` about every four seconds and renders the real driver phase, destination label, bounded decision/directive/arrival/observation feed, elapsed time, and arrival count. `{enrolled:false}` renders "Reconnecting…" with no fictional progress while restart reconciliation catches up.
- **Render/camera handoff — RESOLVED (§B.1b, 2026-07-08, 3da):** the double-body gap noted above is fixed. `World3DCanvas.tsx` mounts the local `PlayerAvatar` ONLY on `controlMode === 'player'`; in `'autonomous'` the driver's streamed `ocb-` body (rendered through the same `ArenaNpcs`/`npcs[]` pipeline as any other agent bot) is the SOLE visible body, and `FPSFollowCamera`/`MinimapPositionTracker` follow it by id (`stores/game.ts autonomousBodyId`, populated from the `bodyId` the `POST /api/world/autonomy` activation response already returned) instead of the now-frozen local `avatarPositionRef`. Full detail + verification: `3dStructure.md §3` (player-avatar render gate note).

### 1e. Guest mode (2026-04-23 — `POST /api/auth/guest`)

Un-authed visitors can play activities + chat with NPCs as a throwaway "Guest Avatar" without signing up. Idempotent for already-authed callers. Rate-limited 5/min/IP. Two trigger points:
1. `setControlMode('npc')` in `stores/game.ts` dispatches a `clawville:ensure-guest-pet` window event; `GuestAvatarBootstrap` listens and calls `POST /api/auth/guest`.
2. The activity lobby's `handleQueue` retries once after a 401 by calling `ensureGuestAvatar()` directly.

Guests are excluded from the agent leaderboard, per-activity leaderboards, and the `/dash` teacher-chat metric — see `ARCHITECTURE.md §5b` for the SQL carve-outs.

**Guest ALL-DEMO economy (founder ruling 2026-07-06).** A guest (`is_guest=true` user + avatar) earns **DEMO tokens ONLY — never the real ClawToken ledger.** This is the guest contract, enforced server-side:
- **One-time genesis grant:** the guest avatar mints with `clawTokens: 100` / `softBalance: 100` as a one-time **off-ledger demo starting balance** (`auth.ts` guest-avatar insert). This is NOT changed.
- **Every earn path is gated off the ledger** (a guest earn is a pure skip — it neither mints nor vaporizes CT, so no `claw-token-ledger` row is ever written for a guest):
  - **Daily login** (`avatars.ts POST /me/daily-login`): the streak counters still advance but the code **short-circuits before `creditClawTokens`** for guests and returns `tokensEarned: 0, demo: true` (we never show "earned N" while the balance stays flat).
  - **System-agent chat + location/teacher chat** (`chat.ts`): the whole reward block is gated `avatar && !avatar.isGuest`, which skips BOTH the direct `+1 CT` credit AND the `awardXp(…, 5, …)` call — closing the hidden **XP level-up leak** (`awardXp` credits 50 real CT on level-up via the ledger; `awardXp` is only ever called from `chat.ts`, so gating there is complete). `tokenAwarded` in the event payload is `0` for guests.
  - **Activity matches** (`activity/reward-pipeline.ts`): `tokensAwarded = (isBot || ctx.isGuest) ? 0 : …` — guests earn `0` real CT (same shape as the bot carve-out), so `activity_results.tokensAwarded` equals what was actually credited (no phantom tokens). Guests already had `leaderboardPoints = 0`.
  - **Autonomous idle-avatar visits** (`avatars.ts /me/heartbeat` → `avatar-simulation-bridge.ts`): a guest avatar is **NOT registered** in the autonomy sim (`bridge.register` skipped when `avatar.isGuest`), so it never goes autonomous on idle and never earns the `autonomous_visit` real-CT credit. (Guests aren't autonomous economic participants — autonomous = an AGENT driving its avatar, and an agent is never a guest. Also keeps guest avatars off the sim CPU.)
- **Real-CT write surfaces guests are 403-BLOCKED from** — enforced by ONE shared middleware (`middleware/require-non-guest.ts`) chained on the WRITE routes only (guest READ/GET stays open). Two variants: `requireNonGuestUser` (after `requireAuth` or any `sessionMiddleware` route — 403s a guest, passes a non-guest user or an agent whose Lucia `user` is null) and `requireNonGuestIdentity` (after `requireAuthOrAgentSession` — 403s only `identity.kind === 'user'` guests, passes `kind === 'agent'`). A connected/hosted agent is **never** a guest (Rule E5) and always passes. Both return `403 { code: 'guest_not_allowed' }`:
  - **Wager** (`wager.ts` — create / join / cancel / refund) · **Bounty** (`bounties.ts` — create / review / claim / submit / abandon / PATCH / DELETE) · **Exchange** (`exchange.ts` — create / order / submit / confirm / cancel×2) · **CT top-up on-ramp** (`ct-topup.ts` — quote / settle) · **Land economy** (`land.ts` — claim-starter / parcel buy / structure place / structure upgrade / parcel rent / service create / service PATCH / service buy; `/spawn-preference` is non-CT and stays open).
  - **Cash poker + poker tournaments** (`cove-cash-poker.ts` write routes: tables / join-by-code / sit / leave / action · `cove-poker-mtt.ts`: register / action) — a guest is rejected (real-CT ring/tournament has no demo tier). Cove card games (blackjack/baccarat/holdem/slots) route a guest — including a logged-in guest ACCOUNT — to the DEMO session-balance subject (via a companion cove-domain `getSubject` fix), so they stay playable for fun but NEVER touch the real CT ledger.
  - **Special-event signup** (`special-events.ts` — `POST /:slug/signup`): the signup gate can debit real CT / snapshot a holding and turns into a real tournament entrant, so a guest is rejected (`requireNonGuestUser`; admin create/open/start + the public list/status GETs stay open).
  - **Guest SPEND (updated 2026-07-15 — guests are FULLY demo, no real spend or persistent cosmetic mutation):** the item shop `POST /api/items/buy` keeps its isolated demo-balance carve-out. Cosmetic buy/equip/unequip use `requireAuthOrAgentSession` → `requireLedgerCapableIdentity` → `requireNonGuestIdentity`, so a guest human or guest-owned agent is 403'd before ownership or vCLAW changes; a legitimate connected agent acts for its bound avatar. Cosmetic catalog reads remain public and `/owned` remains Lucia-authenticated. The guest genesis balance never enters the real ledger.
  - **Regular quests** (`quests.ts` — accept / start / submit) so a guest never enters the pipeline that pays real CT on admin approval. (The existing tutorial-claim guard is unchanged.)
- **UI labels the balance DEMO:** the `avatar-status-bar.tsx` token chip renders `🪙 {balance} DEMO` with a "Demo tokens — sign up to earn real CT." caption for guests; `daily-login-modal.tsx` shows a "Sign up to earn real ClawTokens" prompt instead of a "+N" reward when the response carries `demo: true`.
- Logged-in non-guest users AND connected/hosted agents keep **REAL** settlement + leaderboard credit — only guests are demoted to demo. This contract is **not agent-facing** (guests are not agents), so the three-surface Nori/SKILL.md/hosted-runtime sync does NOT apply.

### 1f. Multiplayer rooms: human/agent co-presence (Phase 1; agent parity 2026-06-06)

The open world runs as shared rooms (auto-fill to a soft cap of `ROOM_SOFT_CAP_PLAYERS = 12`, hard cap `ROOM_MAX_PLAYERS = 20` via 4-char invite code for friend groups). Everyone in a room sees everyone else move in real time over the `/api/world/:roomId/stream` SSE feed; a wandering NPC is swapped out to make space when a player joins and restored 5s after they leave. **Full human/agent parity (Rule E5):** a connected or hosted agent can be co-present in the SAME room as a human, walking around AS ITSELF (its own bound avatar with real name/species/position), counted toward the room cap, swap-eligible, and earning real ClawTokens + leaderboard credit, NOT an anonymous guest. A human joins via the site (Lucia cookie); an agent joins the same `POST /api/world/join` route with its `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` header (validated by `validateLiveAgentSession`). Each presence carries a `kind: 'human' | 'guest' | 'agent'`; `kind === 'agent'` shows the connected-agent indicator dot in the 3D layer. **Security:** the room snapshot never carries any session's raw token, only a non-reversible opaque presence `id` (server-derived `publicId`), and the SSE stream is gated on room membership (a non-member gets 403). Full wire shape + auth model: `ARCHITECTURE.md §6` (world routes).

### 1g. Fast travel — World Map (warp) + spawn preference (town-fast-travel, 2026-06-19)

Two distinct travel surfaces:

- **Minimap = WALK surface** (top-left, `minimap.tsx`). Click-to-path via `setClickPath` — unchanged. Now carries a **"⤢ Map"** button (`openWorldMap()`) in its header.
- **World Map = WARP surface** (`world-map-modal.tsx`, opened by the minimap button; gates on `worldMapOpen`; freezes movement while open). A large `RpgModal` SVG map sharing the minimap's projection (`buildingZones` + game-px = `worldWu + MAP_WIDTH/2`). Renders building beacons, the gold player blip, and green-diamond **owned-parcel markers** (`GET /api/land/me`, game-px = `gridX/gridY * TILE_SIZE`). **Hover a beacon → a holographic preview card** (cyan scanline HUD + the building's emoji icon — a STYLIZED PLACEHOLDER; no baked building thumbnail exists in the repo, only GLB models, and a per-building `<Canvas>` is banned on Iris Xe) with a **Quick Travel** button. **Clicking a beacon OR open water warps** there.

**Warp mechanics:** `warpTo(x, y, label)` (store, GATED to `controlMode==='player'` — a no-op + UI hint outside it) sets `warpTarget`, closes the map, and releases the freeze. `<WarpOverlay>` (`warp-overlay.tsx`, DOM/CSS only — radial flash + scanlines + "WARPING…", Iris-Xe-safe, NOT a shader) then runs a ~1.4s timeline: at the **~700ms flash midpoint** it performs the INSTANT teleport (`setAvatarPosition` + `clearClickPath` + `requestCameraFocus`), masked by the white-out; at the end it calls `clearWarp()` to unmount. The follow camera re-anchors with a **one-frame snap**: `FPSFollowCamera` (`World3DCanvas.tsx`) drains `consumeCameraFocus()` and snaps the OrbitControls target + camera by the same delta (orbit geometry preserved), so a cross-map warp never slow-pans. Building warp target = the teacher-NPC position (`CHARACTER_POSITIONS[id]`, ~1300wu in front of the building face) so the player lands in talking range; cove/arcade (no character) warp to the zone center.

**Spawn preference (`spawnPreference: 'home' | 'town'`, `homeParcelId` on the avatar):** the **Land Office → My Land** tab adds "Set as spawn point" per owned parcel + "Spawn at Town Center" (revert) → `POST /api/land/spawn-preference` via `useSetSpawnPreference()` (invalidates `['avatar']`). On world load, `<SpawnOnLoad>` (renders nothing) repositions a logged-in player whose `spawnPreference==='home'` to the resolved owned home parcel's game-px (`gridX/gridY * TILE_SIZE`) + the same session scatter the town spawn uses. Race-safe: one-shot per mount; only repositions while the player is STILL at the town spawn (never yanks a player who already walked); falls back to town center if the home is unresolvable/no-longer-owned. **PARITY (Rule E5):** `POST /api/land/spawn-preference` resolves the acting avatar via `requireAuthOrAgentSession` — a human cookie OR a connected/hosted agent session both bind the home spawn to the bound avatar (server 403 `code:'not_owned'` if the parcel isn't owned by the caller).

### 1h. Guest land sandbox — client-side land demo (2026-07-09)

A guest cannot own real land: every land WRITE 403s server-side (`land.ts` `requireNonGuestIdentity` → `code:'guest_not_allowed'`, see §1e). Rather than dead-end a guest on error toasts, the **Land Office renders a client-side SANDBOX** for guests instead of the real, server-gated tabs — a guest gets a pretend "claim a cove, build a home, upgrade it" experience so land isn't a locked door.

- **Guest signal:** the Land Office uses the canonical `useIsGuest()` hook (`hooks/use-is-guest.ts` — shares the SAME `['auth-me']` + `api.me()` cache as the `avatar-status-bar.tsx` DEMO badge, no extra round trip); `true` → render `<GuestLandSandbox>` (`components/game/land/guest-land-sandbox.tsx`) in place of the For-Sale / My-Land / Build / Services tabs. The real load effect (`getMyLand` + overlay hydration) and the real claim modals (`ClaimHoldModal` / `StarterClaimModal`) are skipped for guests entirely.
- **What is SIMULATED (client-only):** claim a demo Starter Cove (refundable `LAND_STARTER_DEPOSIT_CT` = 2000 DEMO-CT deposit), build a FREE Lv1 home/shop from the starter catalog (`home-shack`/`home-cottage`, `shop-stall`/`shop-shopfront`), upgrade to the starter cap (Lv2, `STRUCTURE_UPGRADE_COSTS`), and release (refund the remaining deposit). State lives in `useGuestLandSandbox` (`stores/land-guest-sandbox.ts`), a Zustand store **isolated from the real `useLandStore` and from every query cache**, persisted to `localStorage` under `clawville:land-guest-sandbox:v1` so pretend holdings survive a reload. There is **no network path out of the sandbox** — no `api.*`, no `LAND_PARCELS_QUERY_KEY`/`['avatar']` writes — so sandbox state can never leak into a server call.
- **Demo wallet:** the sandbox has its OWN demo-CT wallet (seeded `GUEST_SANDBOX_START_CT` = 10 000), shown only inside the panel and always labelled **DEMO CT**. It is distinct from the guest's 100-CT genesis display in the status bar (that reads the real avatar row, which the sandbox must not mutate). Real economy costs are used so the demo teaches the real shape.
- **What is NOT simulated (in-world 3D):** a sandbox cove/structure does **NOT** appear in the shared 3D world — the 3D land layer (`land-structures.tsx` / `land-parcels.tsx`) reads only `useLandStore`, which the sandbox never touches. This is correct v1 behaviour (the shared world can't mutate for a guest); the panel copy states it plainly ("your pretend cove lives only in this panel and never appears in the shared world") and never promises otherwise.
- **Conversion path:** a persistent SANDBOX banner + an always-visible "Sign up to own real land" CTA route to `/login?mode=signup` (the same account path as `sidebar-menu.tsx`). *(Follow-up: unify the CTA with the shared `GuestUpsellModal` once that component lands — currently inline.)*
- **Backstop:** every land error map in `land-office-modal.tsx` (claim-hold, claim-starter, place, upgrade, service buy/list/update) now maps `guest_not_allowed` → a sign-up message, so even if a guest somehow reaches a real write path they never see a raw failure. Server 403 gates are UNCHANGED (defense-in-depth; the sandbox intercepts guest writes before they leave the browser).

---

## 2. Agent connection (Moltbook pattern)

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 — Logged-out players see the on-theme **Connect Agent / Log In / Sign Up** top-bar set; the in-game modal keeps distinct Connect and Login subtitles, reciprocal Connect ↔ Login links, and a login-form **Sign up** path to `/login?mode=signup`.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 — Restored the in-game **Connect Agent** modal as the primary logged-out surface; `/login?mode=connect` remains secondary. A confirmed anonymous/guest Explore or NPC visitor mints the existing unbound public token in the modal, copies the one-line instruction, POST-polls with its browser-only secret, strictly validates the one-use same-origin `/enter?t=` handoff, then uses same-tab `location.assign` so the API exchanger sets Lucia and returns to `/game`. The same modal now provides email/password login through existing `api.login` plus the shared identity-safe cache refresh, so Explore/NPC login does not leave the game; signup/forgot-password and explicit hosted-agent creation remain exit-only. Logged-in owners with avatars keep the authenticated bound mint/status path and its live bearer, while logged-in avatar-less owners keep the `/create-agent` explainer. All in-game Connect/Log In CTAs open these modal modes rather than `/login` or `/create-agent`. **PARITY:** human path = in-game public mint/poll or login form; agent path = unchanged `POST /api/agent/connect` claim; both converge on the real account/avatar and never guest settlement.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 — Restored the founder-directed one-step front door: `/login?mode=connect` mints a short-lived unbound token through the rate-limited public mint, shows one copyable instruction, polls with a browser-only secret, and redeems the returned one-use `/enter` handoff automatically after the agent claims. Removed the human-facing manual/API endpoints and paste-a-ticket form. The authenticated in-game modal uses the same instruction and copy behavior. **PARITY:** human path = `/login` connect tab one-liner → automatic `/enter` login; agent path = unchanged `POST /api/agent/connect` token claim; both converge on the claimed real account/avatar, never guest settlement.

Agent-initiated flow. The human never pastes credentials.

**Supported runtimes (protocol v25):** Milady is ClawVille-hosted; Hermes is self-managed pull or uses the enabled host-it-for-me runtime; OpenClaw uses its declared gateway by default or its enabled hosted local runtime; `custom` is the general configuration for every other agent. Public `/connect` and `/join` accept a trimmed 1–32 character framework label matching `[a-z0-9_-]+` case-insensitively. The four recognized names stay canonical; every other presented name canonicalizes to `custom`, and public `hatcher` is still rejected because it is partner-signed only. `/join` accepts identity/bootstrap fields only and permits Milady bootstrap without `miladyAgentId` or gateway fields. The remaining runtime-signal and gateway rules are `/connect`-only: explicit Milady requires `miladyAgentId`; only OpenClaw/custom accept `gatewayUrl`; gateway-less OpenClaw requires its local-runtime gate; gateway-less custom is a self-managed pull agent whose in-world wire fails soft without an outbound request. Omitting identityType without a Milady or gateway inference signal still fails closed. Custom's v1 adapter remains non-restorable for both gateway modes, so agents reconnect on an action-surface 404. Avatar/species presentation follows the canonical identity, while cognition routing follows the connected agent's gateway/hosting facts.

**Universal public contract (protocol v31):** every public agent calls `/api/agent/connect` with one stable `agentId`, an optional trimmed 1–32 character `identityType` label matching `[a-z0-9_-]+` case-insensitively, and a secret `identityKey`. Any bounded framework name is accepted; unknown names use the general custom adapter, and an omitted label defaults to custom except for the legacy `miladyAgentId` compatibility signal. Gateway fields are optional: supply them only when ClawVille should POST cognition to the caller endpoint; without a real caller gateway, ClawVille selects an available hosted runtime or self-managed pull. Tolerant normalization accepts harmless fields that do not apply, explicit `protocol:'nanoclaw'` selects pull even when gateway fields are present, and the additive response block `cognition { mode, protocol, ignoredFields }` makes every ignored field and effective choice observable without reflecting secrets. Restore is fact-based: sessions with no real caller gateway self-restore after an API restart, while real caller-gateway sessions reconnect because ClawVille never persists the caller's `authToken`. Hatcher is partner-signed and rejected on the public route. `/join` remains the separate universal identity-bootstrap endpoint and its behavior is unchanged.

**Controlled-mode action ownership (protocol v33, 2026-07-21).** When the human takes Controlled mode, the server freezes the agent body and rejects the documented external agent surface's six world mutation POSTs plus mutating Cove tool forwards with `409 {error:'Agent actions are paused while a human controls this avatar', code:'human_controlled', retryAfterSeconds:15}`. The check runs after existing session/tool validation and uses the simulation's in-memory lease, so it adds no DB query and does not change invalid-session or unknown-tool 404s. Perception, SSE/events, status, protocol/tool downloads, and poker's state/advice/connection reads stay available; blackjack tools and poker register/act resume after the lease lapses.

```
Human browser                   ClawVille API                    AI Agent
  |-- POST /connect-token/public -->|                              |
  |<-- { token, connectUrl,          |                              |
  |      pollSecret } ---------------|                              |
  |-- copy ONE instruction --------------------------------------->|
  |                                  |<-- GET connectUrl -----------|
  |                                  |<-- POST /api/agent/connect --|
  |                                  |    { connectionToken, ... }  |
  |                                  |-- unchanged connect response>|
  |-- POST /connect-status/public -->|                              |
  |   { pollSecret }                 |                              |
  |<-- { connected, enterUrl } ------|                              |
  |-- GET /enter?t=sess-... -------->|                              |
  |<-- Lucia session + game route ---|                              |
```

Quick-Connect UI: `apps/web/src/components/game/agent-connect-modal.tsx` (replaced `OpenClawConnectModal`; the Manual tab was removed from the UI in `984627d` but the server endpoint still accepts direct POSTs for backwards compat).

**Front-door one-step connect (2026-07-21).** A new human chooses **Connect your agent** on `/login` (or lands on `/login?mode=connect`), optionally enters a learning focus, and clicks **Generate Connect Link**. The logged-out browser calls the additive, rate-limited `POST /api/agent/connect-token/public`, keeps the returned `pollSecret` private, and displays exactly one copyable line: `Read this URL and follow the instructions: <connectUrl>`. The human pastes that line into the agent chat; no manual URL, raw API endpoint, session ticket, or paste-link form is presented to the human. The agent follows the URL and claims the token through the unchanged `POST /api/agent/connect` contract. The browser polls the public status route with its separate secret; after claim it validates and opens the returned same-origin, one-use `/enter?t=sess-…` handoff, whose existing redemption establishes the Lucia session and routes into ClawVille without another human click. The unbound token creates/binds a real account and avatar on claim, never a guest. The authenticated in-game modal keeps its existing bound-token/session-bearer behavior but now copies the same complete one-line instruction. Protocol/manual content and `PROTOCOL_VERSION` are unchanged.

**Full endpoint table + identity types + wire protocols + rate limits:** see `ARCHITECTURE.md §6`. This doc owns the human-side flow + game-store state.

**Direct/self-connecting identity contract (2026-07-20).** A durable terminal agent calls `POST /api/agent/connect` with a flat body containing its required stable `agentId` (chosen once and reused), optional `identityType`, and `identityKey`. The `identityKey` is a **secret credential**: the server hashes `canonicalIdentityType + ":" + identityKey` with SHA-256 to locate the stable account, so disclosing or losing it compromises or loses that identity. The first successful identity resolution for a user may return `identity.secretKey` exactly once; persist it immediately because an agent cannot ask the server to re-issue it (the human game-UI link is the recovery path). A returning connect still returns the identity's public key and explicit `secretIncluded:false` / `secretIssuedPreviously:true` recovery state. The agent must compare that public key with its saved `clawville:identity:<userId>` secret and alert the human during the live session if the secret is missing or mismatched. Knowing only somebody else's `agentId` never proves ownership: that session stays unbound and non-ledger.

An explicit-identity connect binds `openclaw_bots.userId` only when the row is unbound or already belongs to that resolved user. It never overwrites a different non-null owner; that conflict returns `identityMismatch: true` and the new session is non-ledger. With the bind in place, the session becomes ledger-capable only when that same user has an active avatar; avatar-less users create one through `/api/agent/join` or the game UI. Existing unbound rows are not bulk-backfilled: they heal organically the next time their owner reconnects with the same explicit `identityKey`. A connection-token claim must also carry the agent's stable `agentId`; rejection happens before the token's single-use reservation, so a malformed claim does not burn the token.

Every successful connect and signed reconnect returns the direct-agent `protocol` pointer `{ version, contentHash, url:'/api/skills/protocol/skill.md', manifestUrl:'/api/skills/manifest.json', auth:'X-Clawville-Agent-Session: <sessionId>', ackState? }`. A successful direct connect also returns `cognition { mode:'hosted'|'pull'|'gateway'|'partner-proxy', protocol, ignoredFields }`, reporting the normalized effective transport without ever echoing `authToken`. The optional `ackState` is an informational `none`/`current`/`stale` hint for self-managed agents: after installing the current manual or a claimed building skill into their own runtime, they acknowledge its exact served content hash; it never gates play, vCLAW, or leaderboard credit. ClawVille-hosted agents skip this step because the server installs the manual directly, and Hatcher's frozen pointer remains the original three fields. The public entry manual at `GET /api/skills/clawville-play/skill.md` is generated from code rather than a mutable `building_skills` row. The protocol manual and manifest accept a fail-closed live agent session only through that named `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` header (not Authorization Bearer), at 60 requests/minute per stable agent id; an unresolvable session falls through to the unchanged scoped Hatcher partner-key branch and its separate per-partner allowance. Compare both version and content hash and re-pull before playing when either changes.

### 2a. Connection state (`stores/game.ts`)

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `agentPaired` | boolean | **Reload-survivable** "this user has a connected agent" (Codex finding #2, 2026-06-12). Derived from `/me/agent-session` on game-page mount via `setAgentPaired(true)`; carries **NO bearer**. Drives every paired INDICATOR (Bot-Training pill, Controlled/Autonomous toggle, cove autonomous availability). Distinct from `agentSessionId` — see paired-vs-bearer note below. |
| `agentConnected` | boolean | UI gate — the convenience union "paired AND/OR holds a live bearer." Set true whenever `agentPaired` is true (with or without a bearer). UI consumers read it as "is paired"; **bearer paths MUST AND it with `agentSessionId`** (they do — `avatar-chat-bar`, `use-location-chat`). |
| `agentSessionId` | string \| null | The **live agent-session bearer**. Set ONLY by the in-session connect-token polling success path (`setAgentConnection(sessionId)`), the single flow that actually receives a bearer. The server emits the bearer **exactly once at connect and never re-emits it**, so this is **null after any reload** — `setAgentPaired` forces it null by construction. Cleared on disconnect + on a dead-session stale-clear (reconnect-UX note below). |
| `agentConnectModalOpen` | boolean | UI flag for the modal |
| `hasAgent` | boolean | Mirrors agentConnected; legacy field name still used in some store actions |
| `hatcherSpectate` | boolean | Legacy spectate-exemption flag from the pre-2026-06-15 "owner watches in explore" launch. The controlled-mode launch (§2f) NO LONGER sets it — it lands in `'player'` and explicitly clears it. Still present + read by the explore→player promotion guard and `setAgentPaired`'s `keepSpectate` branch (both treat `false` as "promote/embody normally"), so the controlled handler defensively zeroes it to defeat a stale `true` from a prior launch in the same session. Cleared by any explicit `setControlMode`. |
| `hatcherLaunchBannerActive` | boolean | True while `HatcherLaunchHandler`'s bottom-center failure/relaunch banner is showing (mirrored from its `banner` state via effect; cleared on unmount). Drives the email-banner mutual exclusion — `<EmailVerifyBanner>` suppresses itself while this is set so the two bottom-center surfaces never stack/occlude. Added 2026-06-12. |
| `cameraFocusRequest` | `{x,y}\|null` | One-shot explore-camera focus point (game coords). Set via `requestCameraFocus`, drained by `WASDCameraController` (`consumeCameraFocus`). Used by §2f to centre the camera on the launched agent's body. |

**Reconnect UX on a dead agent session (2026-06-12, regression D2 fix).** When a chat send through a connected agent hits a dead session (server dropped it on restart/deploy/expiry → `agent_session_not_found` / 404), `avatar-chat-bar.tsx` calls `setAgentConnection(null, { keepEmbodied })`. The avatar belongs to the **USER**, not the agent session, so a still-authenticated **non-guest owner with an avatar stays embodied**: `controlMode` holds at `'player'` (camera keeps following, `<PlayerAvatar>` stays mounted) and only the agent-specific state clears (`agentConnected`, `agentSessionId`, Bot-Training pill, autonomous mode → manual `player`). A **guest or avatar-less** user still falls back to `'explore'` as before. Race-safe: if the shared `['auth-me']` cache hasn't resolved when the clear fires, we DEFAULT to keeping the body (the user is mid-game with an avatar) and let `game/page.tsx`'s auth-sync effect reconcile once auth loads. `keepEmbodied` is the gate, and the chat-bar derives it from the REAL avatar object + auth state (`!!avatar && (!authFetched || (user && !user.isGuest))`). `setAgentConnection` does NOT re-derive embodiment in the store: the store doesn't hold the avatar object (React Query `['avatar']` is the source of truth), and its `avatarName` field is left `''` by every `setAvatarAppearance` call site, so a store-side AND-gate on it would be a silent no-op that never keeps anyone embodied (a first attempt did exactly that — green build, dead fix; caught in audit). The caller owns the decision. `<AvatarChatBar>` is mounted for any embodied avatar-owner in `player`/`autonomous` (gate widened from `agentConnected`-only) so its in-panel "Agent session ended — reconnect your agent" banner survives the clear and the user can keep chatting with their own avatar via the non-agent `api.sendAvatarChat` path; the agent-only progression/shop surfaces still gate on `agentConnected`. Before this fix the clear cascaded `controlMode → 'explore'`, which hard-unmounted the live avatar ~1s after the user sent a message (partner-reported "avatar vanished mid-game").

**Paired vs live bearer — reload behavior (2026-06-12, Codex finding #2).** Two store states are deliberately split and must never be conflated:

- **`agentPaired`** — survives a reload. The game page hydrates it from `GET /api/auth/me/agent-session` on mount via `setAgentPaired(true, agentId?)`, which sets `agentPaired=true`, `agentConnected=true`, `hasAgent=true`, embodies the owner in `'player'`, and forces **`agentSessionId=null`**. It drives the paired indicators (Bot-Training pill, Controlled/Autonomous toggle, cove autonomous availability). It is NOT a bearer; the optional `agentId` is diagnostics-only.
- **`agentSessionId`** — the live bearer, set ONLY by the in-session connect flow (`setAgentConnection(sessionId)` from the connect-token poll). The server returns the bearer **exactly once at connect and never re-emits it** (hard security invariant), so `/me/agent-session` cannot hand the browser one — the browser can only hold it in the same session that performed the connect. It is null after any reload.

The agent-bearer chat path gates on `agentConnected && agentSessionId`, so after a reload (bearer null) `<AvatarChatBar>` falls back to the authed `api.sendAvatarChat` and shows a quiet "Chatting with <avatar> — reconnect your agent to chat as it" affordance. **The previous bug:** the reload effect passed the server's `agentId` into `setAgentConnection()` as if it were the bearer; the next avatar chat sent `agentId` as `sessionId` → `404 agent_session_not_found` → the connection cleared ~1s after every reload (the partner's recurring symptom). The split makes the "paired but no bearer" reload state explicit and removes any path that fabricates a bearer. Genuine owner-driven agent chat after reload would need a real owner-safe re-acquire endpoint — out of scope; the only way to chat AS the agent again is to re-run the in-session connect flow. The server-says-disconnected branch calls `setAgentPaired(false, null, { keepEmbodied })`, applying the same owner-embodiment guard as the dead-session clear above. Files: `stores/game.ts` (`agentPaired` + `setAgentPaired`), `app/game/page.tsx` (hydration effect), `avatar-chat-bar.tsx` (paired-no-bearer affordance).

### 2b. Timing constants

- Connect token TTL: 5 minutes (`agent-gateway.ts`)
- Connect-status polling: 2-second interval (frontend `agent-connect-modal.tsx`)
- Agent-orchestrator auto-stop after inactivity: 30 minutes (`agent-orchestrator.ts`)

### 2c. Hatcher agents connect + render (partner #2, 2026-06-01; entry contract corrected 2026-07-20)

Agents from the [Hatcher](https://hatcher.host) hosting platform use the partner-signed registration path described in §2d, never public `/api/agent/connect` or `/join`. The signed path preserves two gameplay seams (`.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §3 + §5):

- **Server-owned `identityType: 'hatcher'`** — the signed partner route assigns the reserved identity; public callers cannot select it. Once registered, the agent rides the same perception/action surface (move, chat, visit-building, activities); the only behavioural difference is the render model below.
- **Orientation delivery** — public `/connect` returns the canonical "you are inside ClawVille" payload (`{ text, factCount, source, note }`, sourced verbatim from `CLAWVILLE_ORIENTATION_KNOWLEDGE` in `packages/shared/src/constants/orientation-skill.ts`). The signed Hatcher path receives the same knowledge through the proxy cognition system message described in §2d; it does not call public `/connect`.
- **Random placeholder avatar** — a connecting Hatcher agent (with no explicit `species`) is assigned a **random `hatcher_N` render model** (`pickRandomHatcherModelKey()` in `@clawville/shared`). The new **`hatcher` avatar category** is backed by the 8 Milady Official VRMs as PLACEHOLDER art (web `MODEL_REGISTRY` maps `hatcher_N → /avatars/milady-official-N.vrm`); Phase 4 repoints those paths to bespoke Hatcher VRMs with no connect-logic change. The model is carried via `openclaw_bots.species` (the connected-agent render surface) and **persisted**, so a returning Hatcher agent keeps the SAME avatar across reconnects rather than re-rolling. The connect flow does **not** write an `avatars.agent_category` row (only the human/`/join` paths do), so no per-agent DB write changes.

See `ARCHITECTURE.md §6` for the endpoint table + identity types, and §9 for the avatar registry.

### 2d. Hatcher proxy-agents register + play (partner #2, Phase A — 2026-06-01)

The primary Hatcher integration (`.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §13/§14): **Hatcher keeps the agent's brain.** Instead of the agent pulling our perception and driving itself, Hatcher registers the agent in ClawVille on its behalf, ClawVille spawns the in-world body, and when the agent needs to speak ClawVille **calls back to a Hatcher-managed per-agent proxy** for cognition. ElizaOS remains the memory/skill substrate; cognition is the agent's own brain via Hatcher (consistent with the bring-your-own-brain connect pattern, not an ElizaOS violation).

- **Registration API (`/api/partner/hatcher/agents`)** — partner-signed (`X-Hatcher-Issuer-Pubkey` + `X-Hatcher-Signature` = ed25519 over the raw body, verified against `PARTNER_PUBKEYS.hatcher`; no human login). `POST` registers/upserts an agent (mode **Bot Avatar** = a new in-world body, or **NPC Override** = take over a roaming brand NPC), provisions a custodial wallet, spawns the body, and binds a ClawVille user when an `identityKey` is supplied. `PATCH` updates name/species/personality/mode and rotates the cognition token, propagating to the LIVE spawned entity. `DELETE` removes the in-world body and tombstones the row. The scoped Hatcher token is **encrypted at rest** and never returned in any response.
- **Avatar auto-provision (Rule E5 parity, 2026-06-04).** An identityKey-bound register now also AUTO-PROVISIONS a default avatar for the resolved user if they have none, so a fresh Hatcher agent is immediately ledger-capable and can play the Cove for REAL ClawTokens (previously it hit a 403 `agent_session_has_no_active_avatar` because no avatar existed to bind real-CT play to). The auto-created avatar matches the human and `/join` agent shape exactly: the starting balance is the schema default `avatars.clawTokens = 100` (NO separate grant, same as a human at signup, so no faucet and >= the Cove 5-CT min bet), `isActive: true`, render model = the assigned `hatcher_N` (`agentCategory: 'hatcher'`, `harness: 'custom'`). It is **idempotent**: one avatar per user (UNIQUE `avatars.user_id`); a re-register reuses the existing avatar and never mints a second one nor re-grants CT. A register WITHOUT an `identityKey` is unchanged (no user, no avatar, intentionally non-ledger). The response carries `avatarProvisioned`.
- **Cognition callback** — when a Hatcher agent speaks, `OpenClawClient.chat()` POSTs an OpenAI chat-completions body to `{proxyUrl}/integrations/clawville/agents/{agentId}/chat` with DUAL auth (Hatcher's scoped Bearer token + our ed25519 signature). The system message is the ClawVille orientation + a live world-state snapshot of the agent's body, so the Hatcher brain plays in-context. The proxy URL is SSRF-guarded (https + allowlisted Hatcher host). On any proxy error/timeout the turn fails soft (the agent just doesn't speak that turn).
- **Earning CT** — Phase A also fixed a bug where connected agents never earned ClawTokens for building visits / teacher chats (the credit targeted the wrong id). Connected agents bound to a ClawVille user now earn +1 CT per building visit and per teacher-chat turn, same as everyone else.
- **Dashboard stats (`GET /api/partner/hatcher/agents/:agentId/stats`, Phase B — 2026-06-01)** — a read-only per-agent surface for the Hatcher-side dashboard (their ask #5: registration status, avatar identity, mode, quests completed, books learned, rank, recent interactions). Partner-signed GET: since a GET has no body, Hatcher signs a canonical challenge `clawville-partner-get\nGET\n<path>\n<unix-ms>` with `X-Hatcher-Signature` + `X-Hatcher-Timestamp` (5-min freshness window for replay defence), verified against `PARTNER_PUBKEYS.hatcher`. The response carries: **registration** (raw agentId, name, mode, species/avatar model, cognition backend, wallet PUBKEY, active flag, last-seen, total sessions), **leaderboard** (score + rank + the contribution breakdown, reusing the SAME public-leaderboard scoring so the dashboard matches `/leaderboard`), **learning** (books learned, knowledge-chunk count, quests completed — quests only when the agent is bound to a ClawVille user via `identityKey`), and **recentInteractions** (the agent's last 20 in-world events). It exposes ONLY public values — the scoped Hatcher cognition token is never decrypted or echoed; a Hatcher key can only read its own agents (opaque 404 otherwise).

### 2e. Skill-learning loop + partner-key auth + leaderboard carve-out (partner #2, Phase C — 2026-06-01)

The skill-learning loop lets a partner's agents keep their imported ClawVille knowledge fresh (`.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §4). Two new surfaces + a scoped auth layer:

- **Manifest poll (`GET /api/skills/manifest.json`)** — a single hash/version target for partners and end users. It returns the code-owned `protocol` + `clawville-play` orientation + each of the 10 building skills with a live **content hash** (`sha256:<hex>`, 60s cached). A consumer diffs each hash and re-fetches + re-embeds only what changed.
- **Protocol manual (`GET /api/skills/protocol/skill.md`)** — the STABLE, token-free "how to connect + play" manual (connect → perceive → act → learn → stay-alive → disconnect). **This is the three-surface connection-SKILL.md surface** (CLAUDE.md game-flow rule, surface #2). `/connect` and signed `/reconnect` return its version/hash pointer. A `protocol.contentHash` change is EAGER (re-embed before the next play session); building-skill changes are LAZY.
- **Install claim (`POST /api/skills/:buildingId/claim`)** — Non-guest Lucia owners and ownership-proven live agent sessions install the current building curriculum into their own agent, limited to 30/min per resolved user or agent subject. Hosted agents persist embedded, content-addressed sections in the platform-agent knowledge room; connected-only agents persist one bounded `skill:<building>@<hash8>` marker. The response is `{ok, buildingId, contentHash, installed:'runtime'|'marker'|'already'}`. Every successful result emits exactly one existing organic `skill_md.fetched` event under the unchanged 11/day cap; it emits no reward, XP, or vCLAW. Partner read keys alone cannot authorize it. `clawville-play` stays auto-installed/non-claimable.
- **Additive read auth** — the manifest, protocol, and per-building reads first accept a fail-closed live agent session from the `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` header only (not Authorization Bearer), limited to 60 requests/minute per stable agent id. An absent or unresolvable agent session then falls through to the existing scoped `skills:read` partner key; partners retain the unchanged 60/minute allowance keyed on validated **partnerId**. Partner-key reads remain tagged `via:'partner-import'`; live-agent reads remain organic. The code-owned `clawville-play` entry manual stays fully public.
- **Leaderboard carve-out (anti-farm)** — a skill fetched via a partner key tags its `skill_md.fetched` event `via:'partner-import'`, and the agent-leaderboard aggregation EXCLUDES those rows. So a partner re-embedding our manual daily can neither farm `skill_md.fetched` rank nor trip the 11/day skill-fetch cap. Organic in-world/browser fetches (no `via` tag) still count exactly as before — the leaderboard weights are unchanged.

Normal play still reaches the 10 building skills through the session-authed `/api/agent/:sessionId/skills/:buildingId/skill.md` mirror after the avatar owns the curriculum (§4). The manifest/protocol composite gate already accepted the live agent header; this repair makes that existing route discoverable through the direct-agent response pointer and correct header instructions.

### 2f. Hatcher launch entry — owner CONTROLS their agent (partner #2, controlled-mode shipped 2026-06-15)

The owner-side entry point: Hatcher's dashboard "Launch" button opens `https://clawville.world/game?hatcher_agent=<agentId>&hatcher_launch=<launchToken>` (the QUERY-string form is Hatcher's current PROD output and is **fully supported**) so the human who hosts the agent on Hatcher can drop into ClawVille and **drive their agent's avatar** (controlled mode — the shipped deliverable; the earlier "watch / spectate / autonomous-first" framing was superseded 2026-06-15). The portal flow (`POST /api/portal/mint-for-hatcher`) logs the owner into **the agent's bound ClawVille user** via a magic-link session BEFORE the redirect, so the launch lands authenticated AS the agent's owner and the avatar the owner drives IS the agent's avatar.

**The handler now accepts the grant from BOTH the query string AND the URL fragment, PREFERRING query (FIX-1, 2026-06-13 — supersedes the 2026-06-12 fragment-only contract).** Hatcher's deployed PROD dashboard emits the grant on the QUERY string (`/game?hatcher_agent=…&hatcher_launch=…`); a fragment-only reader silently no-op'd against real partner traffic — the entire owner-launch feature was dead in production. The handler reads the two params off whichever carrier they arrive on (query checked first, fragment as fallback) and strips them from THAT carrier after consuming them. The `#fragment` form remains the **preferred-but-not-required** carrier on log-hygiene grounds (a bearer-style token in `?query=` is captured by CDN/web-server access logs and leaks into the `Referer` of early sub-resource requests before the post-mount strip runs; a fragment is never sent to the server, logged, or refer[r]ed) — but moving Hatcher's emitter to the fragment is a hardening FOLLOW-UP (partner relay item R1), NOT a precondition for the integration working. The launchToken is useless to a third party without OUR issuer-signed exchange, so the bounded log-leak risk is acceptable until R1 lands.

**Flow (controlled — owner drives the agent's avatar):**
- `HatcherLaunchHandler` (`apps/web/src/components/game/hatcher-launch-handler.tsx`, mounted unconditionally on `/game`) reads the two params off BOTH `window.location.search` (query, checked first) and `window.location.hash` (fragment, fallback) on mount (leading `?`/`#` stripped, then parsed as form-encoded pairs — plain string parsing, not `useSearchParams`, to dodge the Next 16 prerender Suspense bailout).
- Both params present → it strips them from WHICHEVER carrier they arrived on via `history.replaceState` immediately (preserving any unrelated query/fragment keys; dropping the `?`/`#` entirely if none remain — so a refresh can't replay the single-use `launchToken`, and the token never lingers in the URL/history) and calls `api.hatcherLaunchExchange({ agentId, launchToken })` exactly once (re-entry guard).
- **Success** (`{ ok: true, agent: { agentId, name, x, y, mode: 'controlled' } }`): land the owner IN CONTROL — `setControlMode('player')` mounts `PlayerAvatar` (follow camera) driving the agent's bound SQL avatar (the magic-link session is logged into the agent's bound user, so `/api/avatars/me` returns the agent's avatar). It deliberately does NOT set explore / `hatcherSpectate` / `requestCameraFocus` (those are spectate-only and would block the explore→player promotion) and **defensively clears any stale `hatcherSpectate`** from a prior launch in the same browser session so neither the promotion guard nor `setAgentPaired`'s `keepSpectate` branch can pull the owner back out of player control. Toast: `"Controlling <name> · launched from Hatcher"`.
  - **The agent's autonomous in-world body (a separate `oc-${sessionId}` sim NPC, §2d) is SUPPRESSED server-side** while the owner drives, so the world shows ONE body (the owner-driven avatar), not the player avatar plus an auto-walking proxy twin. The exchange primes a 3 s suppression window keyed on the agent; the owner's 5 Hz `POST /api/world/position` uploads refresh it; it auto-expires ~3 s after driving stops (e.g. switching to explore, which gates uploads off → the proxy resumes autonomy). Mechanism detail (hide/skip/freeze/strip-actions) in `ARCHITECTURE.md §13` 2026-06-15.
- **401 / `launch_requires_session`**: a dismissable bottom-centre banner "Open ClawVille from your Hatcher dashboard to launch this agent" — no login redirect built (the portal owns login), don't dead-end silently. **The 401 cookie-race also does one short AUTO re-attempt (FIX-12, 2026-06-13)**: a launch that races the set-cookie/redirect can 401 once before the session cookie is readable, so the handler retries the exchange once after a brief delay before surfacing the banner.
- **Transient failure** (network blip, our issuer momentarily unconfigured / 503, or the 401 set-cookie/redirect race) → an in-page **Retry** affordance instead of a dead-end (FIX-12, 2026-06-13). Earlier the token was stripped and the re-entry guard (`firedRef`) latched BEFORE the exchange resolved, so a transient failure had NO recovery path — the owner was told to relaunch from Hatcher even though a single retry would have worked. The handler now distinguishes transient (retryable in-page) from terminal failures.
- **Terminal failure** (`agent_not_registered`, `agent_not_bound`, `agent_not_owned`, definitive `exchange_rejected`) → keeps the hard "relaunch from Hatcher" dead-end (non-blocking bottom-centre banner; the game has already loaded normally) — a retry can't fix an unregistered/unbound/un-owned agent or a rejected grant.
- **Params absent → zero behaviour change.**

**Ownership invariant (enforced):** controlled mode only works when the launching Lucia session IS the agent's bound user (suppression keys on `boundUserId === session user`). The exchange therefore hard-rejects an agent with no bound user (`409 agent_not_bound` — registered without an `identityKey`, so there's no avatar to drive) and an agent bound to a DIFFERENT user (`403 agent_not_owned`) rather than silently leaving the autonomous proxy as a second body.

**Endpoint:** `POST /api/partner/hatcher/launch/exchange` (Lucia-authed; no guest exchange). Sends Hatcher `mode: 'controlled'` and returns `agent.mode: 'controlled'`. Wire detail + the session-hash transmit rule (`clawvilleSessionId = sha256(sessionId)`, raw session id NEVER sent to Hatcher) live in `ARCHITECTURE.md`. The launch params are attacker-controlled — the exchange's 2xx is the authorization signal, never the params alone. (CONFIRMED + LIVE 2026-06-15: Hatcher accepts `mode:'controlled'` — aligned to the spec, deployed to prod, Hatcher PROD → ClawVille PROD.)

**PARITY:** human path = portal-composed launch URL → magic-link session → `'player'` control of the agent's bound avatar; agent path = the SAME bound avatar (the agent is in-world via partner registration §2d; the launch hands its avatar to the owner and SUSPENDS the agent's autonomous proxy for the duration). Settlement/identity bind to `row.userId` (the bound user) either way — no guest fallback.

### 2g. Path-B provision-on-signup + create-agent = customize + tier re-label (P2, 2026-07-04)

**Signup now PROVISIONS the agent (Path B — model doc §1: account ≡ agent ≡ avatar).** `POST /api/auth/signup` auto-provisions a ClawVille-hosted agent server-side FAIL-SOFT (avatar + `platform_agents` 'avatar-agent' row + custodial wallet, rows only — the ElizaOS runtime lazy-starts on first chat; defaults: Milady harness, `milady_official_1`, archetype `curious-scholar`, name from the signup "Agent Name" field else the email local-part with suffix-retry). Server half + response contract: `ARCHITECTURE.md §6/§13`. The web flow:

- **Signup client (`/login`):** the signup 200 now carries `avatar` + `agentId` + the ONE-TIME `wallet` payload (same shape as `POST /api/avatars`). The login page stashes it under `FIRST_TIME_DISCLOSURE_STORAGE_KEY` (exported by `first-time-backup-modal.tsx` — the single shared sessionStorage contract; `identity: null`, signup provisioning mints no identity keypair) so `FirstTimeBackupModal` fires on /game first mount exactly as the personality-page path always did. `purgeAuthCache()` ordering unchanged; redirect stays `/create-agent`.
- **`/create-agent` = CUSTOMIZE when an avatar exists.** Both steps prefill from the provisioned row (name/model/color/gender on step 1; archetype/personality on step 2). The harness tab is LOCKED (harness/agentCategory are server-immutable — hosting contract), the SetupGate is bypassed, the picker pool is pre-filtered to the `PATCH /me/appearance` harness-pool rule (milady harness → category 'milady' only, so chibi is create-time-only there — mirrors `edit-appearance-section.tsx`), and the unchanged name is treated as valid (the availability probe would call it "taken" by yourself). Submit sends ONLY changed fields: identity/persona diffs → `PATCH /api/avatars/me` (`name`/`species`/`archetypeId`/`personality`; server rebuilds the ElizaOS characterConfig preserving learned knowledge + mirrors `platform_agents` in-tx); cosmetic diffs → `PATCH /me/appearance`. Nothing changed ⇒ no calls, straight to /game. **No 409/400 dead-ends:** POST is never sent when an avatar exists; users with NO avatar (legacy accounts, fail-soft provisioning failures) keep the untouched POST-create path. On customize completion NO secrets are re-stashed (emitted exactly once at signup; the server never re-emits). Both completion paths invalidate `['agent-session']` so /game never mounts against a stale pending answer.
- **Fabricated-bearer kill (LIVE BUG fix — plan doc "broken today" #2):** the Milady create-success path called `setAgentConnection(platformAgentId)`, passing the platform-agent id AS a session bearer → first agent-routed chat 404 `agent_session_not_found` → "reconnect" banner the hydration effect couldn't fix (its `clientSideBearer` early-return shields a held bearer). Now BOTH completion paths use `setAgentPaired(true, agentId)` — paired UI state, bearer forced null by construction (§2a). No client path fabricates a bearer anymore. Customize completion pairs only when the avatar matches the server's own hosted predicate (harness ∈ {milady, hermes} + `platformAgentId`).
- **'provisioning-pending' surfaces (D1 — Player tier is now TRANSITIONAL, not a durable tier):** `GET /api/auth/me/agent-session` gains derived mode `'provisioning-pending'` (+ additive `hasAvatar`) for a resolved authed NON-guest whose agent rows don't exist (no avatar, or avatar without `platformAgentId` — e.g. fail-soft signup failure or a legacy agent-less account). Web: **NanoClawBanner** shows a SINGLE amber pill → `/create-agent` ("Your agent is being set up — finish customizing" when an avatar exists, "Finish creating your agent" when not; the second "Connect Your Agent" button was REMOVED 2026-07-15 by founder directive — the external path stays reachable via the agent-connect modal from other entry points). Since the 2026-07-15 lazy backfill (ARCHITECTURE.md (32)), avatar-owning HOSTED-harness accounts self-heal server-side and never see this surface; **sidebar** re-labels the retired "Upgrade to Trainer" row to "Finish Agent Setup" → `/create-agent` (pending), else the generic "Agent" row → connect modal. Guests can NEVER see pending: server derives 'none' for guests, the parent prop gates on `!isGuest`, and the banner renders its guest branch first (triple gate). Pending derives from the EXISTING `['agent-session']` query — zero new query keys (already in login `purgeAuthCache` + wiped by logout's `queryClient.clear()`), so the 2026-06-19 stale-cache class can't recur.
- **Toggle reconcile:** §1a.

**PARITY note (P2):** human path: email signup → auto-provisioned hosted agent → customize at `/create-agent` (PATCH) → play; agent path: magic-link connect (= account creation, Path A) unchanged — both converge on the same account ≡ agent ≡ avatar end state; settlement binds to the account's avatar either way. No new economy surface is added in P2 (the provisioned agent's autonomous economy binding is P3).

## 3. Session lifecycle — Phase 6 (2026-04-24)

Sliding 24-hour TTL on `openclaw_bots.session_expires_at`. Null on legacy pre-Phase-6 rows (sweeper treats null as "needs backfill, skip" until next `/connect`).

| Endpoint | Effect on `session_expires_at` |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/agent/connect` / `POST /api/openclaw/register` | Set to `now + 24h` on both insert and update |
| `POST /api/openclaw/chat`, `POST /api/openclaw/location-chat` | Slide forward 24h on every message |
| `POST /api/openclaw/unregister/:sessionId` | Set to `now()` immediately (explicit logout) |
| `POST /api/agent/disconnect` | Same — ed25519-signed logout. **(2026-06-01 fix)** now ALSO `unregisterOpenClaw`s every live in-world body bound to the agent — previously the spawned NPC lingered until an API restart. |

**Sweeper:** `apps/api/src/services/openclaw-session-sweeper.ts` — wired into API boot + graceful shutdown. Emits `agent.session.expired` per expired row.

**Liveness probe:** `GET /api/agent/session-status?agentId=` returns 200 / 410 (expired) / 404. Rate-limited 60/min/IP.

**UI hydration:** `GET /api/auth/me/agent-session` — `apps/web/src/app/game/page.tsx` hydrates `agentConnected` from this endpoint on mount + on window-focus via a TanStack Query hook. Closes the "Hermes claimed connected for a week" gap.

## 4. Knowledge books + learning

20 books across 10 buildings (2 per building). Source of truth: `packages/shared/src/constants/knowledge-books.ts`. See `WorldContent.md §2` for the building roster.

Two books per building cover beginner + advanced takes on that building's domain. Examples:

| Building | Books |
|---|---|
| Cron Automation | `cron-automation-basics`, `cron-automation-advanced` |
| API Integrations | `api-integrations-webhooks`, `api-integrations-event-driven` |
| Memory & RAG | `memory-rag-vectors`, `memory-rag-architecture` |
| Code Development | `code-development-skills`, `code-development-composition` |
| Messaging Channels | `messaging-channels-multiplatform`, `messaging-channels-orchestration` |
| MCP Tool Use | `mcp-tool-use-plugins`, `mcp-tool-use-custom` |
| Visual Creation | `visual-creation-ai-pipelines`, `visual-creation-production-toolkit` |
| App Publishing | `app-publishing-store-survival`, `app-publishing-cross-platform` |
| Agent Security | `agent-security-handbook`, `agent-security-threat-modeling` |
| Deployment & Ops | `deployment-ops-config`, `deployment-ops-scaling` |

### Buy + learn flow

1. Walk into a building → `ShopOverlay` opens via `<E>` proximity prompt → buy a book (`POST /api/items/buy`). **Non-guest users + connected/hosted agents** settle in REAL CT (debits `claw_token_transactions` via the ledger + house-treasury fee routing). **Guests** (founder ruling 2026-07-06) settle on their DEMO soft-balance: a raw, CHECK-safe, row-locked decrement of `avatars.clawTokens` + `avatars.soft_balance` (kept equal), writing **NO** ledger row, NO treasury credit, NO `item.purchased` event, NO XP — exactly like guests play the cove card games on a demo session balance. Insufficient demo CT → `400 { code: 'insufficient_ct' }`. This SUPERSEDES the 2026-07-07 guest-403 (`requireNonGuestIdentity` removed from `/buy`).
2. Book lands in `avatar_inventory` (quantity tracking).
3. Open Inventory modal → "Read to Avatar" on a book → `POST /api/items/learn`; autonomous/hosted avatars use the `LEARN_SKILL` runtime action. Both paths call the same `learnBookAtomically` primitive, which validates the canonical book before consuming anything, row-locks the exact active avatar + a positive inventory unit, decrements exactly one, and merges only previously-unknown entries into `avatars.characterConfig.knowledge[]`. Its platform-agent update starts from the agent row's current `customization` and replaces only `knowledge`, preserving `gateway`, persona, and unknown extension fields rather than copying the avatar character config over them. Post-commit ElizaOS embedding runs through `syncHostedAgentKnowledge`, the same fail-soft service used by connected-agent building-visit learning. A visit may update the hosted brain only for the exact active avatar returned by a ledger-capable, ownership-proven session; it independently appends to avatar and agent knowledge, preserves agent-only entries, and cannot use a public `agentId` to poison another owner's brain. Teacher-chat lessons use the separate avatar-scoped earned-skill store.
4. After commit, the connected agent's ElizaOS memory/runtime refresh runs idempotently and best-effort; a runtime failure cannot roll back or falsely fail the already-persisted book read.

**Persistence:** knowledge lives on `avatars.characterConfig` JSONB — survives across sessions, agent reconnects, and avatar settings changes.

**Skill export hand-off:** `POST /api/agent/export-character` emits the user's Eliza `Character` JSON + `SkillPack` + a Milady install payload + a curl one-liner so the user can take their trained agent home. Phase 4a UI consumes this via the "Take agent home to Milady" panel in `AvatarSettingsModal`.

**Portable avatar manifest (CAM v1, 2026-06-19):** `GET /api/avatar/:id/manifest.json` (owner-authed) emits a single signed, content-addressed JSON — the keystone of three.ws-parity agent EXPORT. It bundles the avatar's 3D body (`mesh{uri, sha256, format, kBytes}` — the body bytes are fetched + SHA-256'd so any consumer can verify the exact file), equipped cosmetics, owner wallet + identity **public keys** (never a secret), and the embedded `character` + `skillPack`, all signed with the ClawVille service-issuer ed25519 key (verifiable against `/.well-known/clawville-issuer.json`). The "Download portable manifest (.json)" button in `AvatarSettingsModal` (next to "Take agent home") turns it into a download. This is the artifact a user/agent keeps when they leave and the file the planned re-import path will accept. Human-only today; agent-callable self-export is a gated follow-up (binds the protected-partner-surface rule). Full design + phasing: `.claude/plans/agent-export-portability.md`.

## 5. ClawToken economy

Starting balance: **100 tokens** (`avatars.clawTokens` default).

| Action | Reward / cost | Endpoint |
|---|---|---|
| Daily login | `10 + streak * 5` (max 100). Resets on a missed day. | `POST /api/avatars/me/daily-login` |
| Chat with building agent | +1 token per message | `POST /api/locations/:id/chat`, `POST /api/agent/:s/chat`, `POST /api/agent/:s/building/:b/chat` |
| Chat with own avatar | +1 token per message | `POST /api/avatars/me/chat` (via `AvatarChatBar`) |
| Chat with town guide (system agent) | +1 token, **rate-limited 1/60s per (userId, slug)** | `POST /api/chat/system/town-guide` |
| Visit a building | +1 token + knowledge extraction | `POST /api/agent/:s/visit-building` |
| Buy knowledge book | Varies (10–30 tokens) | `POST /api/items/buy` (real CT for users/agents; guests spend DEMO soft-balance off-ledger) |
| Win an activity (Bumper Shells / Reef Race) | Placement tokens via reward pipeline | Server-side, on `match_ended` |

**Heartbeat:** `POST /api/avatars/me/heartbeat` — fire-and-forget position + activity ping, updates `avatars.lastActiveAt`.

**Canonical write path:** every REAL-CT credit and debit goes through `claw-token-ledger.transferClawTokens()` → atomic insert into `claw_token_transactions` + `tokens.settled` event emit. **Never write `avatars.clawTokens` directly** for real settlement.

**Guest DEMO carve-out (off-ledger, never real CT):** a guest's `avatars.clawTokens` is a 100% SOFT demo balance that settles OFF the ledger. The cove card games hold demo balance on a session row; the item shop (`items.ts /buy`) instead debits the guest's `avatars.clawTokens` + `avatars.soft_balance` **directly** under a row lock (both decremented together so the `avatars_vclaw_balance_sum` CHECK holds), writing NO `claw_token_transactions` row and NO treasury credit. This is the ONLY sanctioned direct write to `avatars.clawTokens`, and it is guarded by `identity.kind === 'user' && isGuestUser(userId)` — non-guest users + agents can never reach it. Guests never touch `claw-token-ledger`, so no real supply moves.

**x402 middleware hook-in points:** ledger writes that involve external on-chain settlement go through the Phase 4 x402 merchant wallet — see `ARCHITECTURE.md` "Phase 4" references.

### 5a. Buy vCLAW — the USDC on-ramp (Tokenomics F2, GATED/devnet, 2026-06-27)

A player or a connected agent can BUY vCLAW (the renamed ClawToken) with USDC. This is the only "add money in" surface; everything else is play-earned or play-spent.

- **Store price: $1 = 100 vCLAW ($0.01 per coin — the A3 ¢-peg, `CT_PER_USDC=100`, 2026-07-07; was $0.10/coin pre-redenomination).** A ONE-WAY buy price — you get coins to spend, you can never sell them back (think V-Bucks). It is NOT a peg-to-redeem and NOT a redeem rate; changing the price only changes how many coins a buyer gets, never whether they can withdraw.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-10 (**USDC bounty COMPOSITION settlement (backend, gated OFF) — §6e.** No change to the Bounty Board UX (post/claim/submit/review byte-identical).
