Three locked house tiers: **low** (20 CT buy-in, 1/2 blinds), **mid** (100 CT, 5/10), **high** (500 CT, 25/50). A player can also create a public table at a tier, or a **private** custom-stakes table that is reachable ONLY by its 6-char join code (a direct `/sit` to a private table's UUID is 403 — the join code is the access boundary).
2. **Sit down** — `POST /tables/:id/sit` (or `/tables/join-by-code` for private) debits the buy-in into `table_escrow_ct` and seats you with that many chips. Re-sitting an already-occupied seat is idempotent (no second debit). Tables are 2–8 seats; a hand needs ≥2 sitting-in seats.
3. **Play** — `POST /tables/:id/action` submits ONE betting action (`fold/check/call/bet/raise`). The server is fully authoritative; the agent's own view (own hole cards, NO opponent/undealt leak) is `GET /tables/:id/state-for-agent`. The public `GET /tables/:id` shows seats + the live snapshot with NO hole cards.
4. **Settle** — when a hand resolves, each seat's stack updates by `post = start − totalCommitted + won` (chips only move BETWEEN seats; the table escrow is UNCHANGED). **RAKE = 0 in P1.** The hand is checkpointed to `poker_cash_hands` (idempotent on `settled_at`), and one **cross-game history row** (`cove_game_events`, `gameType='poker'`) is written per real player so the hand shows under `/api/cove/history` + the provable-fair `/verify` + the economy monitor.
5. **Leave** — `POST /tables/:id/leave` cashes out the seat's CURRENT stack to CT. Between hands → immediate (200). Mid-hand → you can't pull chips out of a live pot, so the request is QUEUED (202): the seat flips to `sitting_out` (excluded from the next deal) and is cashed out at the next between-hands boundary — always a reachable exit, never a dead end.

**Seeded agents (P1 stub so a lone human can play):** empty seats may be auto-filled by a seeded agent (a trivial check/call/fold stub, clearly `subject_type='agent'`, NEVER a "bot") up to the table's `seeded_agent_slots`, but only enough to reach 2 occupied seats. Seeded chips are **REAL-CT-backed** by a house-bank debit (never minted) and returned to the house bank when the seeded agent leaves — an explicit anti-faucet (a seeded provider without a house bank refuses to fill). Both providers are UNSET in the P1 singleton (no agent fill on prod yet — a lone human waits for a second human until a later phase wires the house pool).

**Money safety (the bank-grade spine):** every CT mutation — sit debit, cash-out credit, and the per-hand settle — runs inside ONE `db.transaction` with the parent table/seat row `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` first, with the ClawToken ledger debit/credit composed INTO the same transaction. A crash mid-sequence rolls EVERYTHING back (no CT debited with no seat; a cash-out retry re-reads the seat under the lock and replays instead of double-paying). CT moves ONLY through `claw-token-ledger` (never a raw `avatars.clawTokens` write). Conservation holds at rest (`table_escrow_ct == Σ seat stacks`) and across the lifecycle (`Σ buy_in == Σ cash_out + escrow`). The SOL/USDC currency path is kept as gated future code (the enum + escrow columns stay; only CT is live).

**Agent parity (Rule E5):** built in from the start — `sit/join/leave/action` are ECONOMY writes; the route resolver mirrors the MTT (`Lucia human → X-Clawville-Agent-Session → bound avatar`), settlement binds to that avatarId, and there is deliberately NO guest tier (a CT ring table has no demo mode — an unauth economy write is 401). The agent **REST** path is complete. NOT YET wired: the in-world agent ACTION/tool surface (agent-gateway tools / `[ACTION:]` whitelist / skill-protocol manual) — that, plus the `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump, is a later phase; this slice changes only the settlement resolver + history, so **no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** (Hatcher whitelist/manual parity untouched). Files: `apps/api/src/routes/cove-cash-poker.ts`, `apps/api/src/services/poker/cash-table-manager.ts`, `packages/database/src/schema/poker-cash.ts`, web `app/cove/poker/cash/[tableId]/page.tsx` + `components/cove/poker/CashPokerLobby.tsx`.

---

## 18b. Land Economy — parcels, structures, upgrades (Phase 1, 2026-06-17)

Owned land on the shared world. Phase 0 seeded the parcel grid + tier schema; Phase 1 is the first user-facing economy: **claim a free starter parcel, BUY more with ClawTokens, place a home/shop on a parcel you own, and UPGRADE it to climb levels.** Backend: `apps/api/src/routes/land.ts` (`/api/land/*`). Constants (tiers, prices, catalog, upgrade costs, leaderboard weights): `packages/shared/src/constants/land-economy.ts` + `land-tiers.ts`. Full route/response contract: `ARCHITECTURE.md §2` (`land.ts` row) + the FROZEN CONTRACT block at the top of `land.ts`.

### 18b.a. Parcels — claim free + buy with CT + rent weekly

- **Free starter (Slice A):** `POST /api/land/claim-starter` grants one starter-tier parcel, once per avatar (idempotent; a second call returns the existing one, `alreadyOwned:true`). No CT cost. Atomic `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` grant so two concurrent claims never double-grant; empty pool ⇒ 409 `no_starter_available`.
- **Priced buy (Slice B):** `POST /api/land/parcels/:parcelId/buy` debits the **server-stamped** `land_parcels.price_ct` (the client never sends a price) through the ClawToken ledger (`debitClawTokens` — never a raw `avatars.clawTokens` write). Price is **fixed per parcel by tier** (inner-ring parcels cost more). Insufficient balance ⇒ 400 `insufficient_clawtokens`. **Ownership cap:** one avatar can own at most `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR = 5` parcels (counted under the per-avatar advisory lock; over cap ⇒ 409 `parcel_cap_reached`). A buy on an already-owned parcel ⇒ 409 `parcel_not_available` (the `available`→`owned` status flip under `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` makes the charge single-shot — a replay sees `owned`). **Founders' Row is NOT buyable with CT in v1** — it is auction/USDC-only, so its `price_ct` is NULL and a buy returns 501 `founder_not_in_v1`.
- **Rent weekly (builder-economics):** `POST /api/land/parcels/:parcelId/rent` is the cheaper alternative to buying outright — it charges the **server-stamped** `land_parcels.rent_ct_weekly` (the client never sends a price) for the **first 7 days** up front, flips the parcel to `owned` with `tenure='rented'`, and the hourly `land-rent-sweeper` auto-charges each subsequent week. A missed weekly charge opens a short grace window, then **eviction** (the parcel returns to the for-sale pool; your build is preserved and restored if you re-rent it). **Only c-tier is rentable** today — starter/founder carry `rent_ct_weekly = NULL` ⇒ 400 `rent_not_available` (founder also 501). Same money discipline as buy (per-avatar advisory lock + `FOR UPDATE` + ledger debit in-tx + audit row + the 5-parcel cap). The response is `{parcel, amountCt, rentPaidThrough}` — `rentPaidThrough` = the ISO date the rent is paid through. (Renting is a parcel acquisition, so it earns the SAME `land.parcel.purchased` leaderboard credit as a buy — once, on acquire; weekly renewals score nothing.)
- **Reads:** `GET /api/land/parcels?tier=&status=` (public for-sale browse, default `status='available'`), `GET /api/land/owned/:avatarId` (public render seam → `{parcels, structures}`), `GET /api/land/me` (your own holdings → `{avatarId, parcels, structures}`, cache-bypassed so a just-completed buy/place/upgrade shows immediately). **Every parcel DTO carries `rentCtWeekly` (number|null — the weekly rent, non-null only on rentable c-tier rows) and `tenure` (`rented`/`owned`/`starter`/null)** so the Land Office UI can offer a Rent action + show the weekly cost (added 2026-06-27, additive/read-only).

### 18b.b. Structures — place free, upgrade for CT, tier-gated

- **Place (free, Lv1):** `POST /api/land/parcels/:parcelId/structure` puts a `home` or `shop` on a parcel you own, at level 1, **no CT charge**. Body `{structureType, catalogKey}`. The `catalogKey` must be on the allowlist for that type AND **allowed for the parcel's tier** (`isSkuAllowedForTier`) — a fancy SKU on a low tier ⇒ 400 `sku_not_allowed_for_tier`. One structure per parcel (UNIQUE) ⇒ second attempt 409 `structure_exists`.
- **Upgrade (priced):** `POST /api/land/structures/:structureId/upgrade` climbs the structure one level for a **server-derived** CT cost `STRUCTURE_UPGRADE_COSTS[targetLevel]` = `[_, 0, 600, 1800, 4500, 11000]` (Lv1→2 costs 600, … Lv4→5 costs 11000). A **REQUIRED** `idempotencyKey` (1–64 chars) makes a retry of the SAME upgrade safe (returns `idempotencyReplay:true`, no second debit) — it is **mandatory** because a keyless retry would otherwise be charged again as a fresh Lv+1 upgrade (a paid double-charge): absent/empty key ⇒ 400 `idempotency_key_required`, unparseable body ⇒ 400 `invalid_body`, both rejected **before** any lock/debit. The key is GLOBAL across all structures, so reusing it on a DIFFERENT structure ⇒ 409 `idempotency_key_conflict` (use a fresh key per upgrade; ownership is checked before any replay so a key can't touch someone else's structure). **Ownership is asserted against the authoritative `land_parcels.owner_avatar_id`** — the structure AND its parent parcel are both row-locked (`FOR UPDATE OF s, p`), so a stale denormalized structure owner can never pay to upgrade a parcel they no longer hold (owner drift ⇒ 409 `ownership_desync`). Insufficient balance ⇒ 400 `insufficient_clawtokens`.
- **THE KEY TIER RULE:** higher-tier parcels unlock **nicer buildings** AND let you **upgrade them further**. Each tier is a superset of the one below:

  | Tier | Max upgrade level | Homes unlocked | Shops unlocked |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **Starter** | 2 | shack, cottage | stall, shopfront |
  | **C** | 3 | + house | + market |
  | **B** | 4 | + villa | + emporium |
  | **A** | 5 | + mansion | + grand bazaar |
  | **Founder** | 5 + premium | ALL + `Founders' Estate` | ALL + `Founders' Exchange` |

  An upgrade past the parcel's tier ceiling ⇒ 409 `tier_max_level`; past the global Lv5 cap ⇒ 409 `max_level_reached`. So a Starter plot caps low; a Founders' plot climbs to the top level and unlocks the premium founder-only buildings.

### 18b.c. Leaderboard credit (same engine, same weights as everything else)

Land actions feed the one unified leaderboard (`leaderboard.ts`, see §7 + `ARCHITECTURE.md §5b`), scored from `LAND_EVENT_WEIGHTS`/`LAND_EVENT_DAILY_CAPS` in `@clawville/shared`:

| Action | Event | Weight | Daily cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| Acquire a parcel (free starter OR priced buy) | `land.parcel.purchased` | 5 | 5 |
| Place a home/shop | `land.structure.placed` | 3 | 5 |
| Upgrade a structure | `land.structure.upgraded` | 5 | 10 |
| Sell a shop service | `land.service.sold` | 40 | 50 |

`land.service.sold` (selling a shop service, see §18b.i) is emitted on every fresh settled sale AND, as of the P3 Slice-4 leaderboard-progression pickup (2026-07-05), **IS now scored** — a settled sale credits the SELLER's `/leaderboard` rank (`ARCHITECTURE.md §5b`). Caps are per (subject, day). Players (avatar-only) and Trainers (agent) score land **identically** — the same FILTER columns sit in both the `agent_daily` and `avatar_daily` CTEs. Because this is the one CROSS-SUBJECT land event (the buyer pays, the SELLER is scored) at the highest land weight (40), it carries two anti-farm rules the other land events don't: it counts **paid sales only** (a free `priceCt=0` sale earns no rank — still logged for audit), and it counts **distinct buyers only** (`COUNT(DISTINCT buyerAvatarId)`), so a single buyer buying the same shop 50× credits the seller just once that day. To actually reach the 50/day cap a shop must serve 50 *different* paying customers — wash-trading with one accomplice is worth a single point-unit, not the cap.

### 18b.d. Human / agent PARITY (Rule E5)

Every land write — claim, buy, place, upgrade — goes through `requireAuthOrAgentSession`, which resolves a REAL avatar for BOTH a logged-in **human** (Lucia cookie) AND a **connected/hosted agent** (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → its bound avatar). There is **no guest fallback** on these routes (guests get 401), so an agent buys/places/upgrades **as itself**, spending its own CT and earning the same leaderboard credit a human would. No client value reaches any debit (avatar server-resolved, price server-read, upgrade cost server-derived). **PARITY note —** human path: the `/api/land/*` endpoints via cookie; agent path: the same endpoints via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`; settlement binds to `identity.avatarId`. Phase 3 adds the agent `tools.json` + `[ACTION:]` discovery surface so an agent can DRIVE the land economy through its action channel (settlement is already parity-bound; that phase is discovery-only).

### 18b.e. "FOR RENT" showroom — decorative model homes on outer lots (2026-06-18)

So the land world reads as **populated** rather than 180 identical empty for-sale lots, a **client-side, deterministic showroom** pre-fills ~16 outer **starter-tier** lots with model buildings, each carrying a **"FOR RENT" sign**. Purely **visual** — it writes nothing to the economy/DB; it advertises the two buyer journeys (buy an empty lot and build, OR grab a ready-looking lot and fix it up via the existing buy → place → upgrade flow).

- **Seed:** `LAND_SHOWROOM` in `packages/shared/src/constants/land-showroom.ts` — pure math (no RNG/clock, multiplayer-safe). **Starter showcase (FOR RENT):** every 7th starter lot (16 of 108), evenly spread around the outer perimeter; each gets a style (coastal/fantasy/driftwood, cycled), home/shop (alternating), low level (1–2, scale only). **Premium showcase (PREMIUM):** the first 6 of the 8 Founders'-Row (inner) lots, alternating a **skyscraper** (`premium-tower`, a `home`) and a **mall** (`premium-mall`, a `shop`) at level 5 so they tower over the starter cottages — the "higher tier = nicer buildings" payoff made visible (2 founder lots left open for the real auction). Both model pairs are Kenney CC0 low-poly. `SHOWROOM_PARCEL_IDS` is the combined id set.
- **Render:** `apps/web/src/lib/three/land-showroom.tsx` (mounted in `World3DCanvas` beside `LandStructures`). Reuses the proven GLB clone/normalize/ground/cull pipeline from `land-structures.tsx`; the 6 cottage GLBs + the 2 premium GLBs (`premium-tower/home.glb`, `premium-mall/shop.glb`) back it (primitive fallback if a model is missing). Each lot's sign is a per-lot plank+post placed by the same edge-facing-origin math as the for-sale signs, using one of two shared `CanvasTexture`s picked by the entry's `signLabel`: amber **"FOR RENT" / "CLAWVILLE ESTATES"** for starter lots, gold **"PREMIUM" / "FOUNDERS' ROW"** for founder lots. Iris-Xe-safe (no drei Text, no ShaderMaterial, zero per-frame allocs). On every showroom lot, `land-parcels.tsx` **suppresses the FOR-SALE sign** (the lot frame stays) so they read FOR RENT / PREMIUM only.
- **Hide-when-owned:** each showroom lot's group hides the instant its parcel status flips to `owned` in the land store, so a real buyer's placed structure (`LandStructures`) cleanly takes over with no overlap. (Today this fires only once parcel-ownership is hydrated into the store; until that wire lands the showroom is always-on decoration — see the TODO for the "claim a model home & inherit its level" transfer economy + interiors.)

### 18b.f. FOR-SALE signage — 3 categories (regular / premium / premium-partner)

Every one of the 180 plots carries a **FOR SALE sign**, styled by a **visual category** (separate from the economic tier — signage is purely cosmetic, `packages/shared/src/constants/land-signage.ts`, rendered by `land-parcels.tsx`). Three categories, escalating in size + fanciness:

| Category | Plots | Sign | Look |
|---|---|---|---|
| **regular** | `b` / `c` / `starter` tiers (the larger outer square frames) | basic, 68×28wu plank | dark plank, thin tan border, "FOR SALE" |
| **premium** | `founder` + `a` tiers (the inner square frames just outside the town circle) | ~1.35× bigger, 92×38wu | gold double-border + corner ticks, "FOR SALE" + "PREMIUM" |
| **premium-partner** | a **game-owner-curated subset** of premium plots (`PREMIUM_PARTNER_PARCEL_IDS`) | ~1.7× bigger, 116×48wu | cyan/platinum ornate border + topper bar, "FOR SALE" + "PARTNER" |

**Premium and premium-partner share the same land** (the inner premium ring) — partner plots are specific premium lots WE reserve for partners (default 6: 3 founder + 3 a-tier, spread around the ring; edit the set to re-designate). `getLandSignCategory(parcel)` resolves the category (partner-id → premium-partner; founder/a tier → premium; else regular). Draw budget: 5 tier-body meshes + 3 sign-post + 3 sign-plank = 11 (was 7). The signs are merged per category (one mesh each), Iris-Xe-safe (text baked into per-category `CanvasTexture`s, no drei Text), all disposed on unmount. **Most plots stay empty = "builder" plots** (buy + build your own); only ~22 carry the showroom example buildings. The land-showroom layer no longer draws its own sign (signage is unified here), so a showroom lot shows its example building **plus** its category FOR-SALE sign.

### 18b.g. World-reactive parcel state + 3D click-to-buy bridge (2026-06-22)

**Reactive world hydration:** `LandStateHydrator` (headless, `apps/web/src/lib/three/land-state-hydrator.tsx`) is mounted in `World3DCanvas` next to the land groups. On mount it fetches ALL parcels (`available` + `owned`) from the public `/api/land/parcels` endpoint in parallel and writes the results (keyed by `parcelCode`) into `useLandStore.parcels` via `setParcels(...)`. Uses TanStack Query with a 60s stale time and a shared `['landParcels']` query key. After a buy/claim/place/upgrade the Land Office modal calls `invalidateQueries(['landParcels'])`, which triggers an automatic re-fetch — so the FOR-SALE sign on a freshly-bought lot disappears within seconds, live, without a page reload. Guest-safe (public endpoint).

**Store key fix:** `useLandStore.structures` is now keyed by `parcelCode` (= `LAND_PARCELS[i].id`, e.g. `parcel-a-01`), not by the DB UUID. `PlacedStructure` carries both `parcelId` (UUID, backward-compat) and `parcelCode` (render key). This closes the join bug where owned buildings never rendered because the 3D layer joins on `parcelCode` while the store was keyed on UUID.

**`openLandOffice(parcelCode?: string)` click bridge:** `useGameStore.openLandOffice` now accepts an optional `parcelCode`. The 3D render layer calls `openLandOffice(parcelCode)` on a FOR-SALE sign click; the modal opens on the For-Sale tab, auto-scrolls to the matching parcel card (highlighted with a cyan glow ring), and clears the focus after the user scrolls. No focus = modal opens at the top as before. Focus is also cleared on modal close.

### 18b.h. Rent in the Land Office UI (2026-06-27)

The rent backend (§18b.a) is now surfaced in the Land Office modal (`apps/web/src/components/game/land/land-office-modal.tsx`). On the **For Sale** tab, a parcel card shows a **Rent** action (alongside Buy) **only when `parcel.rentCtWeekly != null`** — i.e. c-tier parcels, which offer BOTH paths so the player chooses buy-outright vs rent-weekly. Starter shows only its existing Buy/Claim path; founder shows neither (auction). The Rent button greys to "Need more CT" when `clawTokens < rentCtWeekly`.

Confirming opens a **RentModal** (mirrors the BuyModal exactly): it states the **weekly cost** (`rentCtWeekly` CT/week), the **7-day** first period, that rent **auto-charges weekly** with a grace window then **eviction** if unpaid (your build is preserved), and "your balance". Confirm → `api.rentParcel(id)` with an **empty body** (the rent comes from the server row, never the client). On success it toasts the paid-through date, refreshes My Land, re-hydrates the 3D ownership overlay, and invalidates `['landParcels']` so the in-world FOR-SALE sign vanishes live (same flow as a buy). Error mapping: insufficient CT → "Not enough ClawTokens — need {rent}/wk, you have {have}"; `parcel_not_available`/409 → "Someone just took this parcel"; `parcel_cap_reached` → the 5-parcel limit; `rent_not_available`/`founder_not_in_v1` → "This parcel isn't rentable".

**PARITY (Rule E5):** the route is `requireAuthOrAgentSession`, so a connected/hosted agent rents via the SAME endpoint to its own avatar with REAL CT — the UI just calls it (no human-only regression).

**ACTIVATION NOTE:** the current world geometry seeds ZERO c-tier parcels (only founder + starter today), so no parcel is CT-rentable in-world *yet* — the Rent UI is correct and ready the moment a CT-priced c-tier ring is added to the world (the rent-engine activation gap from `ARCHITECTURE.md`'s 2026-06-24 entry). Until then the For-Sale list shows only Buy/Claim because no card carries a non-null `rentCtWeekly`.

### 18b.i. Run-a-store — service listings (P3 Slice 4, 2026-07-05, BUILT — pending staging e2e + founder sign-off)

Owning a **shop** structure (§18b.b) now does something beyond decoration: a shop owner can **list a peer service** — a piece of work another avatar (human or agent) pays ClawTokens for. Backend: `apps/api/src/routes/land.ts` (6 new routes over `service_listings`/`service_purchases`; full contract in `ARCHITECTURE.md §2` "Slice 4" callout). This was a backend-first slice — the routes + schema were already built; this pass added the UI + tests + this doc entry, plus a same-day adversarial money-audit hardening of the buy path.

- **What a shop service is:** a short listing (`title` 1–80 chars, optional `description` ≤500 chars, `priceCt` 0–1,000,000) tied to ONE owned/rented, ACTIVE `'shop'` structure. Not a marketplace SKU, not an in-game item — it's a peer-to-peer commerce primitive (coaching, a crafted good, a one-off favor) that settles in CT directly between two avatars.
- **List:** from the Land Office modal's **🛍️ Services → My Listings** panel (only visible if you own an active shop), fill in title/description/price and submit. Server-side: must own the shop (via the AUTHORITATIVE `land_parcels.owner_avatar_id`, same discipline as upgrade) → 403 `not_structure_owner` otherwise; must actually be a `'shop'` (not a `'home'`) → 400 `not_a_shop`; the structure must be active (not eviction-archived) → 409 `structure_archived`.
- **The 6-listing cap:** a shop may have at most **6 simultaneously ACTIVE** listings (`MAX_ACTIVE_LISTINGS_PER_STRUCTURE`, counted under the per-avatar advisory lock) — a 7th active listing ⇒ 409 `listing_cap_reached`. The Manage panel shows a live "`X / 6 active`" counter and disables the list button at the cap. Pausing or delisting one frees a slot.
- **Manage (owner-only):** `PATCH /api/land/services/:listingId` edits title/description/price and/or flips `status` between `active` / `paused` / `delisted` — non-owner attempts ⇒ 403 `not_listing_owner`. Pausing pulls a listing out of both the public browse feeds AND the active-listing cap count without deleting it; delisting is a soft, permanent removal (no un-delist path in v1). An owner reads ALL their own listings — active AND paused/delisted — via `GET /api/land/services/mine` (auth, owner-scoped by `identity.avatarId`, uncached; `api.getMyServices()`), the backend for the Manage panel's across-reload view + re-activate flow (the public browse feeds are active-only, so this is the only surface that shows a paused listing).
- **Browse + buy:** anyone (logged-in human or connected/hosted agent — no guest write path) can browse **🛍️ Services → Browse** — a paged, all-active feed (`GET /api/land/services?page=&limit=`, newest first) — or view one shop's active listings (`GET /api/land/structures/:structureId/services`). Buying (`POST /api/land/services/:listingId/buy`) requires a fresh `idempotencyKey` (8–64 chars) generated per buy-click, exactly like a structure upgrade — a genuine retry of the SAME click reuses the key (server replays the cached purchase, `cached:true`, no double charge); a NEW click gets a NEW key. Rejections: buying your own listing ⇒ 409 `self_purchase`; a paused/delisted listing ⇒ 409 `listing_not_active`; insufficient balance ⇒ 400 `insufficient_clawtokens`. Adversarial-audit buy guards (2026-07-05): the listing's shop is re-read UNDER LOCK before any CT moves, so a shop that was evicted/archived (rent-lapse — the rent sweeper does NOT cascade-delist listings) or whose parcel changed hands ⇒ 409 `structure_unavailable`; a non-CT USDC (`partner`) listing can never settle in CT ⇒ 409 `not_a_peer_listing`; and a rare mutual-buy deadlock ⇒ 409 `concurrent_retry` (fully rolled back — safe to retry with the same key).
- **CT settlement — full transfer, NO rake (v1 design):** buying a service moves the listed `priceCt` **entirely** from buyer to seller through the canonical ledger (`debitClawTokens` + `creditClawTokens`, composed in ONE transaction with the purchase row — conservation: buyer −P, seller +P, total unchanged). `platformFeeBps` exists on the schema but is reserved at 0 — this is peer commerce, not a house-banked casino game, so there is no house cut in v1 (a future rake would be a one-line route change, not a migration).
- **Leaderboard credit (defined, not yet scored):** every fresh sale emits `land.service.sold` (weight 40, cap 50/day — see §7 + `ARCHITECTURE.md §5b`) keyed to the **seller** (a run-a-store sale is the seller's contribution, not the buyer's). The event row is written on every settled sale today, but the scoring CTE wiring itself is a **deferred cross-domain pickup** owned by the leaderboard-progression domain — so a sale does not yet move `/leaderboard` rank (see §18b.c above and `ARCHITECTURE.md §5b`).
- **Human / agent PARITY (Rule E5) — inherited, not new:** list/manage/buy all resolve through the SAME `requireAuthOrAgentSession` every other land route uses, so a connected/hosted agent lists, manages, and buys services **as itself** — its own avatar, its own CT, no guest fallback (guest = 401). No new resolver was written for this slice.
- **UI:** `apps/web/src/components/game/land/land-office-modal.tsx`'s new **🛍️ Services** tab, always visible (browse works for anyone; the Manage sub-tab only appears once you own an active shop). TanStack Query key namespace `['landServices', 'browse'|'structure'|'mine', ...]`. The public Browse feeds stay active-only; the **Manage panel now reads the owner's listings across ALL statuses** via `GET /api/land/services/mine` (`['landServices','mine']`, `api.getMyServices()`), so a paused listing stays visible with a Re-activate action and survives a reload. Every list/edit/pause/delist invalidates `['landServices','mine']` (the durable source of truth — the earlier session-only optimistic-merge workaround was removed) plus the browse pages and the `['avatar']` CT-balance query.

### 18b.j. Phase B tenure model — deposit-escrow (starter) + CLV hold-to-keep (c/b/a/founder) (Tokenomics Phase B, 2026-07-07)

The founder-locked tokenomics model (`.clawville-brain/plans/tokenomics-*.md §M3`) replaces **buy-outright** with two tenure mechanisms: land is **never sold permanently** — you either post a refundable **vCLAW deposit** (starter) or **hold CLV** (c/b/a/founder) to keep a parcel, and a weekly CT flow keeps holding it honest. This is the **"free to play, hold to own"** land layer. Backend: `apps/api/src/routes/land.ts` (routes) + `apps/api/src/services/land-rent-sweeper.ts` (the weekly settle) + schema/migration `packages/database/.../0013_land_tenure_phaseB.sql`. Full route/settle/conservation contract: `ARCHITECTURE.md §13` (2026-07-07 Phase B entry).

- **BUY + RENT are RETIRED (all tiers).** `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/buy` and `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/rent` now return **409 `tenure_model_active`** for every tier (they answer BEFORE auth/DB — dead ends; the old bodies live in git history). §18b.a/§18b.h describe the retired legacy model. Acquisition is now `claim-starter` (deposit-escrow) or `claim-hold` (CLV hold-to-keep). The legacy `tenure='rented'` rows already in the world keep working (the sweeper's `rented` branch is unchanged) but **no new rentals can start**.

- **Starter = deposit-escrow (B1).** `POST /api/land/claim-starter` now **debits a refundable `LAND_STARTER_DEPOSIT_CT` (2000 units ≈ $20 at the ¢-peg) INTO ESCROW** — the CT leaves the claimant's balance and is recorded as a NUMBER on the parcel (`deposit_remaining_ct`), credited to **nobody** (it is not revenue). The parcel becomes `tenure='deposit'`, `rent_ct_weekly=100`, paid through +7 days. Still **one starter per avatar**, idempotent (`alreadyOwned:true` replays are NEVER re-charged). Insufficient balance ⇒ 400 `insufficient_clawtokens`. No linked wallet required.
  - **Top-up:** `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/deposit-topup {amountCt: 1..1_000_000}` (owner + `tenure='deposit'` only) grows the escrow remainder; re-covering a full week clears an open grace window. A self-debit — can never move value to anyone else.
  - **Weekly draw (sweeper):** each week auto-draws `min(remainder, rent_ct_weekly)` from the escrow → **house treasury** with **no tenant debit** (balanced by the claim/top-up debit). A partial (remainder < a week) drains to zero and opens grace WITHOUT buying the week; exhaustion → grace → **lapse forfeits the remainder to the treasury** (nothing refunded) and the parcel reverts to the pool.
  - **Release:** `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/release` on a deposit parcel **refunds the exact remainder** back to the claimant (receiver-SOFT) and reverts the parcel. **Conservation (unit-proven): deposit + Σ top-ups == Σ weekly draws + refund + forfeit, exactly** — the escrow can never mint; net CT supply change over a tenancy is ≤ 0.
  - **Deed-lock guard (marketplace C4 seam, 2026-07-07):** releasing a parcel whose deed is escrow-locked by a **live P2P marketplace listing** now refuses **409 `deed_locked_by_listing`** (cancel the listing first — that releases the lock); the rent sweeper likewise **parks** (suppresses) a lapse-eviction on a deed-locked parcel instead of reverting it (grace untouched, no forfeit while parked; normal eviction resumes once the lock clears) — so a listed deed can never be double-sold out from under a settling buyer. Same 409 for human and agent (guard sits before any identity branch). Contract: `ARCHITECTURE.md` Last Audited (deed-lock guard entry).

- **c/b/a/founder = CLV hold-to-keep (B2).** `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/claim-hold` acquires a parcel with **NO CT debit** — the consideration is **holding CLV**. The caller must hold CLV `uiAmount ≥ SUM(existing non-grandfathered hold thresholds) + this tier's threshold` (thresholds **STACK**): **c 100k · b 500k · a 2.5M · founder 10M CLV** (`LAND_HOLD_THRESHOLDS_CLV`). A weekly **CT upkeep** (c/b/a keep their stamped `rent_ct_weekly`; founder is stamped `FOUNDER_UPKEEP_CT_WEEKLY=2400`) draws from the **holder's avatar balance → treasury**.
  - **CLV read (fail-CLOSED at claim):** human (`identity.kind='user'`) → `users.linked_wallet_pubkey` (proven via `routes/wallet-link.ts`), agent (`identity.kind='agent'`) → the agent's own `avatars.wallet_address` custodial wallet. Errors: no linked wallet ⇒ 403 `wallet_not_linked`; agent with no custodial pubkey ⇒ 403 `agent_wallet_missing`; RPC unavailable ⇒ 503 `clv_balance_unavailable` (a hold is NEVER granted on an unconfirmed balance); below the stacked requirement ⇒ 403 `insufficient_clv_hold {requiredClv, heldClv}`. Starter tier ⇒ 400 `use_claim_starter`. The RPC runs BEFORE the money tx; the stacked-sum + compare + flip run under `advisory(avatar)` + `FOR UPDATE`.
  - **Weekly re-check (sweeper, fail-OPEN):** the hold is re-verified against the subject's LIVE CLV by `hold_subject`. Every unconfirmable path (RPC down, unlinked-after-claim, missing pubkey) **fails OPEN** — LOUD warn + still charge upkeep, NEVER grace/lapse on an unconfirmed balance. Confirmed-below-threshold → grace → lapse; insufficient CT for upkeep → grace → lapse. `release` on a hold reverts with **no refund** (nothing was escrowed).

- **Grandfathering (legacy owners).** `apps/api/scripts/migrate-land-tenure-phaseB.ts` (idempotent, explicit-URL-only) migrates every legacy buy-outright parcel (`tenure='owned'`, tier c/b/a/founder) into a **grandfathered hold**: `grandfathered=true`, `hold_subject=NULL`, a fresh 7-day paid-through, upkeep = its existing `rent_ct_weekly` (founder → 2400). A grandfathered hold **pays the normal weekly upkeep but is NEVER CLV-checked and is EXCLUDED from every stacked-threshold sum** — existing owners are not retroactively priced out. Legacy `tenure='starter'` (free) and `tenure='rented'` rows are deliberately left untouched.

- **B/A inventory seeded (DB-only).** The canonical `apps/api/scripts/seed-land-parcels.ts` now ALSO seeds **12 B-tier + 6 A-tier** parcels (`parcel-b-00..11`, `parcel-a-00..05`) via the shared pure helper `generateParcelsForTier(tier, count)` (`packages/shared/src/constants/land-parcels.ts`) — the SAME per-tier square-perimeter generator that builds `LAND_PARCELS`, called at a seed-only count. This does **NOT** change `LAND_PARCELS`/`PARCEL_TIER_COUNTS` (render supply stays 56; proven byte-identical), so the hold tiers get claimable supply while the 3D render stays untouched. The seed now also stamps `rent_ct_weekly` on every row (the weekly hold upkeep the sweeper draws). Idempotent (`ON CONFLICT (parcel_code) DO NOTHING`); the a/b cells are asserted disjoint from all render-backed cells (fails loud in dry-run before any DB write). **⚠ Known world↔backend parity gap (intentional):** these 18 rows are economic inventory reachable via the API (`claim-hold`), but are **NOT yet drawn in the 3D world** — wiring the a/b render (bumping `PARCEL_TIER_COUNTS` + re-proving the no-overlap geometry + browser verify) is a separate land + 3da pass, deliberately out of this economy slice.

- **PARITY (Rule E5) — money + hold bind to the agent.** Every Phase B write goes through `requireAuthOrAgentSession`, so a connected/hosted agent claims/tops-up/releases/holds **as itself** (its own avatar, its own CT). Uniquely for hold-to-keep, the CLV that backs the parcel binds to the **agent's own custodial wallet** (not a human's), and the sweeper's weekly re-check reads that same custodial wallet — full agent parity on a money-and-identity path. **PARITY note —** human path: `POST /api/land/{claim-starter, parcels/:id/claim-hold, parcels/:id/deposit-topup, parcels/:id/release}` via Lucia cookie; agent path: the same endpoints via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar; settlement binds to `identity.avatarId`; hold CLV read: human = linked wallet, agent = `avatars.wallet_address`.

### 18b.k. Claim (hold-to-keep) + Starter deposit-claim in the Land Office UI (2026-07-08)

The §18b.j tenure backend gets its HUMAN web surface in the Land Office modal (`apps/web/src/components/game/land/land-office-modal.tsx`) — before this, NO tier could be acquired from the UI: hold tiers (c/b/a/founder) had no claim UI, and BOTH the starter and hold For-Sale cards still showed the retired **Buy** button (a guaranteed 409 `tenure_model_active`), while the My Land "Claim free home" CTA silently charged the 2000-CT deposit under stale "free" copy.

- **Every For-Sale tier now acquires via a Claim modal — the dead Buy AND Rent paths are fully removed.** Hold tiers → `ClaimHoldModal`; starter → `StarterClaimModal` (below). `POST /buy` and `POST /rent` are both architecturally retired for every tier (§18b.j; 409 `tenure_model_active`), so the land `BuyModal`/`buyErrorMessage` and `RentModal`/`rentErrorMessage`/`formatPaidThrough` components were deleted (the `PriceText` helper too — its only consumer was the Buy branch). `api.buyParcel`/`api.rentParcel` are left in the client, unused-but-harmless. (The **ServicesTab** service-listing buy path is a separate, live CT surface and is untouched.)
- **For Sale cards (hold tiers c/b/a/founder):** the dead Buy button is REPLACED by a **Claim** button showing the tier's CLV hold requirement compact-formatted (`Hold 100K/500K/2.5M/10M $CLAWVILLE` — DERIVED from `holdThresholdForTier(tier)`, because available parcels carry `holdThresholdCt = null` until the server stamps it at claim) plus the weekly CT upkeep (`rentCtWeekly`, or `FOUNDER_UPKEEP_CT_WEEKLY = 2400` for founder rows), and a small **✓ $CLAWVILLE qualified / $CLAWVILLE short** badge when the linked-wallet balance is readable.
- **Qualification read:** the wallet-visibility-ui team's shared **`useWalletLink()`** hook (`@/hooks/use-wallet-link`, the `['wallet-link']` TanStack cache shared with the HUD wallet chip) — returns `{ linked, walletPubkey, clvUiAmount, clvAvailable, isLoading }`. The modal collapses it to `heldClv = linked && clvAvailable ? clvUiAmount : null`; not-linked / read-down → the Link-a-wallet path. (No `api.getWalletLink()`/`WalletLinkStatus` in the land modal anymore — deduped onto the hook per the cross-team contract.)
- **Stacking, computed client-side:** the server requires CLV ≥ Σ(existing non-grandfathered hold thresholds) + this tier's threshold. Because `honoRequest` DROPS the `requiredClv`/`heldClv` extras from the 403 error body, the UI computes `existingHoldSum` from the player's owned `tenure='hold'` parcels (preferring the server-stamped `holdThresholdCt`, falling back to tier derivation) and shows the breakdown in the modal ("this parcel 500K + 100K already held = 600K"). **Known limitation:** the parcel DTO carries no `grandfathered` flag, so a grandfathered legacy owner's holds (excluded from the SERVER sum) are counted client-side — the display can over-state for those few accounts; the Claim button is therefore NEVER disabled on a client-side "short" verdict (server is authoritative).
- **ClaimHoldModal** (mirrors BuyModal): parcel code + tier badge, "$CLAWVILLE hold required" (with stacking breakdown), "You hold" from the linked wallet ("Wallet not linked" / "Can't verify right now" fallbacks), a qualified/short indicator, and the explainer — **no CT is spent to claim**; you keep the parcel by HOLDING the CLV plus a weekly CT upkeep auto-charged like rent (grace → evict, build preserved). Not-linked users get a **"Link a wallet"** primary CTA (Claim hidden) → wallet seam: `onRequestWalletLink` closes the claim modal and calls the wallet team's **`openWalletLink()`** store action (WalletModal is mounted top-level in `/game`; the land modal mounts nothing). After linking, the shared `['wallet-link']` cache refresh flips `linked`/`heldClv` automatically.
- **Confirm →** `api.claimHoldParcel(id)` — `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/claim-hold` with an **EMPTY body** (`emptyStrictBodySchema`; no client price/threshold ever). Success toasts "🏝️ Claimed {code} — held with {threshold} $CLAWVILLE!" then runs the SAME parity refresh as a buy (refresh My Land → re-hydrate 3D overlay → invalidate `['landParcels']` → land on My Land) so the in-world FOR-SALE sign flips live.
- **Error map** (`claimHoldErrorMessage`, switched on `errCode()` — never prose-matched): `wallet_not_linked` → link CTA copy · `agent_wallet_missing` (defensive, agent-only) · `insufficient_clv_hold` → client-computed required + held · `clv_balance_unavailable` → "can't verify right now — try again" (fail-closed RPC read) · `parcel_not_available` / `parcel_cap_reached` (5-parcel max) · `use_claim_starter` → "claimed with a refundable CT deposit, not a $CLAWVILLE hold" · `deed_locked_by_listing` (defensive marketplace-lock branch) · `parcel_not_found` / `invalid_parcel_id` / `invalid_body` · 401 → "Log in to claim land.".
- **STARTER deposit-claim (`StarterClaimModal`, parcel-agnostic):** starter is acquired with a **refundable `LAND_STARTER_DEPOSIT_CT` = 2000 CT deposit** held in escrow (NOT a purchase), from which the **`LAND_STARTER_RENT_CT_WEEKLY` = 100 CT/week** upkeep auto-draws; the remainder is refunded on `release`, and a deposit that can't cover a week → grace → the cove is released. `POST /claim-starter` **auto-assigns** an available Starter Cove (no parcelId — SKIP LOCKED pick), so the modal DISCLOSES the auto-assignment. It is reachable from BOTH the For-Sale starter cards (dead Buy → **Claim** button, showing "Refundable 2,000 CT deposit" + the upkeep line) AND the **My Land "🏡 Claim your first home" CTA** — which previously charged the 2000 CT SILENTLY under "free" copy and now routes through this confirm modal (honest terms, `disabled` "Need more CT" when balance < 2000). Success toasts honestly ("held as a refundable deposit", or "You already have your Starter Cove {code}." on the idempotent `alreadyOwned` path) then runs the same parity refresh. `starterClaimErrorMessage`: `insufficient_clawtokens` → "need 2,000 CT for the refundable deposit" · `no_starter_available` → "all Starter Coves taken" · 401 → "Log in to claim a home." No client amount is ever sent (`claimStarterPlot()` has no body).
- **PARITY (Rule E5):** these are the HUMAN paths over the already-parity-bound routes (§18b.j) — no API/route/schema change in this diff. **PARITY note —** human path: Land Office → For Sale/My Land → Claim → `POST /api/land/parcels/:id/claim-hold` (hold) or `POST /api/land/claim-starter` (starter) via Lucia cookie; agent path (pre-existing, unchanged): same endpoints via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar; settlement binds to `identity.avatarId`.

## 19. Map layout

Source: `packages/shared/src/constants/map-locations.ts`. 160×160 tile grid, 32 px/tile = 5120×5120 world units. Village center tile `(80, 80)` → world `(0, 0)`. Building ring at radius 68 tiles = 2176 wu, 10 slots at 36° spacing.

See **`3dStructure.md §1`** for the full coordinate system + axis conventions, and **`WorldContent.md §2`** for the building roster.

**Route-isolated Reef Race v7 track (2026-07-18):** the activity course is not part of the village grid. Its canonical map is `packages/shared/src/reef-race/track-layout.ts`: a 52-CP, 95,741.0wu closed surf loop with 40 curvature reversals, broad overtaking sections, a `t=.1742–.3260` pinched S-chicane, and a `t=.4911–.7357` pinched near-hairpin. Half-width varies smoothly from 454.6 to 1615.6wu through the one shared `ReefSpline.widthAt(t)` profile used by server authority and the 3D ribbon. See §18d and `3dStructure.md §10b` for gameplay and render contracts. Local only; not deployed or signed off. No human/agent protocol, economy, or settlement change.

## 20. Recent material changes

Compact log. The audit-history wall at the top of the prior version of this doc has been replaced with this. Entries are gameplay-facing — backend/service changes belong in `ARCHITECTURE.md §13`, 3D-render changes in `3dStructure.md §13`.

- 2026-07-08 — **Land Office Claim + Starter deposit-claim UI (§18b.k).** The human web surface for §18b.j land acquisition. For-Sale hold tiers (c/b/a/founder) → **Claim** button + **ClaimHoldModal** (CLV hold threshold + weekly upkeep + qualified/short badge, client-computed stacking, no-CT-to-claim explainer). Starter → **StarterClaimModal** surfacing the refundable **2000 CT deposit** honestly (auto-assignment disclosed; 100 CT/wk drawn from it; refundable-on-release), reached from the For-Sale starter Claim button AND the My Land "Claim your first home" CTA — which previously charged the 2000 CT SILENTLY under "free" copy and now confirms first. The dead **Buy AND Rent** For-Sale paths are fully removed (`BuyModal`/`buyErrorMessage`/`PriceText` + `RentModal`/`rentErrorMessage`/`formatPaidThrough` deleted; every tier acquires via a Claim modal now); `api.buyParcel`/`api.rentParcel` left unused; the live ServicesTab buy path is untouched. Wallet qualification read consumes the wallet-visibility-ui **`useWalletLink()`** hook (shared `['wallet-link']` cache) and the Link-a-wallet CTA calls **`openWalletLink()`** (no own `getWalletLink`/`WalletLinkStatus`). Buy-parity success refresh (My Land + 3D overlay + `['landParcels']`). Client-only — `LandParcelDTO` gains `depositRemainingCt`/`holdThresholdCt`, new `ClaimHoldResponse`, `api.claimHoldParcel()` (empty body). No API/route/schema change.
- 2026-07-08 — **Autonomous double-body render fix — §1f render/camera handoff RESOLVED (§B.1b, 3da, `feat/agent-autonomy-parity`).** Flipping Autonomous previously left the human's local avatar visibly rendered NEXT TO the server-streamed `ocb-` driver body (two copies of the same character) — flagged as an open "3da follow-up" in §1f when §B.1 shipped. Fixed: the local `PlayerAvatar` now mounts ONLY in Controlled (`player`); Autonomous shows the SOLE streamed driver body, and both the follow camera and the minimap blip retarget onto it (by the `bodyId` the activation call already returns) instead of the human's now-static position. No gameplay/economy change — render/camera only. Full detail: `3dStructure.md §3`.
- 2026-07-07 — **Tokenomics Phase B — land tenure model (new §18b.j).** Buy-outright is replaced with two tenure mechanisms and **`/buy` + `/rent` are RETIRED for all tiers** (409 `tenure_model_active`). **Starter → deposit-escrow (B1):** `claim-starter` debits a refundable 2000-unit deposit INTO escrow (credited to nobody), weekly rent auto-draws from the escrow → treasury, `deposit-topup` grows it, `release` refunds the remainder, exhaustion forfeits it (conservation unit-proven: deposit + top-ups == draws + refund + forfeit). **c/b/a/founder → CLV hold-to-keep (B2):** `claim-hold` grants with no CT debit but requires the subject's linked/custodial CLV ≥ stacked per-tier thresholds (c 100k/b 500k/a 2.5M/founder 10M), weekly CT upkeep drawn from the holder; the sweeper re-checks CLV fail-OPEN. **Grandfathering:** `migrate-land-tenure-phaseB.ts` migrates legacy owners to grandfathered holds (pay upkeep, never CLV-checked, excluded from stacking). **B/A inventory:** the canonical `seed-land-parcels.ts` (via a new shared `generateParcelsForTier` helper, no change to the render supply) also seeds 12 B + 6 A parcels as DB-only economic supply + stamps `rent_ct_weekly` for all rows (not yet rendered — flagged world↔backend parity gap). Full agent parity: hold CLV binds to the agent's own custodial wallet. ## 6. Quests + bounties

### 6a. Quest board (`/api/quests/*` — `apps/api/src/routes/quests.ts`)

Admin-created quest definitions. Player submissions land in `quest_submissions` and payouts in `quest_rewards` (linked to the ClawToken ledger). UI: `<QuestBoardModal>` in the sidebar menu.

### 6b. Bounty board (`/api/bounties/*` — `apps/api/src/routes/bounties.ts`)

Community-posted bounties with reputation tracking. Tables: `bounties`, `bounty_attempts`, `bounty_rewards`, `bounty_reputation`. UI: `<BountyBoardModal>`.

**Human/agent parity (Rule E5, 2026-06-30).** Every write route (`/create`, `/:id/claim`, `/:id/submit`, `/:id/abandon`, `/attempts/:attemptId/review`, `PATCH /:id`, `DELETE /:id`, `/my-bounties`, `/my-attempts`) now binds to `requireAuthOrAgentSession → identity.avatarId` — a human (Lucia cookie) AND a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) both post/claim/complete AS THEMSELVES. Previously the whole board was `requireAuth` (human-only), a parity defect; the CT settlement + reputation now bind to the resolved avatar for either.

**Two payout rails (`payment_rail` column, default `'ct'`):**
- **`ct` (classic, unchanged):** the creator's ClawTokens are debited into escrow at create (`bounty_escrow`) and released to the winning hunter on approve (`bounty_reward`). This is the entire live board.
- **`usdc` (Phase 1, gated OFF + dry-run by default):** the reward is on-chain USDC settled through the SAP Option-C escrow gate — NOT ClawTokens. A USDC bounty MUST carry `acceptance_criteria` (a verdict needs something to judge against) and must be `maxAttempts=1` (single-call escrow, one winning hunter). No CT is debited. The escrow is opened LAZILY at approve time (depositor = creator avatar, worker = hunter avatar, `jobId = bounty.id`): the creator-review **approve IS the v1 requester-approval verdict** → the linkage runs `open → approve → settle` on the SAP escrow (PASS → releases USDC to the hunter; the escrow gate's at-most-once + funds-ceiling guards apply), a reject records a FAIL verdict (no escrow was opened, so nothing to refund). The `covenant_*` / `escrow_*` columns record the verdict provenance (audit-root hex, pass/fail, escrow PDA). **The USDC rail is triple-gated (`SAP_ENABLED` / `SAP_ESCROW_ENABLED` / `SAP_USDC_ESCROW_ENABLED`, all default false) + `SAP_DRY_RUN=true`, so a `payment_rail=usdc` create returns 503 until a deliberate founder flip — no real money moves.** A connected agent must be ledger-capable (proven avatar ownership) to post/settle a USDC bounty; a non-ledger agent is 403 (never a guest demotion).
- **Admin FAIL-refund:** `POST /:id/admin-fail-refund` (admin-only) force-refunds a USDC bounty's opened escrow back to the creator (depositor) — the operator "fail/refund" path the depositor-only SAP refund lacked. The admin can only *trigger* the depositor-bound withdraw; the escrow gate re-asserts depositor identity + its own atomic refund claim.
- **Phase 3 seam (NOT wired):** the `covenant_verdict_id` column + the `VerificationProvider` interface are left untouched so a `CovenantVerificationProvider` (external signed verdict) drops in later without a schema change. Covenant co-signer `DKxXrxxCzAwLSXRUWzUouiW46GNf4PR2mjjhAbtCAkcK`, treasury `J7PyZAGKvprCz4SQ5DKBLAHstJxgVqZcz6kguUoWpP7P` — Phase 3, not referenced by v1 code.
- **Concurrency + integrity guards (2026-06-30, post-audit):** the review approve/reject transitions are now an ATOMIC CLAIM (`UPDATE ... WHERE id=? AND status='submitted' RETURNING`; loser → 409) so two concurrent approves can't double-credit the hunter (the CT double-pay faucet) and two concurrent rejects can't double-release the attempt slot; a partial UNIQUE INDEX (`bounty_attempts_one_approved_per_bounty`) is the DB backstop. A USDC approve PRE-FLIGHTS the escrow rail (503) BEFORE committing, so it never locks a bounty `completed` it then can't settle/refund. PATCH re-asserts `maxAttempts=1` for a USDC bounty. Cancel refuses (409) a USDC bounty that has an open escrow (route via admin-fail-refund). The list/detail/my-bounties/my-attempts responses now carry `paymentRail` (+ `acceptanceCriteria` on detail) so `tokenReward` is unambiguously CT vs whole-USDC.

### 6c. NPC integration

Quest-giver NPC at world center (`<QuestNpc>`) plus the QuestBountyPavilion (`<QuestBountyPavilion>`) anchor the boards in 3D space. The pavilion is an octagonal open-air structure at (0, groundedY, −1220) — 1100 wu behind the town directory sign — housing both boards under one roof. Click the left half of the pavilion (boards 1+2) to open the Quest Board, click the right half (boards 3+4) to open the Bounty Board. Bio-luminescent floating labels ("Quests" cyan / "Bounties" amber) hover above each half. The earlier standalone `<BountyBoardObject>` was removed 2026-05-21 — the pavilion supersedes it.

### 6d. Exchange — peer marketplace (`/api/exchange/*` — `apps/api/src/routes/exchange.ts`)

The town-center Exchange stand where humans + agents trade in ClawTokens. Two listing types (tables `exchange_listings`, `exchange_orders`):
- **NEED** — the poster wants something done and escrows `priceCt × capacity` up front (debited at create); each slot's reward is released to a claimant when the poster confirms their submission.
- **OFFER** — the seller offers something; a buyer escrows `priceCt` when they place an order, released to the seller on confirm. `offer_mode ∈ {one_shot, repeatable}`.

**Human/agent parity (Rule E5, 2026-07-02).** Every WRITE route (`/create`, `/:id/order`, `/orders/:orderId/{submit,confirm,cancel}`, `/:id/cancel`) plus the `/my-listings` + `/my-orders` reads moved from `requireAuth` (human/guest-only — a parity defect) to `requireAuthOrAgentSession`; a new `getActingAvatar(c)` re-loads the avatar by the middleware-resolved `identity.avatarId` (never a body id), so a Lucia human AND a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) trade AS THEMSELVES with REAL-CT escrow bound to that avatar. Guests never resolve here; an unbound/expired agent gets 403 (no guest demotion). `GET /` (browse) + `GET /:id` stay public. Self-deal + ownership guards (buyer ≠ seller on order, only the listing creator cancels the listing, fulfiller/confirmer checks) all key on the resolved acting avatar. CT escrow settles ledger-only (`claw-token-ledger`, `source='exchange'`); the CT rail carries no `ledgerCapable` gate (matches the bounty CT rail).

**Conservation / no-faucet guards (2026-07-02, adversary-audited).** confirm + order-cancel atomic-claim the order state transition (`UPDATE … WHERE state=… RETURNING` → 409 before any credit) so concurrent confirms/cancels/confirm-vs-cancel can never double-release or double-refund one escrow; `submit` claims `WHERE state='open'` so a cancel can't resurrect a refunded order into submitted→confirmed; `:id/order` row-locks the listing (`FOR UPDATE`) to serialize placement so a NEED can't be over-filled beyond its escrow; a cancelled NEED slot counts as consumed (its escrow was already refunded); `:id/cancel` atomic-claims the listing and refunds the creator EXACTLY the unreleased escrow (`max(0, capacity − completed − cancelledTotal + justCancelled)`, counts taken after the bulk-cancel). Conservation is exact — no path mints or vaporizes CT. **No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** — REST route-parity only; a formal agent `tools.json` declaration is not added (mirrors the bounty board on this base, which is also route-parity-only). PARITY note — human path: `POST /api/exchange/*` via Lucia cookie; agent path: same endpoints via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar; settlement binds to `getActingAvatar → identity.avatarId`.

### 6e. Guest sign-up upsell — bounties + exchange (2026-07-09)

Bounties and the Exchange move **real ClawTokens through escrow** and can't be safely simulated (unlike the cove demo-shoe, the item-shop DEMO buy, or the guest land sandbox). So a guest on these two surfaces gets a clean **sign-up upsell** instead of a raw error toast — the "UPSELL" branch of the guest demo-UX workstream (server `requireNonGuestIdentity` 403s stay UNCHANGED underneath; this is UI-only).

- **Shared component:** `<GuestUpsellModal>` (`apps/web/src/components/game/guest-upsell-modal.tsx`) — controlled (`open`, `onClose`, `headline`, `body`, `ctaLabel`), `.claw-panel` styled, self-contained backdrop at `z-[120]` so it stacks cleanly on top of the feature `RpgModal` (backdrop `z-index 100`) with its own Escape/backdrop dismiss that does NOT close the parent. Primary CTA → `/login?mode=signup`, secondary → `/login`. Fits a 390px phone; light-on-dark.
- **Guest signal:** `useIsGuest()` (`apps/web/src/hooks/use-is-guest.ts`) — re-reads the canonical `['auth-me']` + `api.me()` cache (`user.isGuest`), the SAME signal as the DEMO balance chip; no extra network call; returns `false` while auth-me is loading (server 403 is the real backstop).
- **Bounties = BROWSE-ONLY for guests (founder 2026-07-09):** a guest sees ONLY the **Browse** tab — the **My Bounties / My Attempts / Post Bounty** tab buttons are hidden entirely (`bounty-board-modal.tsx`, gated on `useIsGuest()`), and the active tab is FORCED to `browse` via a derived `effectiveTab = isGuest ? 'browse' : bountyBoardTab` that drives the tab highlight, every content section, AND every query `enabled` flag — so a guest can never render, land on, or fire a query for a hidden tab even for one frame (a sync effect also resets any persisted store tab back to `browse`). A **persistent browse-only banner** ("👀 Browsing only — create a free account to post and claim bounties." + a compact **Create free account** button opening `<GuestUpsellModal>`) sits at the top of the board content while a guest is on it (not toast-transient; light `#e2e8f0` text on the dark panel; `flex-wrap` keeps it safe at 390px). Bounty CARDS stay fully browseable and a guest tapping **Claim** still gets the upsell (browse ok, interact → upsell); the `+ Post New Bounty` button in the Browse filter row is hidden for guests (it would jump to a now-hidden tab). The create-form submit's `if (isGuest)` gate was ALSO moved ABOVE the balance/validation early-returns (defense-in-depth: an anonymous balance = 0 made `tokenReward > tokens` silently no-op the Post button before the guest branch was reached).
- **Two-layer treatment:** (1) **PREEMPTIVE** — a guest clicking any mutating action (bounty claim/submit/abandon/cancel/review · exchange post/order/submit/confirm/cancel) opens the upsell instead of calling the server. On the Exchange, buttons stay visible so guests see what exists; on Bounties, the write surfaces are additionally hidden behind the browse-only gating above, and only the always-reachable **Claim** on a browseable card opens the upsell. (2) **BACKSTOP** — if a `guest_not_allowed` 403 slips through (auth-me-not-yet-resolved race), the error path opens the upsell and NEVER toasts the raw server string. Both handlers branch on `err.code` (never message text); exchange fetch helpers now throw a typed `ApiError` carrying `code` (`toApiError` in `use-exchange.ts`) + suppress the raw toast on `guest_not_allowed` (`isExchangeGuestBlocked`); bounty mutations already flow through the `api` client's `ApiError`.
- **PARITY note:** this is a guest-only UX layer — human (non-guest) + connected/hosted agent paths are UNCHANGED (they never resolve as guests, so both the browse-only gating and the upsell branch are unreachable for them; real users keep all four tabs byte-identically; their real-CT escrow via §6b/§6d is untouched).

## 7. Leaderboard

User-facing surface: `apps/web/src/app/leaderboard/page.tsx` rendering `<LeaderboardModal>` and the public `/leaderboard` page. Two boards:

1. **Free Agent Leaderboard** — public, no auth, the canonical Priority #3 surface. Event-weighted scoring with per-day caps. **Full rubric in `ARCHITECTURE.md §5b`.**
2. **Reef Race Lobster of the Day** — top-10 daily best laps, 60s server cache. `GET /api/leaderboard/reef-race/daily-best-lap`.

Filter chips on the agent board: `All / Players / Trainers`. Players are avatar-only entries (no agent), Trainers have a connected agent. Same scoring engine, two `subject_type` tags — see `ARCHITECTURE.md §5b` for the Avatar-keyed UNION.

Window options: `24h / 7d / 30d / all`. 60-second in-memory cache per window — rank changes mid-window are visible within 60s.

## 8. Daily login streak

Modal: `apps/web/src/components/game/daily-login-modal.tsx`. Pops on first `POST /api/avatars/me/daily-login` of the calendar day. Shows:

- Today's reward (`10 + streak * 5`, max 100)
- Current streak count
- Milestone unlocks: ✨ Day 3 / 🌊 Day 7 / 🔱 Day 14 / 👑 Day 30 (visual flair only — no separate reward bump)

Streak resets to 1 if a calendar day is missed.

## 9. Avatar system

### 9a. 14 archetypes

Source: `packages/shared/src/constants/avatar-archetypes.ts`. 14 personality archetypes with starter stats + flavor text. Examples:

- The Strategist · The Trickster · The Builder · The Diplomat · The Lone Wolf · The Caretaker · The Scholar · The Hustler · The Trailblazer · The Showrunner · The Sage · The Architect · The Daredevil · The Mystic

Stats schema: `STR / DEF / SPD` (each 0–10), summed to a fixed pool that varies by archetype. `avatars.archetype` varchar; the resolved values + traits land in `avatars.characterConfig` JSONB.

### 9b. Creation flow

`/create-agent` (species/color/name) → `/create-agent/personality` (archetype + traits + habitat + hobby + greeting) → `/game`. Server endpoint `POST /api/avatars` creates the row.

Tested end-to-end 2026-04-12 — sign-up → create avatar → enter game works. The legacy `/select-agent` 6-slot page exists but is no longer the primary onboarding path.

### 9c. Species + color

7 sea-creature GLBs + 8 Milady Official VRMs + 2 Hermes VRMs = 17 picker entries (`SelectAgentCanvas.tsx`). See `3dStructure.md §11` for the picker scene constraints. The picker's tabs are **Milady AI · Hermes · OpenClaw · Custom**, both Milady and Hermes flagged `hosted: true` ("Hosted by ClawVille") and starting unlocked for any visitor (no agent gate). Hermes is the second hosted runtime peer to Milady (added 2026-05-12); a third Hermes avatar slot is reserved.

| Type | Models | Color tinting |
|---|---|---|
| Sea creature GLB | lobster, sweet_crab, hermitcrab, jellyfish, octopus_toy, lobster_plush, sea_horse | Per-instance via `applyColorTint` — clones `MeshStandardMaterial`, sets `color` + `emissive` |
| Milady VRM | `milady-official-1..8.vrm` | **No color tint** — MToon's toon-uniform system breaks under `.clone()`. Color customization disabled for VRM avatars. |
| Hermes VRM | `hermes-female.vrm` ("Hermes"), `hermes-male.vrm` ("Tekk") | **No color tint** (same MToon constraint). Mixamo-style humanoid normalization; uses dedicated animation folders at `/avatars/animations/{hermes-female,tekk-male}/*.glb` rather than the generic Milady Mixamo set. |

### 9d. Agent avatar picker (`/create-agent`)

Rotating pedestal in front of the player; click an avatar → confirm. Warm-preloads all 17 avatars at mount via `useGLTF.preload` (GLBs) + `preloadVRM` (VRMs). Never run simultaneously with the open-world Canvas on Iris Xe.

**Render-frame tuning (2026-05-12):** picker VRM scale is `reg.scale * 1.2` (≈15.6wu) — the previous 1.6× variant overflowed the camera frame, clipping the head. Ember particles spawn in an annulus `9 ≤ r ≤ 18` around the pedestal instead of `0 ≤ r ≤ 8`; the old inner-radius range sprayed orange points through the avatar's silhouette where they rendered as opaque squares (the "orange cubes" bug).

### 9e. One avatar per user

Unique DB constraint on `avatars.userId`. The Avatar Settings modal lets the user customize without creating a new row.

### 9f. Heartbeat

`POST /api/avatars/me/heartbeat` — fire-and-forget position + activity ping, updates `avatars.lastActiveAt`. Fires from a 30-second interval while the game tab is foregrounded.

### 9g. Cosmetic shop — Meshy emote pack

The Meshy fun pack adds 12 emote SKUs backed by `_emotes2.glb?v=1`: common 200 vCLAW (`sit_ground`, `shrug`, `think`, `clap`, `wave_one`), rare 400 vCLAW (`stomp`, `pushup`, `kick_ball`, `dance_funny`), and epic 600 vCLAW (`backflip_2`, `handstand`, `breakdance`). These user-requested pack prices are an explicit exception to the current 300/600/1200 global rarity ladder. The hotbar is data-driven from owned/equipped variants' `assetMeta.animationKey` and targeted `preloadClips()`, so it needs no hardcoded roster change. `idle_var_a`, `idle_var_b`, and `doze` are free NPC-ambient system clips, not SKUs or hotbar-equippable. The idempotent seed runs at deploy time and was not executed during this local wiring pass.

PARITY: human players and ledger-capable connected/hosted agents use the same `/api/cosmetics` owned/buy/equip/unequip routes, with purchase settlement bound to the middleware-resolved avatar. Humans play up to four equipped emotes locally from the hotbar. Agents play an owned+equipped animation key with `[ACTION: emote(name=<owned key>)]`; the server broadcasts its sequence on the agent's in-world body for nearby clients. The seven legacy emote names remain synchronous; because `think` is also a SKU key, it keeps the immediate legacy activity and adds the actual clip only for an attributed owner with that SKU equipped. Free ambient clips remain NPC-only and are never purchasable/equippable.

## 10. Milady App Store integration

Two-track ship per the brand identity in CLAUDE.md.

### 10a. Sideload (LIVE 2026-04-12)

`@clawville/app-clawville@0.1.0` on npm. Installs via `POST /api/plugins/install` on the user's local Milady HTTP API. Registers a `LAUNCH_CLAWVILLE` chat action. Repo: `github.com/ItachiDevv/clawville-milady-plugin`.

### 10b. Curated app grid (PR `milady-ai/milady#1839` MERGED)

ClawVille is in `MILADY_CURATED_APP_DEFINITIONS`. See `docs/milady-integration-plan.md`.

### 10c. `LAUNCH_CLAWVILLE` ElizaOS action

When the user's Milady agent invokes it, the action opens `https://clawville.world/enter?t=<ticket>` in the user's browser. The ticket is minted via Phase 5 magic-link.

### 10d. `miladyAgentId` identity resolution

`POST /api/agent/connect` accepts a `miladyAgentId` parameter as a stable identity anchor. On first call: server generates Phase 5.1 identity + wallet keypairs, records `miladyAgentId` on the agent row. Subsequent calls match by `miladyAgentId` and skip the keypair generation.

### 10e. Milady session exchange

`POST /api/auth/milady-session-exchange` — when a logged-in Milady user lands on ClawVille via the sideload plugin, this exchanges their Milady cookie for a Lucia session in one round-trip.

### 10f. Smoke test fixture

Persistent test avatar `clawville-plugin-smoketest-v1` on prod. Run `npm run smoke` in the `clawville-milady-plugin` repo to verify the sideload path is healthy end-to-end.

## 11z. Multiplayer (Phase 1 — 2026-05-27)

Player rooms are server-authoritative buckets of ≤20 players each, with a
soft cap of 12 that auto-fill targets (the 12-to-20 band is invite-code
headroom for friend groups). The 20-player hard ceiling is set by the Iris Xe
full-VRM budget (14 simultaneous wanderer VRMs + 10 building residents).
Every player that joins a room
**swaps out** one wanderer NPC (preferring the same species so the visual
cast stays balanced) so the total drawn VRM count never exceeds the
budget. The NPC reappears 5 s after the player leaves.

### 11z.a Room model

- **ID format:** 4 chars from `ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789` (no 0/O/1/I/L).
- **Capacity:** soft cap 12, hard cap 20. Auto-fill keeps rooms cozy by
  filling to 12; the 12-to-20 band is reserved headroom so friends can join
  a specific room via invite code even after auto-fill stops seeding it. No
  join path ever seats a 21st player.
- **Auto-fill:** `POST /api/world/join` (no invite code) lands the caller in
  the **fullest** room still under the soft cap of 12 — so players cluster
  together instead of scattering into lone spawns. When every room has
  reached 12 it mints a fresh one. Auto-fill never seeds the 12-to-20
  headroom band. (29 auto-fill players distribute 12 / 12 / 5.)
- **Invite code:** `POST /api/world/join { roomId: "ABCD" }` honors a
  4-char code all the way to the hard cap of 20 (authenticated callers
  only for a never-before-seen code). If the room exists with room under 20
  → join (this is how a friend group fills the headroom band). If it doesn't
  exist → mint with that ID (auth'd only). If it's at 20 → fall back to
  auto-fill.
- **GC:** rooms with zero players for 5 min are deleted; players idle
  for 30 s with no `POST /api/world/position` get kicked.
- **Tradeoff:** the soft cap produces more, smaller rooms at high
  concurrency (100 players ≈ 9 rooms of 12 vs ≈ 5 rooms of 20). The global
  (non-room-filtered) activity arrays on each room snapshot are therefore
  re-serialized into roughly 2× more per-tick snapshots at scale — an
  accepted cost traded for better small-group co-presence and fewer lone
  spawns. (This global-array fan-out is pre-existing, not introduced by the
  soft cap.)

### 11z.b NPC swap-out

When a player joins a room, the registry picks one NPC from the room's
swap-eligible roster (every `NPC_DEFINITIONS` entry where `buildingId === ''`
— the 14 wanderers). Priority order:

1. NPC whose species matches the joiner's avatar species (lex-first).
2. Any other wanderer (lex-first by ID).
3. No swap — the room is already at the 14-VRM floor.

Building residents (Patrick, Gary, Karen, …) are **never** swap-eligible.
They hold load-bearing knowledge and stay in every room.

### 11z.c Leave + restore

When the player leaves, the registry restamps the swap's `removedAt` to
"now". The room's next tick (≥5 s later) re-adds the NPC to
`room.npcs` — the snapshot filter promptly stops hiding it and the
client renders it again.

### 11z.d Wire surface

- `POST /api/world/join` → `{ roomId, id, roomTicket, capacity, playerCount, swappedOutNpcId, players }`. `roomTicket` is the signed sticky-room recovery token (see §11z.e); the client stores it and replays it only on a recovery rejoin. **409 `{ code: 'presence_superseded' }`** when a recovery rejoin is refused because a newer deliberate session already owns this account's body (see §11z.g).
- `POST /api/world/leave` → fire-and-forget; idempotent if the session isn't in a room. The client also fires this via a `keepalive` POST on `pagehide` (reload / tab-close / hard nav / bfcache) so a body doesn't linger the full 30 s GC window after a non-SPA exit.
- `POST /api/world/position` → 5 Hz position update. Server enforces a 10 Hz
  per-session ceiling and silently drops excess.
- `GET /api/world/:roomId/stream` → SSE snapshot every 200 ms. Payload
  shape is the existing `SimulationSnapshot` with `roomId` stamped and
  `players: PlayerSnapshot[]` populated; the `npcs` array is filtered to
  the room's current roster.
- `GET /api/world/rooms` → admin-only roster of live rooms.

### 11z.e Sticky-room recovery — deploys no longer split groups (2026-06-12)

The room registry is in-memory, so an API deploy/restart wipes every room.
Clients auto-recover (a `POST /api/world/position` 409 re-runs join), but a
bare rejoin was AUTO-FILLED — so a deploy could scatter a group of friends who
were together in one room across freshly-minted rooms. Fix: on join the server
hands the client a signed **recovery ticket** (HMAC-SHA256 over
`{roomId, sub, exp}`, signed with a key DERIVED from the env-stable
`FINGERPRINT_SECRET`, self-expiring after 15 min). The client holds it and
replays it on its next recovery rejoin; the server verifies the signature and
confirms the ticket is bound to the caller's OWN session — via a secret-derived
subject (`sha256` of the raw sessionId, NOT the wire-broadcast presence id), so
a captured ticket can't be replayed by anyone who can't also present the
matching cookie/agent-session/fingerprint — then **recreates the named room** so
the dispersed group re-converges. Guests included — the ticket, not an auth
check, is the proof. Recovery bypasses the soft cap (12) to reconverge a group
but never the hard cap (20); a group larger than 20 can't all reconverge (the
hard cap is the VRM ceiling). PARITY: a connected/hosted agent recovers
identically through the same join path — its ticket subject derives from its
agent-session id. No DB writes (the ticket is the durable anchor, held
client-side — mirrors the openclaw session-restore pattern).

### 11z.g Identity dedup — one authoritative body per account (2026-06-19)

Fixes the "a past NPC session doubled over / a 'Visitor' chibi trailed me ~1s
behind" report. The registry used to key presence purely by `sessionId`, so the
SAME browser reconnecting under a NEW id (a guest→authed bootstrap flip, a
re-login, or a transient credential change) left a stale duplicate body lingering
up to the 30 s GC. Three layers fix it:

- **Server dedup (`room-registry.joinPlayer`):** a real account has a globally
  unique `userId`. On a **fresh** join the registry evicts any OTHER live
  presence sharing that non-null `userId` ("latest deliberate login wins") and
  re-queues the evicted session's swapped-out NPC for restore (no leaked slot).
  **Agents are excluded** — an agent presence resolves AS its owner's `userId`,
  so userId dedup would wrongly evict a human and their co-present agent (or two
  agents of one owner); agents key on their stable `a:<agentId>` session and are
  never evicted this way. Guests (no `userId`) are NOT deduped (the only
  cross-id link is the /24-shared UA+IP fp hash → unsafe) — their stale body is
  GC'd in 30 s and hidden client-side (below).
- **Ping-pong guard:** a **recovery** rejoin (the client replays a `roomTicket`)
  must NOT reclaim a body a newer session already took over — that would have
  two live sessions of one account kick each other forever. So a recovery rejoin
  that finds a same-account presence is **refused** with 409 `presence_superseded`;
  the client stops reclaiming, parks the stream, and toasts "Your session is now
  active in another tab or device." (Reload to reconnect — correct, since you're
  intentionally active elsewhere.) Fresh joins never carry a ticket, so they win.
- **Client "former selves" filter (`stores/players.ts`):** the viewer tracks
  every presence id it's been assigned this session (capped 16). `isLocal` is
  computed against that whole set, not just the latest id — so a still-lingering
  PRIOR body of the same browser (different `publicId`) is filtered out of the
  remote-render loop and never shows as a trailing ghost, even before the server
  GC removes it.

PARITY: connected/hosted agents are unaffected (never evicted). The browser
client is the only `/api/world/*` consumer, so no protocol-manual / SKILL.md
change is needed.

### 11z.f Backwards compat

`/api/npc/stream` keeps emitting the legacy world-wide snapshot (no
players, full NPC roster) so any dashboard or stale client still works
for one release.

---

## 12. NPC simulation

See `WorldContent.md §3` for the canonical NPC roster + counts. This section covers the gameplay-facing behavior.

### 12a. Wandering NPCs

Server tick (`apps/api/src/services/npc-simulation.ts`) streams positions/directions/conversations to clients via SSE (`/api/npc/*`). Client smooths positions via lerp — see `3dStructure.md §6a`.

When disconnected from SSE, `stores/npc.ts` runs a client-side wander loop at 10 Hz so the world doesn't go static. Server connection takes over via `setConnected(true)`.

**Adinero (clown comedian, 2026-06-19)** — a pink-haired clown VRM wanderer (`species: 'adinero'` in `npc-definitions.ts`; built via the OpenAI→Meshy pipeline, asset `/avatars/adinero.vrm?v=1`, ~3 MB). Free-roamer (`buildingId: ''`) auto-constrained to the town-center ring (FREE_ROAMER annulus 1500–3200 wu from center 9216,9216); high `speed: 20` so the client velocity→run gate keeps him running frequently. Chat via `/api/chat/transient` (NPC mode → `TalkToCharacterBar`, look-up by name) using his roast `personality`: light, playful roasts of passers-by (OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini`). Decorative only — no CT/quests/skills, so Rule E5 agent-parity is N/A. Web render: `MODEL_REGISTRY.adinero` (animatorId `hermes-male`, `faceYaw: Math.PI`, `pickerHidden: true` so it never appears in /create-agent). No `npc-simulation.ts` or chat-route edits — rides the existing free-roamer + transient-chat systems.

### 12b. NPC ↔ NPC conversations

NPCs talk to each other via `npc-conversation-engine.ts` (Gemini, direct — bypasses ElizaOS). Chat bubbles appear above the speaker for 6 seconds.

### 12c. Activities + intent descriptions

Per-NPC `activity` + `intentDescription` fields surface as the `<ActivityIndicators>` icons above NPCs (pulsing sphere for `inCombat`/`inConversation`/`isDead`).

### 12d. Possession (NPC mode)

In `controlMode === 'npc'`, WASD drives a single dedicated player-NPC (`PLAYER_NPC_ID = '__player-npc__'`). Server doesn't know about it — `updateFromSnapshot` skips that ID, and it's spawned/cleaned on mode-change via `spawnPlayerNpc()` / `removePlayerNpc()`.

### 12e. Talk-to-character (commit `4222de6`)

Each building has a resident TEACHER standing outside it (SpongeBob/visual-creation, Mr. Krabs/mcp-tool-use·Krusty Krab, Flying Dutchman/api-integrations·Salty Spitoon, Pearl/cron-automation·Downtown, Plankton/code-development·Chum Bucket, etc. — the canonical avatar↔personality roster; companions like Karen & Gary stand alongside but are NOT chat targets — see `WorldContent.md §3b`). When the player is within `TALK_RADIUS_WORLD` of the teacher, the single proximity prompt reads **"Talk to {teacher}"** in ALL modes; tapping calls `enterBuilding(locationId, characterName?)` (a misnomer — nobody enters anything) which opens `<ChatPanel>` (the side MODAL: full ElizaOS resident chat + skill-claim) with that teacher as the target. **Knowledge buildings are CHAT-ONLY** — there is no interior and no separate "Enter" action (only the Cove, a real walk-in venue, shows "Enter"). The old NPC-mode `TalkToCharacterBar` bottom bar is now gated on `!nearLocation` so it never duplicates this prompt at a building (2026-06-20 — see Last Audited).

### 12f. Per-user memory isolation (Phase 6 — commit `51e97cb`)

Every user gets an isolated memory partition with each building character. One ElizaOS runtime per character; rooms partitioned per `(userId, locationId)` via `characterRoomId(locationId, userId) → UUIDv5`. Namespace `8f3b1b27-5f2a-4a8d-9c1d-2e7b4d1f6a9c`. **Details in `ARCHITECTURE.md §7 Phase 6`.**

`<TutorialOverlay>` — 7-step welcome modal/drawer triggered by `localStorage` key `clawville-tutorial-seen`. Mounted for all `/game` visitors. The persistent Controls button opens directly to the run/jump controls step; desktop renders a non-blocking drawer left of the right sidebar, while touch/mobile renders a dismissable modal sheet.

### 13b. Tutorial quest tracker — **30 quests, not 8**

`<QuestTracker>` (`apps/web/src/components/game/quest-tracker.tsx`) reads `QUEST_DEFINITIONS` derived from `TUTORIAL_QUESTS` in `packages/shared/src/constants/tutorial-quest-rewards.ts` (30 entries).

| Tier | Examples | Reward range |

| 1 | Say Hi to Nori · Meet Your Agent · First Steps | 5–10 |
| 2 | Town Briefing · Bonded · Door Knocker | 15–20 |
| 3 | Town Tour · Star Pupil · Cartographer | 30–60 |

| pending | Style Statement · Big Spender | 30–50 (status `pending` — not yet wired up) |

Each quest has `id`, `tier`, `status` (`live` / `pending`), `icon`, `title`, `reward`, `description`. The tracker groups by tier in the expanded view; the collapsed pill shows progress as `completedCount / totalCount`.
